## 4. Per-candidate notes

Every B1 below is needs-live (tracer T5, docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-05-assembly-design.md:453, open; at the desk read, 2026-10-03; superseded: the 2026-10-04 run took B1 live for 29 rows, each with a ◊ line, 28 of them pass and Novel word count needs-live◊¤ after the post-run check, while the other 42 stay needs-live, §6.5); §6 lists what each live leg measures. For core rows every B1 cite includes the loadPlugin chain (`app.js:1@3819978` in `B-core-1`, `@byte 3825058` in `B-core-2`), and every B5 is n/a on the asar's `package.json:5`. A *named key* is the setting key and value the worker gave for B3 or B4; it is a fact about what achieves the must, not a choice of setting.

### 4.1 Core plugins, `B-core-1` (offsets are characters)

**Audio recorder (`audio-recorder`).**

- Writes: only on a ribbon or command gesture. `saveRecording` writes `Recording <YYYYMMDDHHmmss>.webm` (or `.m4a`) with `createBinary` at the path set by `app.json` `attachmentFolderPath` (default `/`), then inserts an embed at the cursor of the active Markdown note (`app.js:1@3077681`); with no Markdown view active it opens the recording instead. No settings file. `defaultOn` unset, so off in a fresh vault (`@3073930`; the enable logic turns on a missing key only if `defaultOn` is truthy, `@2918891`).
- B2 fail: no step covers audio, and the floor's vault ships it off (`erazlogo/obsidian-history-vault@a3d22d0a .obsidian/core-plugins-migration.json:23`; assembly-design.md:168).
- B3 named key: `app.json` `attachmentFolderPath` pointing outside literature/, fulltext/, wiki/, log/ and system/; the default `/` does. A `./`-relative value would follow the active note into a machine folder. The embed lands in whichever note is active, which is a gesture.
- B4: the audio file is not `.md`, so OKF §11 (`SPEC.md@ad30107:736-747`) does not apply to it. The embed is a wikilink unless `app.json` `useMarkdownLinks` is true (default false, `@1246673`). The verifier added that OKF §6.1 (`SPEC.md@ad30107:439-446`) permits markdown links, while the deviation register registers wikilinks only for claim and stance links (`docs/agents/okf-conformance.md:41`).
- Verifier: confirmed every must. It re-checked the pin at obsidian-releases `e76f2c3` and the asar hash against `desktop-releases.json:5`.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass and B4 pass unchanged. Its one judged write is the recording file it places itself; the embed goes into the note the person is recording from, which is a chosen-file edit. B3 seed key: `app.json` `"attachmentFolderPath": "/"` (the default; a `./`-relative value would follow the active note) (`app.js:1@3077681`; `@1393032`; `@1246673`; `hooks/pretooluse_guard.py:11-23`). B4 seed key: `app.json` `"useMarkdownLinks": true`, since the embed comes from `generateMarkdownLink`, which honours it (`@1783805`; `okf-conformance.md:41`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass → pass — basis moved: nothing is counted. It writes only when the person records from its ribbon item or command (the recording file, then an embed in the active note), and it registers no CLI or protocol handler and no writing hook, so `app.json` `attachmentFolderPath` no longer decides B3; the default `"/"` is a fact about where the person's recording lands. The core `command` CLI handler can start and stop a recording remotely; it is Obsidian's, not this plugin's (§2A, Second ruling). The verifier counted 44 `registerCliHandler` call sites; the re-judgement's 46 also counted the two wrapper definitions (`app.js:1@byte 3082619`; `@byte 3079251`, `@byte 3079450`; protocol handlers only for daily and unique, `@byte 3360601`, `@byte 3707665`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass → fail∆ — failing path: after saving a recording it replaces the selection in the active Markdown note with an embed of the recording, from the ribbon item or the start/stop command (which the core `command` CLI handler can also reach). No key stops the embed: the plugin has no settings file, `app.json` `"attachmentFolderPath": "/"` (the default) places only the recording file, and `core-plugins.json` `"audio-recorder": false` disables rather than confines. Undetected in an open fulltext/ note (gitignored, so the commit gate never sees it), in root `log.md` (shape check only) and as an append at the end of a log/ day file; in a literature note evidence-layer catches the body change. Verifier correction, result unchanged: the claim that nothing detects it in wiki/ is false in the default link form, because captured-set reports a wikilink embed of a `.webm` or `.m4a` as not a page; wiki/ goes undetected only under `app.json` `"useMarkdownLinks": true`, this row's B4 seed (`app.js:1@byte 3082621`, `@byte 3083108`; `research_vault/captured.py:20`, `:94-108`, `:173-188`; `verify.py:54-64`; `templates/vault/gitignore:5`; `templates/git/pre-commit:29`; `structure.py:117-134`; `lints.py:109-157`).
- ◇ 2026-10-04 fourth ruling: B3 fail∆ → pass∆◇ — the embed that replaces the selection in the active note is open-note editing and is not judged, and the core `command` CLI handler that can start or stop a recording is Obsidian's, not this plugin's. Counted: only the recording file, which it creates at the attachment path for the active note from the ribbon item or the command. Setting: `app.json` `"attachmentFolderPath": "/"` (the default, seeded explicitly), which puts that file at the vault root from every trigger; a `./`-relative value would follow the active note into a machine folder. Verifier: confirmed; the insert and binary-create calls just before the plugin's id belong to the mobile share receiver, an app path (`app.js:1@byte 3082300-3083120`, `@byte 1397786`, `@byte 3074068`, `@byte 3076897`).

**Backlinks (`backlink`).**

- Writes: its one setting `backlinkInDocument` in `<configDir>/backlink.json` (`@3004305`; settings file name is `<id>.json`, `@2916062`). The verifier found a note write the desk missed: each Unlinked mentions match carries a Link button that reads the mentioning note, builds a link with `generateMarkdownLink` and calls `vault.modify` on it (`@3022280`, `@3022660`), and that note can be in literature/ or wiki/.
- B2: floor, enabled at `a3d22d0a .obsidian/core-plugins.json:6`; comparison doc `:94` names Graph view and backlinks.
- B3: desk pass on a claim basis (help `Backlinks.md:24-68`: read-only pane). The verifier kept pass and upgraded the basis to evidence, but refuted the read-only premise: the Link write is a gesture on a file the user chose to link. Its controlling key is `app.json` `userIgnoreFilters` listing the machine-surface folders, because unlinked search skips `isUserIgnored` files (`@3021060`); the cost is that those folders also drop out of search and graph. `backlinkInDocument` does not govern the write.
- B4: the inserted link follows `useMarkdownLinks` and `newLinkFormat`; ordinary link text (OKF §6.1).
- Exit (desk): Stop invoking it; only backlink.json in the config dir remains.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass unchanged and needs no key. Each Link click reads and rewrites only the one mentioning file on that match row, a chosen-file edit, so `userIgnoreFilters` no longer carries the result (`app.js:1@3022660`). B4 seed key: `app.json` `"useMarkdownLinks": true` (`@1783805`; `okf-conformance.md:41`).
- ◊ 2026-10-04 live: B1 pass — registered on 1.13.7 (defaultOn true) and already on in the vault's first-open `core-plugins.json` (§6.5), so no toggle was needed: enabled, loaded, 3 commands, view `backlink`. `dev:errors` captured nothing and `dev:console` held only CLI echoes (`/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/step1-core.log`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass → pass — basis moved back to a key, which the 2026-10-03 pass had dropped. Its only vault write is the Link button on an Unlinked mentions row, which reads the mentioning note, splices in a link and writes it back with `vault.modify`. The unlinked search skips user-ignored files before listing a row, so with `app.json` `"userIgnoreFilters": ["literature/", "log/", "wiki/", "fulltext/", "system/propagations/", "log.md", "inbox/review-queue.md", "system/bibliography.json"]` no machine-surface note is listed and the button has no machine-surface target; each entry compiles to a `^`-anchored, case-insensitive prefix. Verifier: confirmed; `addLinkFunction` has a single call site, and the plugin otherwise writes only `saveData` of `backlinkInDocument` and layout state and registers no CLI handler (`app.js:1@byte 3025992`, `@byte 3026567`, `@byte 3027113-3027700`, `@byte 1758536-1759300`).

**Bases (`bases`).**

- Writes, desk: (a) the `.base` file itself when a view is used: the file view's save function calls `requestSave` (`@2448310`), and resizing a column stores `columnSize` in the view config (`@3136329`); (b) frontmatter of a row's note on paste or clear, through `processFrontMatter` (`@3112742`, `@3129613`); (c) new files from new item, carrying the view's filter-derived properties, in `newItemFolder` or the app's new-file parent (`@3155354`). No settings file: the class never calls `loadData` (`@3145371-3160000`).
- Writes, verifier additions: every view-config setter (set, order, sort, limit, group-by, summaries, filters) calls `query.save` (`@2447240`, `@2313310-2314464`, `@3136317`); the main cell edit is `updateProperty` (`@2491475`); the desk's `@3155354` is the Obsidian CLI `base:create` handler, while the UI new-item path is `@2449700-2450669`, which picks `newItemFolder`, then the filter-derived folder, then `getNewFileParent(base path)`, so `app.json` `newFileLocation` `current` lands new notes in system/bases/. `wQ` (`@2325004`) stamps the filter-derived frontmatter on the new note: `type` literature from `trust-tier.base` and concept from `open-questions.base`, which makes a hand-made literature note against vault `AGENTS.md:9`. Dropping files onto a view (`@2499068`) merges the filter-derived frontmatter into each dropped note: a literature note dropped on `open-questions.base` gets its capture-owned `type` rewritten to concept, which `lints.py:615-618` flags, and the note moves (`@1408950`) when a filter implies a folder. The `renameProperty` path (`@2462642` to `@1776126`) fires only on the add-property path for a name not already known (`@2458360`) and is inert in practice.
- B1 desk finding: `research_vault/templates/vault/system/bases/open-questions.base:8` calls `file.content`, which 1.13.7's Bases does not expose (File key list `@2173021`, `@2173600`; the File-type functions registered near `@2202319` are asLink, hasLink, inFolder, hasTag, hasProperty; help `Bases syntax.md:231-245`). The formula is expected to error or return nothing; T5 confirms. The minimum Obsidian version for `.base` files is recorded nowhere (assembly spec:248). Related to issue #161.
- B2: vault-setup, because the seed ships two `.base` files and only Bases renders them (`trust-tier.base:1-6`, `open-questions.base:1-8`, `research_vault/scaffold.py:17`). The desk also named search (comparison `:100`, `:355-357`); the verifier struck it, since assembly spec `:143` binds search to PRISMA-S literature search and comparison `:100` is a Retrieval row in no step. The result stands on vault-setup alone.
- B3 fail: write (a) is implicit, rewrites a file the scaffold seeded, and has no key to stop it. Under the shared facts system/ is a machine surface; under vault `AGENTS.md:7`, which names only `system/bibliography.json` and `system/propagations/`, it would pass (§7.3). Writes (b) and (e) touch literature/ rows with no read-only setting, and `lints.py:614-622` flags unattested changes to capture fields. Write (c) follows `app.json` `newFileLocation` and `newFileFolderPath`. The only off switch is `core-plugins.json` `"bases": false`, which leaves the seeded `.base` files unrendered.
- B4: `.base` files are YAML and fall outside §11 (`SPEC.md@ad30107:736-747`, which covers `.md`). Frontmatter edits stay plain YAML; whether `processFrontMatter` keeps capture's quoting is needs-live.
- Exit (desk): Disable it; .base files stay as inert YAML and any frontmatter it edited is ordinary YAML.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation, B3: fail unchanged, on a new basis. Under the ruled roster `system/bases/` is not a machine surface, so the implicit `.base` rewrites are not judged. The re-judgement moved B3 to pass: with the seeded type-only filters (`trust-tier.base:4-6`), new-item notes go to `newItemFolder` or the new-file parent (`app.js:1@2449925`; `@2325004`; `@2316564`). The verifier refuted that, and its fail is recorded. claude-obsidian 2.2.0, the tool that writes wiki/ (`README.md:72`), keeps `.base` files under wiki/ with an in-folder wiki filter (`skills/obsidian-bases/SKILL.md:42`, `:63`). For such a base, New creates the note in wiki/ before `app.json` is consulted, the drop handler moves dropped files there inside the link updater (`@2499182`; `@1408959`), and the `base:create` CLI handler uses the same folder order (`@byte 3158512`). These are files the plugin creates or moves on its own, and no research-vault-seedable key reaches a `.base` the compile tool writes.
- ‡ 2026-10-03 normalisation: B4 pass → fail. The re-judgement kept pass because a new-item note is stamped with the filter-derived `type`. The verifier refuted that, and its result is recorded: the stamp happens only for type-equality filters, and with no keys `processFrontMatter` writes no block. A new item in the compile tool's wiki/ base is therefore an empty `.md` with no frontmatter in wiki/, outside the inbox exemption, which breaks OKF §11 rule 1, and no seedable key changes it (`app.js:1@2449925`; `@2325004`; `@byte 1791700-1792030`; `structure.py:50-51`; `SPEC.md@ad30107:740-742`; claude-obsidian 2.2.0 `skills/obsidian-bases/SKILL.md:42`, `:63`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 fail → fail — the basis narrows to the `base:create` Obsidian-CLI handler, a remote write. A caller names any base, or uses the active base view, and the handler creates a note in the base's `newItemFolder`, else its filter-derived folder, else the base's new-file parent, and stamps the filter-derived frontmatter. A caller-supplied name keeps its slashes through path cleaning, so a compile-tool base under wiki/ with an in-folder filter, or a name such as `wiki/x` under the root parent, creates the note in wiki/. New, drop-onto-view, cell edits and view-config saves are now the person editing the base they have open. No setting confines the handler: the CLI switch is per-machine Electron IPC, `newItemFolder` lives in the `.base` file, `app.json` `newFileLocation` is read only when the base yields no folder, and `core-plugins.json` `"bases": false` disables the plugin. Whether a base rewrites itself on first render stays the §6.3 live leg (`app.js:1@byte 3158512`; `@byte 551508`; `@byte 550852`; `@byte 3478003`; claude-obsidian 2.2.0 `skills/obsidian-bases/SKILL.md:42`, `:63`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 fail → fail — basis widened from `base:create` alone to every path into wiki/. Failing paths: (a) every view-config change (sort, filter, limit, group, column resize) saves the open `.base`, including a base the compile tool keeps under wiki/, and no check reads non-Markdown files; (b) New item creates a note in the base's `newItemFolder`, else its filter-derived folder (wiki/ for an in-folder wiki filter), stamped with filter-derived frontmatter, and a note stamped with a `type` passes verify; (c) dropping files onto a view merges filter-derived frontmatter into them and moves them into the implied folder; (d) the `base:create` CLI handler does (b) remotely; (e) cell edits, paste and clear rewrite row notes' frontmatter, undetected on a wiki/ row or on a literature row's non-capture keys. Setting: `app.json` `userIgnoreFilters` set to the roster list drops machine-surface notes from view rows and so confines (e) only; `newItemFolder` lives in each `.base` file, not in seedable config; `core-plugins.json` `"bases": false` disables. Verifier: confirmed (`app.js:1@byte 2512194`, `@byte 3158512`; `@2447240`, `@2449700-2450669`, `@2499068` character offsets; claude-obsidian 2.2.0 `skills/obsidian-bases/SKILL.md:42`, `:63`).

**Bookmarks (`bookmarks`).**

- Writes: only `<configDir>/bookmarks.json` (items: files, folders, searches, headings, blocks, links). The verifier located the write: `saveData({items})` (`@3187379`); legacy `starred.json` is only read (`@3184093`). It succeeds the legacy id `starred` (help `Bookmarks.md:3`). `defaultOn` true (`@3175657`).
- B2: floor, enabled at `a3d22d0a .obsidian/core-plugins.json:15`, configured with saved tag searches by source and entity type (`.obsidian/bookmarks.json:2-51`).
- B3 and B4: config-dir JSON only. Desk basis was claim (help `Bookmarks.md:6-127`); the verifier upgraded it to evidence.
- ◊ 2026-10-04 live: B1 pass — registered on 1.13.7 and on at first open: enabled, loaded, 7 commands, view `bookmarks`; no error captured (`/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/step1-core.log`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass → fail∆ — earlier passes recorded it as writing `bookmarks.json` only. Failing paths: (a) the Bookmark block command (`bookmarks:bookmark-current-section`) inserts a generated `^id` at the block end of the active note when the block has none, once the person confirms the bookmark modal; undetected in wiki/ and fulltext/, and an append in the last block of a log/ day file; (b) opening a file bookmark whose file no longer exists (click, or open all) goes through `openLinkText`, which recreates an empty file at the bookmarked path; the plugin follows renames only, so a page the compile tool or the CLI removed leaves a stale item. Recreations that pass every check: a `.canvas` or `.base` under wiki/, a nested `index.md` or `log.md` under wiki/, and a date-named bare `.md` under log/, which the pre-commit hook stamps `type: "daily"`. Setting: none; `core-plugins.json` `"bookmarks": false` disables. Verifier: confirmed; the block-id insert alone decides the fail, and a recreated log/ file with a non-date name is caught later, once the CLI regenerates root `log.md` and okf-structure flags the resulting heading (`app.js:1@byte 3182083`, `@byte 3182600-3183200`, `@byte 3192667`, `@byte 1539800-1540200`, `@byte 1775900-1776600`, `@byte 3186714`; `research_vault/okf.py:22-32`; `structure.py:117-134`).

**Canvas (`canvas`).**

- Writes, desk: `.canvas` files only on request, at `canvas.json` `newFileLocation` (root, current or folder; default root) and `newFileFolderPath` (`@3344676`, `@3337887`); the rename queue rewrites references inside existing `.canvas` files (`@3332482`). Other `canvas.json` keys: `snapToGrid` and `snapToObjects` (default true, `@3336386`), `zoomBreakpoint`, wheel behaviour. JSON Canvas is MIT (obsidianmd/jsoncanvas, GitHub API).
- Writes, verifier additions: narrow-to-block on a file card generates a block id when the chosen block has none and calls `vault.modify` on the referenced note to insert `^id` (`@3300560`). Convert-to-file creates the note at `fileManager.getNewFileParent(canvasPath)` (`@3304000`, `@1770196`), so `app.json` `newFileLocation` decides where it lands (current means the canvas's own folder). Other note-creating paths: dropping an unresolved link, the empty-embed create button and the file-card suggest (`@3288506`, `@3309709`, `@3194307`); all use `app.json` `newFileLocation`.
- B2: floor, counted at face value: enabled at `a3d22d0a .obsidian/core-plugins.json:7`, but the floor tree has no `.canvas` file and no `canvas.json`. Outgoing links and Editor status get the same face-value rule.
- B3: desk pass with the named key `canvas.json` `newFileLocation` `"root"` (default) or `"folder"` with `newFileFolderPath` outside the machine surfaces. **Verifier: fail.** The narrow-to-block write is made from a gesture on the canvas card but lands in another note, the same shape the desk failed Bases on, and no key prevents it. Bounds: it fires only when the card points at a note in literature/, wiki/ or log/ and only for a block without an id; claim lines already carry `^claim-id`, so narrowing to a claim writes nothing. In a literature note capture regenerates the inserted id away and the canvas reference dangles.
- B4: a convert-to-file note has no frontmatter and fails §11 rules 1-2 (`SPEC.md@ad30107:740-742`) unless it lands in inbox/, the registered deviation (`okf-conformance.md:36`). The desk left its location untraced; the verifier traced it (above). Inserted `^id`s are plain text.
- Exit (desk): Disable it; .canvas files stay as JSON Canvas files any JSON Canvas reader opens.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation, B3: fail† unchanged, on a new basis. Narrow-to-block is now a chosen-file edit, and new canvases and card notes are placed by `canvas.json` `"newFileLocation": "root"` and `app.json` `"newFileLocation"` (`"folder"` with `"newFileFolderPath": "inbox"`, or `"root"`) (`app.js:1@3300560`; `@3337887`). On those keys the re-judgement moved B3 to pass. The verifier refuted it, and its fail is recorded. `onRename` queues every vault rename, and the queue rewrites every indexed `.canvas` that names the old path: an automatic bulk write with no setting and no ignore check (`@3337108`; `@3332482`). The compile tool keeps its boards under `wiki/canvases/` (claude-obsidian 2.2.0 `skills/canvas/SKILL.md:36`; `README.md:72`). The file-card menu's own Rename item also runs the link-updater fan-out that fails Files (`@byte 3306387`; `@byte 1777251-1779300`).
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B4 pass unchanged. Seed keys: `app.json` `"newFileLocation": "folder"`, `"newFileFolderPath": "inbox"`. Convert-to-file then lands its frontmatter-less note under the untyped-inbox deviation (`@3304000`; `okf-conformance.md:36`; `structure.py:50-51`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 fail† → fail† — the basis narrows to the vault rename hook, which reacts to a rename by any writer (the person, another plugin, the core CLI move and rename). It queues the old and new paths, then rewrites through `vault.process` every indexed `.canvas` whose file or group nodes name the old path. The index admits every `.canvas` on an extension test with no ignore-filter check, and the run shows a notice, not a confirm, so a board under wiki/canvases/ is rewritten. Narrow-to-block, the file-card Rename (whose link update goes through the confirm modal), new canvases and convert-to-file are now the person's own actions. No setting confines the hook: `canvas.json` holds only snap and new-file-location keys and the hook reads none of them, and `core-plugins.json` `"canvas": false` disables the plugin (`app.js:1@byte 3340886`; `@byte 3342092`; `@byte 3337490`; `@byte 3337927`; `@byte 3331000`; `@byte 3333164`; claude-obsidian 2.2.0 `skills/canvas/SKILL.md:36`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 fail† → fail† — basis widened. Failing paths: (a) the vault rename hook, reacting to a rename by any writer, rewrites every indexed `.canvas` that names the old path, boards under wiki/canvases/ included, undetected; (b) narrow-to-block inserts a `^id` into the card's note, undetected in wiki/; (c) the file-card Rename runs the link-updater fan-out across the vault; and, added by the verifier, (e) file cards for Markdown notes are editable embeds that save into the linked note, and (f) every board edit saves the open `.canvas`, a board under wiki/canvases/ included. (d) New canvases and convert-to-file notes are confined by `canvas.json` `"newFileLocation": "root"` and `app.json` `"newFileLocation": "folder"` with `"newFileFolderPath": "inbox"`; nothing confines (a)-(c), (e) or (f); `core-plugins.json` `"canvas": false` disables. The earlier bound that capture regenerates an inserted id away in a literature note carries no weight (superseded 2026-10-04, third ruling) (`app.js:1@byte 3340886`, `@byte 3342082`, `@byte 3337490-3338700`, `@byte 3331000`; `@3300560` character offset; `@byte 3302175`, `@byte 3306387`, `@byte 1777402-1779300`; claude-obsidian 2.2.0 `skills/canvas/SKILL.md:36`).

**Command palette (`command-palette`).**

- Writes: `<configDir>/command-palette.json` (the pinned list, `saveData` at `@3348811`); recent commands go to the app's localStorage outside the vault (`@3348843`). Commands it runs write on behalf of their own plugins. `defaultOn` true (`@3347365`).
- B2: floor, enabled at `a3d22d0a .obsidian/core-plugins.json:13` with five pinned commands (`.obsidian/command-palette.json:2-8`).
- ◊ 2026-10-04 live: B1 pass — registered on 1.13.7 and on at first open: enabled, loaded, 1 command; no error captured. Seeded pinned commands were not tested, because no seed exists (`/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/step1-core.log`).

**Daily notes (`daily-notes`).**

- Writes: `<folder>/<moment(format)>.md`, created from the template if missing. Triggers: open-today's-note (ribbon, command, hotkey); startup when `app.json` `openBehavior` is `"daily"`; the `obsidian://daily` URI (`@3355611`); the CLI handlers `daily:append` and `daily:prepend` (`@3357941`). An empty folder falls back to `app.json` `newFileLocation` and `newFileFolderPath` (`@3358995`); a configured folder that does not exist makes `getDailyNote` error without writing (`~@3360300`, verifier). Keys in `daily-notes.json`: `folder`, `format` (default `YYYY-MM-DD`), `template`; legacy `autorun` is migrated to `openBehavior` `"daily"` and deleted (`@3358581`). `defaultOn` **true** (`@3352677`), so every fresh vault has it unless the seed says false.
- B2: daily-log (help `Daily notes.md:5`; assembly spec:146). The floor is bound to daily-log, yet the floor's vault ships this plugin off (`core-plugins-migration.json:10`) and keeps a dated note under its diary folder plus `meta/templates/diary.md`. research-vault already carries daily-log: the CLI writes `log/<date>.md` (`research_vault/publish.py:416-430`).
- B3 named keys: `app.json` `openBehavior` `""` (default, `@1247433`) and `daily-notes.json` `folder` outside the machine surfaces, or `core-plugins.json` `"daily-notes": false`. Conflict recorded, not resolved: `folder` `log` with format `YYYY-MM-DD` is exactly the CLI's path; the CLI appends to an existing non-empty day file (`publish.py:422-427`), and the append-only lint is a prefix check against HEAD (`lints.py:109-120`, `:146-148`), so creating and appending pass it while a prepend (`daily:prepend`, or a URI prepend) is flagged. With the folder outside log/ the plugin no longer serves the vault's log.
- B4: without a template the new note is empty and fails §11 rules 1-2 (the okf-frontmatter check). Named key: `daily-notes.json` `template` `"system/templates/daily"` (`getDailyNote` appends `.md`). The seeded template carries `type: "daily"` (`research_vault/templates/vault/system/templates/daily.md:1-3`), which is the type log/ derives (`research_vault/structure.py:28-30`). The verifier noted that the template's body comment naming log/ would be copied verbatim if the folder is set elsewhere. Links shown for date properties are display-only (help `Daily notes.md:44-49`).
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass and B4 pass unchanged. B3 seed key: `daily-notes.json` `"folder": "inbox"`, or any existing folder off the roster other than `log`. Every write resolves through that key: open-today, startup under `openBehavior` daily, the daily URI, and the daily, append and prepend CLI handlers. A missing folder throws without writing (`app.js:1@3355611`, `@3357941`, `@3358995`). B4 seed key: `daily-notes.json` `"template": "system/templates/daily"` (`daily.md:1-3`; `structure.py:50-51`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass → pass — basis unchanged, cite corrected by the verifier. Counted: startup when `app.json` `openBehavior` is `daily` (mobile re-runs it on resume after 15 minutes), the `obsidian://daily` URI including its append and prepend content, and the `daily`, `daily:read`, `daily:append` and `daily:prepend` CLI handlers; all resolve the path through `getDailyNote`. Setting: `daily-notes.json` `"folder": "inbox"` (or another existing folder off the roster, never `log`); a missing folder writes nothing. Startup reads `openBehavior` at `app.js:1@byte 3825849` and routes to the plugin at `@byte 3869406`; the re-judgement's `@byte 3825058` is the loadPlugin chain (`@byte 3365173`; `@byte 3360533`; `@byte 3360942-3363243`).
- ◊ 2026-10-04 live: B1 pass — registered on 1.13.7 and on at first open: enabled, loaded, 3 commands; no error captured. No new `.md` appeared anywhere in the vault during the run (git status and every per-plugin tree diff). The vault has no `daily-notes.json`, so a day note would have landed at the root rather than under log/, and none did. Open-today with a seeded `daily-notes.json` and the CLI log append (§6.1) were not run (`/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/step1-core.log`; `/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/plugin-*-tree-*.txt`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass → pass — basis widened to every trigger, key unchanged. Paths judged: open today's note (ribbon, command, hotkey); startup when `openBehavior` is `daily`; the `obsidian://daily` URI with append and prepend; the `daily`, `daily:append` and `daily:prepend` CLI handlers; and, added by the verifier, the share-menu text insert and file-link insert. Every one resolves its path through `getDailyNote`, which refuses a folder that does not exist. Setting: `daily-notes.json` `"folder": "inbox"`, or another existing folder off the roster, never `log` (`app.js:1@byte 3365163`, `@byte 3358818`, `@byte 3359369`, `@byte 3360942-3363243`, `@byte 3366133`).

**File recovery (`file-recovery`).**

- Writes: snapshots to IndexedDB `<appId>-backup` outside the vault (`@3370829`; help `File recovery.md:16`); the vault is written only on an explicit Restore (`@3382675`; help `:29`). `file-recovery.json` keys: `intervalMinutes` (default 5) and `keepDays` (default 7), defined just before `@3370054`. `defaultOn` true.
- B2: floor, enabled at `a3d22d0a .obsidian/core-plugins.json:18` with its open command pinned (`command-palette.json:6`).
- ‡ 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass → fail. The re-judgement kept pass on the UI Restore alone. The verifier refuted it, and its result is recorded: the plugin's `history:restore` Obsidian-CLI handler overwrites a caller-named file with a stored snapshot, without confirmation. That is a URI-class external write that can name any literature/ or wiki/ file. The CLI switch is held per machine through Electron IPC, not in `.obsidian/`, so no seedable key confines it (`app.js:1@byte 3378999`; `@byte 3478003`; `@3382675`). Hinge: if a CLI command that names one file counts as a chosen-file write, the cell returns to pass. Resolved 2026-10-04 by the second ruling.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B4 pass unchanged; both the UI and the CLI restore write back the file's own earlier bytes.
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 fail → fail — the hinge above is settled: the `history:restore` Obsidian-CLI handler is remote, and it overwrites a caller-named file with a stored snapshot through `adapter.write`, without confirmation. Its background snapshots on modify, open, create and an interval go to IndexedDB, not the vault. No setting confines the handler: the CLI switch is per-machine IPC, and `file-recovery.json` `intervalMinutes` and `keepDays` do not gate it. The verifier added that a very large `intervalMinutes` (gate at `@byte 3380751`) stores no snapshot, so a restore finds nothing; that removes the plugin's function and, under the reading applied to Templates, is not a confining setting (`app.js:1@byte 3378999`; `@byte 3375865`; `@byte 3478003`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 fail‡ → fail‡ — basis widened from `history:restore` to both restore routes. Restore of a snapshot the person picks in the File recovery modal, and the `history:restore` CLI handler, overwrite the file with stored bytes through `adapter.write`; the verifier added that the UI restore can recreate a deleted file, making its folder first. Undetected in wiki/ and fulltext/; a literature note restored to an earlier capture-rendered snapshot passes evidence-layer, because its machine-class `generated` differs from HEAD (counted as attested) and its `managed-sha256` matches its own body, and claim-immutability (warn tier) notices only changed claim blocks. Setting: none; `intervalMinutes` and `keepDays` do not gate a restore, and `core-plugins.json` `"file-recovery": false` disables. The snapshot store is a backup and changes no result (`app.js:1@3382675` character offset; `@byte 3378999`, `@byte 3387665-3387800`; `research_vault/lints.py:275-336`, `:661-678`).
- ◇ 2026-10-04 fourth ruling: B3 fail‡ → pass‡◇ — the ruling names File recovery's restore among Obsidian's own vault operations. The re-derivation kept fail on the plugin-registered `history:restore` CLI handler as a remote route; the verifier refuted it, and its result is recorded. Both the modal and that handler, which first resolves an existing file and takes a version number, write the file's own stored snapshot back to its path through `adapter.write`, so the named operation controls whichever route performs it, as merges stay outside A3 whichever add-on performs them (#183). Snapshots go to IndexedDB outside the vault, and the other history handlers only read or open the modal. Counted: nothing. Setting: none needed. Hinge: if a plugin-registered remote route onto a named app operation counts as a remote route, the cell fails with no key, since `intervalMinutes` and `keepDays` do not gate a restore (`app.js:1@byte 3378999-3380000`, `@byte 3387500-3387800`).

**Files (`file-explorer`).**

- Writes: only on gestures (create, rename, move, delete). Rename and move run `renameFile`, then `runAsyncLinkUpdate`, then `updateAllLinks` (`@1772461`; verifier `@1772300`, `@1772990`), which rewrites links in every linking note but prompts first unless `app.json` `alwaysUpdateLinks` is true (`@1774483`); choosing Always update in the prompt sets the key true for good. New notes go to `app.json` `newFileLocation` and `newFileFolderPath` (defaults root and `/`, `@1247380`). Deletes follow `trashOption` (default system). `defaultOn` true (`@3367696`).
- B3 named key: `app.json` `alwaysUpdateLinks` `false` (the default, `@1246673`), which keeps a prompt on every rename; no key forbids link updates outright.
- B4 named key: `app.json` `newFileLocation` `"folder"` with `newFileFolderPath` `"inbox"`, so new empty notes fall under the registered untyped-inbox deviation (`okf-conformance.md:36`; `structure.py:50`). The verifier confirmed that a root-level `Untitled.md` gets `expected_type` None and is flagged, while inbox/ is exempt through `is_fleeting` (`structure.py:50-51`, `:91-100`).
- Verifier conflict: the floor's `app.json` sets `alwaysUpdateLinks` true (`a3d22d0a .obsidian/app.json:2`) and `newFileLocation` `"current"` (`:6`), the opposite of the keys named here.
- ‡ 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass → fail. A rename or move it performs runs Obsidian's link updater. The updater walks every file's references and rewrites changed links, wiki/ and literature/ included, with no ignore-filter check. `app.json` `"alwaysUpdateLinks": false` (the default) only puts a confirm-per-update modal in front, and choosing always-update in that modal sets the key true for good. No seedable key confines the fan-out (`app.js:1@1772461`, = `@byte 1777251`; `@byte 1777402-1779300`; `@byte 1746094`; `@byte 1792033`). The same fan-out follows renames from the view header, the link menu, the core move-file item and the CLI rename and move handlers, so disabling Files does not remove it (`@2444407`; `@byte 2891793`; `@byte 1640602`; `@byte 1639927`). `app.json` `"deleteUnlinkedAttachments": "never"` confines the trashing of a deleted note's now-unlinked attachments (`@byte 1783443`). Hinge: if a confirm-per-update modal counts as confining, the cell returns to pass with `alwaysUpdateLinks` false. Resolved 2026-10-04 by the second ruling.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B4 pass unchanged. Seed keys: `app.json` `"newFileLocation": "folder"`, `"newFileFolderPath": "inbox"` for the nav New note (`@1481487`). Rewritten links keep their wiki or markdown form (`@byte 1741795`). The folder-menu New note creates an empty untyped note in a folder the person picks (`@1478098`). The verifier left it pass as a person-chosen location, a carve-out the B4 ruling does not state.
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 fail → pass — nothing is counted. Create, rename, move and delete are menu or drag actions the person takes on files they chose. The view's vault hooks only redraw the tree, and Files registers no CLI or URI handler; the `move` and `rename` CLI handlers are Obsidian core's (§2A, Second ruling). The link update after a rename or move is a bulk change the person confirms in the update-links modal, which the ruling makes deliberate whatever its scope, and choosing Always update there is a deliberate dialog choice. Deleting a note prompts first, and unlinked attachments are trashed only through the default `ask` checklist modal. Setting named: `app.json` `"alwaysUpdateLinks": false` (the default) keeps the modal. The Auto Note Mover verifier (§4.6) read the link update as part of the person's rename whatever this key says, so the pass may not depend on it (`app.js:1@byte 1777251`; `@byte 1778685`; `@byte 1779262`; `@byte 1478128`; `@byte 1479142-1480088`; `@byte 1783000`; core CLI `@byte 1639396`, `@byte 1640035`).
- ◊ 2026-10-04 live: B1 pass — registered on 1.13.7 and on at first open: enabled, loaded, 3 commands, view `file-explorer`; no error captured. The rename prompt (§6.1) was not run (`/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/step1-core.log`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass‡§ → fail‡§∆ — a confirm no longer changes the result. Failing paths: through the menu, drag or rename field it creates notes, folders, canvases and bases in a folder the person picks, and renames, moves and deletes files; after any rename or move the link updater rewrites links in every linking note, wiki/ included, whether confirmed in the modal or run under `alwaysUpdateLinks: true`; deleting a note can trash its unlinked attachments. Undetected: wiki/ renames, deletions and link rewrites, and a bare new note in log/, which the pre-commit hook stamps `type: "daily"` so it passes verify; in literature/ evidence-layer catches renames, deletions and body rewrites. Setting: none; `app.json` `"alwaysUpdateLinks": false` only puts the modal in front and the updater has no ignore-filter check, `"deleteUnlinkedAttachments": "never"` confines only the attachment trashing, and `core-plugins.json` `"file-explorer": false` disables. Verifier: confirmed, adding that renaming or deleting a linked wiki/ page without updating its links leaves dangling links that captured-set flags, while deleting an unlinked page, a rename whose links were updated and the link rewrites themselves pass (`app.js:1@byte 1777251-1779300`, `@byte 1478128`, `@byte 1783000`, `@byte 1783443`; `research_vault/stamp.py:131`, `:150`; `templates/git/pre-commit:24-26`; `captured.py:173-188`).

It has no CLI or URI handler and no event hook; the core `command` CLI handler can open the modal but cannot press Start. No setting is needed. **Note composer (`note-composer`).**

- Writes: merge inserts the source into the destination, trashes the source (`mergeFile` `@3405600`) and re-points links through `runAsyncLinkUpdate` and `updateAllLinks`, the same `alwaysUpdateLinks` prompt rename uses (`@1774483`). Extract writes the selection into a chosen or new note (`createNewMarkdownFile` at `getNewFileParent(currentFile.path)`, `@3407437`) and replaces it in the source per `replacementText`. `note-composer.json` keys: `askBeforeMerging` (default true, `@3400697`), `replacementText` link, embed or none (default link, `@3410959`), `template`.

The verifier found that the code alone shows the merge prompt fires (`@1772990`), so no live check is needed for that, and added a key the desk missed: an extract follows `app.json` `newFileLocation` relative to the source note, so `"current"` puts the extract beside its source inside literature/ or wiki/. Named key: `newFileLocation` `"root"` (default), or `"folder"` with `newFileFolderPath` outside the machine surfaces. The floor uses `"current"` (`a3d22d0a .obsidian/app.json:6`).
- B4: an extract to a new note is an untyped `.md` that fails §11 rules 1-2 unless `note-composer.json` `template` carries frontmatter with a type, or `newFileLocation` routes it to inbox/. The template has to contain `{{content}}`, or the content is appended after it (`@3405300`).
- ‡ 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass → fail. Merge and extract both run inside Obsidian's link updater. Links in other notes that point at the merged source, or into the extracted range, are rewritten, machine surfaces included, with no ignore-filter check and only the prompt as a gate. No seedable key confines that fan-out (`app.js:1@3404537`, = `@byte 3409527`; `@3405935`, = `@byte 3410925`; `@byte 1777402-1779300`). `app.json` `"newFileLocation": "root"`, or `"folder"` with `"newFileFolderPath": "inbox"`, still confines the extract's new note.

The extract's untyped note conforms under the untyped-inbox deviation, the replacement link comes from the link generator, and rewritten links keep their form (`@3407437`; `@byte 1741795`; `okf-conformance.md:36`, `:41`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 fail → pass — nothing is counted. Merge, split and extract are commands or menu items the person runs on the active note or selection. Merge asks first while `askBeforeMerging` is true, and merge and extract re-point links through the update-links confirm modal. Its only hooks add file-menu and editor-menu items, and it has no CLI or URI handler. Settings: `note-composer.json` `"askBeforeMerging": true`, which a seeded `note-composer.json` must carry because the plugin defaults it to true only when no data file exists, and `app.json` `"alwaysUpdateLinks": false`. `app.json` `"newFileLocation": "folder"` with `"newFileFolderPath": "inbox"` (or `"root"`) is a fact about where the person's extract lands (`app.js:1@byte 3413234`; `@byte 3409527`; `@byte 3410925`; `@byte 1779262`; `@byte 3405687`).

### 4.2 Core plugins, `B-core-2` (offsets are bytes)

The verifier re-read the asar and confirmed its digest against the release. Every row's B1 is confirmed as stated.

**Page preview (`page-preview`).**

- Writes, desk: nothing in the vault; only an optional `page-preview.json` mapping a hover-source id to a require-modifier boolean. `defaultOn` true (`app.js:1@byte 3444364`).
- Writes, verifier: the hover popover is an editable embed (`@2515704`; `onLinkHover` at `@3446409` calls the popover class), so a user can type into any linked note, literature/ and wiki/ included, and no `page-preview.json` key turns that off.
- B2 pass†: result stands; the cite was wrong (`Page preview.md:5` is blank; the description is at `:4`). Comparison `:94` (arrange across sources through graph, backlinks and transclusion) is confirmed.
- B3 pass†: desk basis was read-only toward notes; refuted. It passes only because every write is user-typed, as in the main editor.
- B4 pass†: desk basis was writes nothing; refuted. Popover edits are ordinary Markdown.
- Exit (desk): Set `"page-preview": false` in `core-plugins.json`; it wrote nothing into the vault.
- ◊ 2026-10-04 live: B1 pass — registered on 1.13.7 and on at first open: enabled, loaded, 0 commands; no error captured (`/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/step1-core.log`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass† → fail†∆ — the hover popover is an editable embed: clicking into it, or a source-mode hover, opens an editor that saves into the hovered note, any linked note, wiki/ and fulltext/ included; clicking an empty missing-footnote placeholder in a popover appends a footnote definition through `vault.process`. Triggers: hovering a link in the editor, Backlinks, Search, Graph or any other hover source. Undetected outside literature/. Setting: none; `page-preview.json` sets only which hover sources need a modifier key, and `core-plugins.json` `"page-preview": false` disables. Verifier: confirmed; a possible further path, not verified, is the embed class's inline-title rename handler, which calls `fileManager.renameFile` (`app.js:1@byte 3446409`, `@byte 2516623`, `@byte 2645358`, `@byte 2650346`, `@byte 2647068`).

**Properties view (`properties`).**

- Writes: no automatic writes and no config file. A rename in the All properties view calls `fileManager.renameProperty` (`@3458436`), which rewrites the frontmatter of every cached file holding that key and skips files where `isUserIgnored` is true (`@1780926`); the rename also moves type assignments in `.obsidian/types.json`. The verifier added a vault-wide Delete property (`fileManager.deleteProperty` through `processFrontMatter`, just before `@1780926`; UI callers `@3453104`, `@3454282`, `@3457532`) under the same guard. Filters compile to a case-insensitive regex anchored at `^`, or a `/regex/` literal (`@1759015`); the default `userIgnoreFilters` is null (`@1252041`). `defaultOn` true (`@3447658`).
- B2: floor (comparison `:95`; help `Properties view.md:9-20`).
- B3 named key: `app.json` `"userIgnoreFilters": ["literature/","fulltext/","wiki/","log/","system/","inbox/review-queue.md"]`. Trade-off recorded, not resolved: the same filter drops those files from Search results (help `Search.md:16-17`), ranks them lower in Quick switcher (`Quick switcher.md:33-34`), and, per the verifier, removes them from Tags view (`getTags` `@1743651`) and the All properties list. The verifier also found the list omits root `log.md`, which vault `AGENTS.md:7` names as machine-written (`okf.py:1` is its single writer); `^log/` does not match it, a nil risk while `log.md` carries no frontmatter. Bases calls the same `renameProperty`.
- B4: plain YAML. A rename or, per the verifier, a delete of `type` would breach OKF §11 rule 2 outside the filtered folders; verify's okf-frontmatter check reports either (`okf-conformance.md:9-10,13`).
- Exit (desk): Set `"properties": false`; renames it already made are ordinary frontmatter edits, and capture regenerates them away under literature/. The verifier's correction adds that a deleted `verified` or `failed-verification` list is never restored (`literature_notes.py:475-523`).
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass unchanged, with the seed key restated against the guard roster: `app.json` `"userIgnoreFilters": ["literature/", "log/", "wiki/", "fulltext/", "system/propagations/", "log.md", "inbox/review-queue.md", "system/bibliography.json"]`. This replaces the list above: `system/` is dropped and root `log.md` is added. The vault-wide rename and delete skip ignored files, and each entry compiles to a `^`-anchored, case-insensitive prefix (`app.js:1@byte 1780926`, ignore skip near `@byte 1781311`; `@byte 1780126`; compile `@byte 1758536`, test loop `@byte 1759015`; `hooks/pretooluse_guard.py:11-23`). The §7.7 trade-off still applies. B4 pass unchanged, on a person-content carve-out the B4 ruling does not state: renaming or deleting `type` is content the person chose, and verify reports it. A vault-wide delete would still strip `type` from projects/ drafts outside the filter (`@byte 1780126`; `okf-conformance.md:9-10`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass → pass — basis moved: nothing is counted. Its hooks only refresh its views and status bar, it has no CLI or URI handler (`property:set` and `property:remove` are Obsidian core's and never check this plugin), and its vault-wide rename and delete run only from the person's menu action or inline rename on a property they chose. `app.json` `"userIgnoreFilters"` (the roster list above) no longer decides B3; the verifier found the pass holds either way, because both bulk paths skip ignored files (`app.js:1@byte 3457292`; `@byte 3458436`; `@byte 1780421`; `@byte 1781311`; `@byte 1621207`).
- ◊ 2026-10-04 live: B1 pass — registered on 1.13.7 and on at first open: enabled, loaded, 2 commands, views `all-properties` and `file-properties`; no error captured. The reserved-`tags` check (§6.1, §7.19) was not run (`/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/step1-core.log`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass → fail∆ — earlier passes did not record the File properties pane. The `file-properties` view (opened by its commands, Add file property included) edits the shown note's frontmatter through `saveFrontmatter` and `vault.process`. Undetected: any key on a wiki/ page, and non-capture keys on a literature note such as the machine-written `verified` and `failed-verification` lists, since evidence-layer watches capture-owned keys only and okf-frontmatter catches only a lost `type`. Setting: `app.json` `userIgnoreFilters` set to the roster list confines the vault-wide rename and delete only (both skip ignored files); nothing confines the pane; `core-plugins.json` `"properties": false` disables. Verifier: confirmed (`app.js:1@byte 3448303`, `@byte 3463739-3463900`, `@byte 1780900-1781500`; `research_vault/lints.py:618`; `literature_notes.py:64-68`; `events.py:10`, `:72`).
- ◇ 2026-10-04 fourth ruling: B3 fail∆ → pass∆◇ — the File properties pane is file-bound and saves only its own file's frontmatter, the open note's, so it is not judged. Counted: the All properties vault-wide rename and delete, which rewrite frontmatter across other notes; both skip user-ignored files one by one, and a type change writes only `types.json`. Setting: `app.json` `"userIgnoreFilters": ["literature/", "log/", "wiki/", "fulltext/", "system/propagations/", "log.md", "inbox/review-queue.md", "system/bibliography.json"]`. It has no CLI handler of its own. Verifier: confirmed (`app.js:1@byte 1780100-1781000`, `@byte 1758450-1759100`, `@byte 3453995`, `@byte 3463739-3463900`, `@byte 3465285`).

**Publish (`publish`).**

- Writes, desk: `.obsidian/publish.json` (`siteId`, `host`, `included`, `excluded`; `saveData` at `@3548459`) and, on user action, uploads to Obsidian's servers. No `defaultOn` (`@3541764`); loads only behind `Ab.publish` (`@3825058`), which is true (`@1109776`). Uploading machine-surface content off the machine was recorded as egress, a concern separate from writing into the vault.
- Writes, verifier: two user-initiated restores write the server copy into the vault: Use live version overwrites a local file (`vault.modifyBinary`, `@3540025`), and the context-menu action on a deleted row recreates it (`createFolder` plus `createBinary`, `@3515301` onward).
- B2 fail: a name collision. The step `publish` is research-vault's armed, fail-closed gate (`skills/publish/SKILL.md:11,27`); Obsidian Publish hosts a website; the floor has no publish stage (comparison `:99`).
- B3 pass†: desk basis no write path into notes; refuted. Passes only when configured. Named key: `publish.json` `"excluded": ["literature","fulltext","wiki","log","system"]` (folder names without a trailing slash, matched as `path + '/'` at `@3552892`). Residual: frontmatter `publish: true` overrides the exclusion; the single files `inbox/review-queue.md` and `log.md` cannot be excluded except by whole folder; a remote file whose local copy is excluded is listed as deleted, so `excluded` does not shield a file already on the site. The publish-file command honours the flag (`@3542396`).
- B4 pass†: desk basis writes nothing into notes; refuted. What it restores is earlier published bytes, plain Markdown.
- Exit (desk): Set `"publish": false`; publish.json is inert, but a site already published stays hosted on Obsidian's servers until it is unpublished.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass† and B4 pass† unchanged. B3 needs no key: the only vault writes are the two use-live-version restores, each on one row the person picked. The changed-row restore confirms first; the deleted-row restore does not (`app.js:1@byte 3540025`; `@byte 3515301`). `publish.json` `excluded` governs upload egress, not vault writes, and the `publish:add` and `publish:remove` CLI handlers are egress too (`@byte 3545581`; `@byte 3547045`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass† → fail†∆ — the confirm on one route no longer changes the result. Use live version on a changed row (confirm modal, then `modifyBinary` over the local file) and on a deleted row (`createFolder`, then `createBinary`) writes the site's copy into the vault at its original path. `publish.json` `excluded` governs uploads only; frontmatter `publish: true` overrides the exclusion, and a site that already holds machine-surface files lists them for restore. Undetected in wiki/; a literature note restored to an earlier published version passes the attestation rule. Judged with a site connected, as for Sync. Setting: none; `core-plugins.json` `"publish": false` disables. Verifier: confirmed (`app.js:1@byte 3515200-3516300`, `@byte 3539700-3540300`, `@byte 3552892`; `research_vault/lints.py:661-678`).

**Quick switcher (`switcher`).**

- Writes: when the typed name matches nothing, a new empty note (Enter or Shift+Enter) at `app.json` `newFileLocation` (`getMarkdownNewFileParent` `@1774672`; defaults root and `/` at `@1251971`). The modal always allows creation (`@3555524`); `onChooseSuggestion` calls `openLinkText` (`@3556435`), and a linktext containing `/` skips `getNewFileParent` and creates its folders (`@1775543`). `switcher.json` defaults: `showExistingOnly` false, `showAttachments` true, `showAllFileTypes` false (`@3554149`); none turns off creation. `defaultOn` true (`@3554242`).
- B2 pass†: result stands; the desk cited `Quick switcher.md:25`, which is the create text; the find-by-name text is at `:5` and `:17`, and the comparison's find-and-assemble line spans `:61-62`.
- B3 named key: `app.json` `"newFileLocation": "folder"` with `"newFileFolderPath": "inbox"`. The verifier added that `"current"` would place a note beside the active file, inside literature/ when a literature note is open, and that `switcher.json` `"showExistingOnly": true` removes the unresolved-link route to creation. No key stops an explicitly typed path such as `literature/x`.
- B4: passes only with the inbox routing; an empty root-level note fails okf-frontmatter (`structure.py:50-51`; `okf-conformance.md:9,36`).

A typed path such as `literature/x` is the person choosing the file and is not judged under B3 (`app.js:1@byte 1774672`; `@byte 1775543`). For B4 the same typed path creates an empty untyped `.md` outside inbox/. It is left pass as a person-chosen location, a carve-out the B4 ruling does not state.
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass → pass — basis moved: nothing is counted. A note is created only when the person presses Enter on a typed name that matches nothing, and it has no CLI, URI or writing hook. `app.json` `newFileLocation` and `newFileFolderPath` no longer decide B3; they place the person's note (`app.js:1@byte 3556435`; `@byte 1774672`).

- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass → fail∆ — Enter or Shift+Enter on a typed name that matches nothing, or on an unresolved-link suggestion, creates an empty file at that path through `openLinkText`; a path-qualified name lands in the named folder whatever `newFileLocation` says. Undetected: a date-named bare log/ note (stamped `type: "daily"` by the pre-commit hook, which verify then matches), a nested `index.md` or `log.md` under wiki/, and a `.canvas` or `.base` name in any machine folder; other empty notes are flagged by okf-frontmatter or evidence-layer. Setting: none; the modal always allows creation, `switcher.json` `"showExistingOnly": true` hides only unresolved suggestions, `app.json` `"newFileLocation": "folder"` with `"newFileFolderPath": "inbox"` places only names without a slash, and `core-plugins.json` `"switcher": false` disables. **Slash commands (`slash-command`).**

- Writes: nothing of its own (`@3558529`: no `loadData`, no vault write); any write comes from the command it invokes. `defaultOn` false.
- B2 fail†: result stands; the cited `Slash commands.md:5` is blank and the description is at `:4`.
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass → fail∆ — earlier passes recorded that it writes nothing of its own. When the person picks a suggested command, `selectSuggestion` empties the trigger range before the cursor in the active note with `editor.replaceRange`, then runs the command: a removal from the active note, undetected in a wiki/ page. Setting: none; the plugin has no settings, and `core-plugins.json` `"slash-command": false` (the default) disables. Verifier correction, result unchanged: the trigger matches only a slash at line start or after whitespace, followed by characters that are neither spaces nor slashes up to the cursor, so the removed range is one space-free token, and pre-existing characters are removed only inside it. The narrowest failing path of any row (`app.js:1@byte 3558991`, `@byte 3559437`).
- ◇ 2026-10-04 fourth ruling: B3 fail∆ → pass∆◇ — its one write removes the person's typed trigger token before the cursor in the note being edited, then runs the chosen command; that is open-note editing and is not judged. Commands it runs are judged on their own plugins, and its reach into popover or card editors belongs to the plugin hosting the embed. Counted: nothing; it has no CLI handler. Setting: none needed. Verifier: confirmed (`app.js:1@byte 3558991`, `@byte 3559436`).

- Writes: none (`@3560646`). The slide separator is an ordinary thematic break (help `Slides.md:22`). No `defaultOn`.

**Sync (`sync`).**

- Writes: once connected to a paid remote, every file it pulls, machine surfaces included; it merges conflicting edits by default and stores file-recovery backups. It skips dot-prefixed paths except `.obsidian`, so fulltext/ syncs and `.research-vault/` does not. Settings (excluded folders, allowed types, conflict action) live per device in IndexedDB `<appId>-sync` (`@3655481`), with `conflictAction` defaulting to merge (`@3656363`); `_allowSyncFile` matches folder prefixes only (`@3630420`). `defaultOn` true (`@3643713`), behind `Ab.sync`, which is true.
- B3 fail: it writes into every machine surface as a replication writer. Its one exclusion, `ignoreFolders`, is per-device IndexedDB and cannot be seeded or name a single file; no `.obsidian/` key can be named. The only seedable control is `core-plugins.json` `"sync": false`; leaving the key out enables Sync.
- B4 needs-live: whether an automatic merge of two frontmatter edits still parses as YAML (OKF §11 rules 1-2). The verifier added desk evidence: the merge is a character-level diff-match-patch three-way patch with no YAML awareness, and failed hunks are dropped silently (`@2445168`; merge branch `@3674076` onward).
- Verifier, missed: `patchCorePluginsFile` (`@3682372`) forces `"sync": true` into `core-plugins.json` when Sync merges that file, so a seeded `false` can be reverted from another device (assembly spec §15 item 17 drift).
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 fail and B4 needs-live unchanged. No `.obsidian/` key exists: `ignoreFolders` and `conflictAction` live per device in IndexedDB, and the only seedable control is `core-plugins.json` `"sync": false`, which turns it off. `sync:restore` is a further CLI write (`app.js:1@byte 3655481`; `@byte 3630420`; `@byte 3650356`; `@byte 2445168`; `@byte 3656363`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 fail → fail — basis restated under the third ruling. Once connected, every pulled file is written automatically into every machine surface, and conflicting edits are merged with a character-level patch; `sync:restore` and version-history restores write old versions; `patchCorePluginsFile` forces `"sync": true` back into `core-plugins.json` when Sync merges or pulls it. Undetected in wiki/, log/ appends and fulltext/, and restored older literature versions pass the attestation rule. Setting: none seedable; `ignoreFolders` and `conflictAction` live per device in IndexedDB, and `core-plugins.json` `"sync": false` disables and can be reverted from another device. Verifier: confirmed (`app.js:1@byte 3655481`, `@byte 3630420`, `@byte 3650356`, `@byte 3656363`, `@byte 3676073`, `@byte 3681167`, `@byte 3682372`).

**Tags view (`tag-pane`).**

- Writes: none (`@3686903`); sort and nesting live in view state.
- B2 pass†: desk pass with a needs-live caveat (literature notes carry Zotero tags as `{tag, type}` mappings, `research_vault/literature_notes.py:51`, `:317-323`; `tests/test_literature_notes.py:447`). The verifier settled the caveat at desk: `updateTags` (`@3694677`) calls `metadataCache.getTags`, which skips ignored files and calls `getAllTags` (`@1743651`, `@1378598`), and `parseFrontMatterTags` keeps string items only (`@1371784`). Zotero tags on literature notes never appear, so the floor's Zotero-tag hierarchy (comparison `:200-204`) is not served; the pass survives on tags typed by hand in prose notes. Help cite: the description is at `Tags view.md:6`, not `:5`.
- Still needs-live: whether the in-note properties editor flags the reserved `tags` key and invites a rewrite (validator `@1865332`; array check `@687829`, which requires every item to be a string).
- ◊ 2026-10-04 live: B1 pass — registered on 1.13.7 and on at first open: enabled, loaded, 1 command, view `tag`; no error captured. The properties-editor check above was not run (`/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/step1-core.log`).

**Templates (`templates`).**

- Writes: template text into the active note only when the user runs the insert command (`insertTemplate` `@3699975`); no on-create hook. `templates.json` keys: `folder` (no default), `dateFormat`, `timeFormat` (settings tab `@3702556`). `defaultOn` true (`@3696276`).
- B2: floor (the floor writes notes from templates through Templater; comparison `:64`, `:92`) and long-form through `system/templates/project.md`, filled by hand per `skills/project-flow/SKILL.md:48`.
- B4: inserted text is plain Markdown. Two seams if `folder` is `system/templates`: substitution matches `{{title}}` case-insensitively and expands `{{date}}` and `{{time}}` (`@1565847`), so inserting `project.md` fills `{{TITLE}}` with the file name (`draft` for `projects/NAME/draft.md`) and leaves `{{ACTOR}}` and `{{NOW}}` literal, against project-flow's substitution rule; and inserting `daily.md` (type daily) breaks the folder-type rule where a folder derives another type. The verifier narrowed the desk's "outside log/": the break happens only where a folder derives a type (`projects/<name>/draft.md`, literature/); root, wiki/ and system/ derive none and inbox/ is exempt. It also found that at a cursor other than 0,0 the template's frontmatter is merged through `metadataEditor.insertProperties`, while at 0,0 the whole template, frontmatter block included, is inserted raw above any existing frontmatter.
- ‡ 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass → fail. The re-judgement kept pass on the command path alone. The verifier refuted it, and its result is recorded: the `template:insert` Obsidian-CLI handler lets an external caller insert a template into whatever file is active, which may be in literature/ or wiki/. The CLI switch is per-machine IPC with no `.obsidian/` key (`app.js:1@byte 3698549`; `@byte 3699975`; `@byte 3478003`). The verifier rated this lower in confidence than File recovery, because the target is the file the person has open. Resolved 2026-10-04 by the second ruling.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B4 pass unchanged. Seed key: `templates.json` `"folder": "system/templates"`. The seeded templates carry `type` in parseable frontmatter and contain no links (`daily.md:1-3`; `project.md:1-6`; `app.js:1@byte 3699975`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 fail → fail — the confidence note above is settled: the `template:insert` Obsidian-CLI handler is remote whatever file it targets, and it inserts a named template into whatever file the active editor holds, a literature/ or wiki/ note included. The person's own Insert template command is not counted. No setting confines it. `templates.json` `folder` chooses only where templates come from; leaving it unset makes the handler refuse, but it also stops the person's Insert template command (only the date and time inserts keep working), so it disables the plugin's function rather than confining it, and it conflicts with the B4 seed `"folder": "system/templates"`. The CLI switch is per-machine IPC. Hinge: if an unset `folder` counted as a confining setting, the cell would return to pass (`app.js:1@byte 3698549`; `@byte 3699318`; `@byte 3700719`; `@byte 3478003`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 fail‡ → fail‡ — basis widened: the person's own commands are now judged. Insert template, Insert current date, Insert current time and the ribbon item write into the active note, and the `template:insert` CLI handler inserts a named template into whatever file the active editor holds. Undetected in wiki/ and fulltext/. Setting: none; leaving `templates.json` `folder` unset blocks only Insert template, the date and time inserts still write, and `core-plugins.json` `"templates": false` disables. Verifier: confirmed; `insertTemplate` calls `replaceSelection` and merges the template's frontmatter through `insertProperties`, and the date and time inserts call `editor.transaction` (`app.js:1@byte 3698549`, `@byte 3699975`, `@byte 3700470-3700720`, `@byte 3701682`).

**Unique note creator (`zk-prefixer`).**

- Writes: on command, a timestamp-named note, empty unless a template is set; it can also insert a unique link. `createUniqueNote` uses `folder`, else `getNewFileParent` (`@3709216`); the Obsidian CLI handler uses the same logic (`@3706848`). `zk-prefixer.json` keys: `folder`, `template`, `format` (default `YYYYMMDDHHmm`, `@3704981`). No `defaultOn` (`@3705057`).
- B2 fail†: result stands; the cited `Unique note creator.md:5` is the frontmatter delimiter, and the Zettelkasten description is at `:6`.
- B3 named key: `zk-prefixer.json` `"folder": "inbox"`. B4: passes when so configured; a template carrying `type: fleeting` would conform outright. The seed ships no such template today.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass unchanged. Seed key: `zk-prefixer.json` `"folder": "inbox"`. It confines the note created by command, ribbon, URI and CLI; a missing folder writes nothing (`app.js:1@byte 3709216`; `@byte 3705843`; `@byte 3707665`).
- ‡ 2026-10-03 normalisation: B4 pass → fail. The insert-unique-link command builds a wikilink unconditionally and never reads `useMarkdownLinks`, so no seedable key avoids a wikilink that the deviation register does not cover (OKF §6.1). `zk-prefixer.json` `"folder": "inbox"` covers only the created note (`app.js:1@byte 3710157`; `okf-conformance.md:41`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass → fail∆ — the Insert unique link editor command (`insert-unique-link`) replaces the selection in the active note with a hard-coded wikilink to a new timestamp name and creates no note. Setting: `zk-prefixer.json` `"folder": "inbox"` confines note creation (command, ribbon, `obsidian://unique` URI, CLI) only; nothing confines the link insert; `core-plugins.json` `"zk-prefixer": false` disables. Verifier correction, result unchanged: the claim that nothing detects it in wiki/ is false, because the inserted link always dangles and commit-closing captured-set flags it in a wiki/ page whatever `useMarkdownLinks` says. The fail rests on fulltext/ (the commit gate never sees it), root `log.md` (shape check only) and an insert at the end of a log/ day file (an append) (`app.js:1@byte 3705484`, `@byte 3709216`, `@byte 3710147`; `research_vault/captured.py:173-188`; `templates/vault/gitignore:5`).
- ◇ 2026-10-04 fourth ruling: B3 fail∆ → pass∆◇ — Insert unique link replaces the selection in the active note and is not judged. Counted: creation of the timestamp-named note from the command, the ribbon, the `obsidian://unique` URI and the CLI handler. Setting: `zk-prefixer.json` `"folder": "inbox"`, which places that note from every trigger; a missing folder writes nothing. Verifier: confirmed with an attack run. The CLI's `name` is appended raw to the timestamp, so slashes only nest under inbox/; a `..` segment survives path normalisation, but the adapter treats a dot-prefixed segment as hidden, so the handler throws before writing a note and leaves at most an empty directory (an app property, §2A Fourth ruling) (`app.js:1@byte 3705843-3706900`, `@byte 3708272`, `@byte 3709216`, `@byte 1775859-1776700`, `@byte 1392831`, `@byte 566809`, `@byte 550980`).

**Web viewer (`webviewer`).**

- Writes: Save page writes the page, converted to Markdown, into `markdownPath` (`@2950911`; setting `@2936366`); Extract selection writes a new note at the vault root with no folder setting (`@2953742`); Save image writes to `app.json` `attachmentFolderPath` (`@2954839`). All user-initiated. `webviewer.json` defaults: `openExternalURLs` true, `enableAdblocking` true, `homepage`, `markdownPath` empty (vault root), `searchEngine` duckduckgo (`@2931251`, `@2930916`). `defaultOn` false; loads only on desktop with `Ab.webViewer` (`@3825725`).
- B2 fail: the help page pitches it at web research (`Web viewer.md:4`), but the step `search` runs through find-sources and Zotero admission (assembly spec `:143`), and a saved page is a source-like note outside Zotero, which vault `AGENTS.md:9` rules out.
- B3 named key: `"markdownPath": "inbox"`; extract selection always goes to the root.
- B4 fail: saved pages carry no frontmatter (rule 1) and no source URL (`getReaderModeContent` `@2949023` returns Readability output converted to Markdown). A root-level extract is untyped, blocks okf-frontmatter, and stamp-type cannot repair it because the root derives no type.
- Exit (desk): Set `"webviewer": false`; pages it saved remain as ordinary untyped Markdown wherever it put them, awaiting triage.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass and B4 fail unchanged. B3 seed keys: `webviewer.json` `"markdownPath": "inbox"` and `app.json` `"attachmentFolderPath": "/"` (default). Saved pages, root-level extracts and saved images all land off the roster. Titles have `/`, `\` and `:` replaced, so no file name can nest into a machine folder (`app.js:1@byte 2950911`; `@byte 2953742`; `@byte 2954839`; `@2703426` (char)). For B4 no key suffices: Extract selection always writes a frontmatter-less `.md` at the root, and it has no folder key.
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass → pass — basis moved: nothing is counted into the vault. The `web` CLI handler only opens a URL, and Save page, Extract selection and Save image are commands, buttons or menu items the person runs. `webviewer.json` `"markdownPath": "inbox"` and `app.json` `"attachmentFolderPath": "/"` no longer decide B3; they place the person's saves (`app.js:1@byte 2933997`; `@byte 2950911`; `@byte 2933104`; `@byte 2942004`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass → pass — the keys decide B3 again. Save page creates a new note under `markdownPath` with a sanitised title through `getAvailablePath`, so it never overwrites; Extract selection creates a new note at the vault root under a sanitised, available name, so it cannot land on root `log.md` or `inbox/review-queue.md`, which the seed ships, and a colliding title gets a suffix; Save image goes through `getAvailablePathForAttachments`; the `web` CLI handler only opens a URL. Setting: `webviewer.json` `"markdownPath": "inbox"` and `app.json` `"attachmentFolderPath": "/"` (the default). Verifier: confirmed (`app.js:1@byte 2949023`, `@byte 2950900-2951250`, `@byte 2953700-2954000`, `@byte 2954839`, `@byte 2708295`).
