- Writes: only `.obsidian/workspaces.json` (`workspaces`, `active`; `saveData` `@3720301`). No `defaultOn` (`@3715972`).
- B2: floor; the floor switches workspaces through Advanced URI and QuickAdd (comparison `:64-65`; help `Workspaces.md:4`).
- Fact: the seed's `.gitignore:2` pattern `.obsidian/workspace*` also matches `workspaces.json` (verified with `git check-ignore`, re-run by the verifier in `/tmp`), so `scaffold`'s `_trackable_paths` would skip a seeded layout.
- ◊ 2026-10-04 live: B1 pass — registered on 1.13.7 and off at first open. `enable(true)` resolved: enabled, loaded, 4 commands. Enabling rewrote `core-plugins.json` (sha256 `c3fa0210…`) and created `.obsidian/workspaces.json` (38 B, an empty workspaces map and an empty active name, sha256 `3cd59274…`), which git status lists as ignored through the vault `.gitignore` pattern `.obsidian/workspace*`. `disable(true)` returned `core-plugins.json` to `763cf20a…`, and the restore removed `workspaces.json`. No error captured (`/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/step1-core.log`; `/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/step2-restricted.log`; `/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/step4-restore.log`).

### 4.3 Top-20 community plugins

No research-vault vault exists, so nothing was installed; release assets were read under `/tmp` (at the desk read, 2026-10-03; superseded: the 2026-10-04 run copied pinned release assets, one plugin at a time, into a scratch research-vault scaffold, §6.5). The Obsidian registry on this machine (`/mnt/c/Users/<user>/AppData/Roaming/obsidian/obsidian.json`) lists three vaults, none with the literature/, wiki/ and `.raw/` layout (matches assembly spec:248).

**Excalidraw (`obsidian-excalidraw-plugin`), `B-community-1`.**

- Pin: release 2.28.1, tag commit `128a9ef47bdc2c4fd342cede26ab7dedd6dcfe13` (2026-09-29); HEAD `f30b4c5` has `manifest.json:4` 2.28.1. Assets: `main.js` (4,987,704 B), `manifest.json`, `styles.css` (353,360 B).
- Licence: AGPL-3.0 (`LICENSE @128a9ef`, 661 lines; GitHub SPDX AGPL-3.0). The only third-party notice in `main.js` is pako 2.1.0, licensed MIT AND Zlib (`main.js:5`). `package.json @128a9ef` lists other bundled dependencies with no notice: `@zsviczian/excalidraw` 0.18.140 (embedded, compressed), react and react-dom 19.2.8, roughjs, lz-string, chroma-js, nanoid and others. The verifier found `package.json:33` declares MIT, which conflicts with the AGPL LICENSE file and GitHub's SPDX.
- Writes: `.excalidraw.md` drawings in `folder` (default `Excalidraw`, `src/core/settingsDefaults.ts:498`); their frontmatter carries only `excalidraw-plugin: parsed` and tags (`src/constants/constants.ts:469-479`), and the scene is LZ-string-compressed while `compress` is true (`:515`). On user action it creates `Excalidraw/Scripts`, `Excalidraw/Template` and `Excalidraw/CJK Fonts` (`:507-511`). SVG and PNG exports only with `autoexport` on (default off, `:591-592`). The create-and-embed command inserts an embed into the active note, and the drawing goes to the core attachment folder unless `embedUseExcalidrawFolder` is set (`src/core/managers/CommandManager.ts:649-657`). Its own `data.json`; an image cache in IndexedDB (`src/shared/ImageCache.ts:82`). Verifier additions: a fresh install sets `libraryStorageMode` to vault and writes `Excalidraw/Libraries/local-library.excalidrawlib` (`PluginSettingsManager.ts:178-197`; `settingsDefaults.ts:499-501`; `StencilLibraryManager.ts:461-485`); `cropFolder` and `annotateFolder` default to empty, which resolves to the host note's core attachment folder with a `.md` output (`src/utils/fileUtils.ts:508-547`); the file-menu create-new-drawing writes into whichever folder the user clicks (`CommandManager.ts:190-201`), and in literature/ stamp-type would derive `type: literature` onto a drawing that verify then reads as a literature note.
- B2 fail: a general drawing tool; no diagramming step (assembly spec `:146`, `:168`), and the floor's plugin list (comparison `:64-67`) omits it. Nearest step: long-form, for figures. The verifier noted that excluding long-form is a judgement, since ACM manuscripts carry figures.
- B3 pass†: desk named `data.json` `embedUseExcalidrawFolder: true`, with `folder`, `templateFilePath`, `scriptFolderPath`, `fontAssetsPath`, `cropFolder` and `annotateFolder` outside the machine surfaces, and said the defaults already do that (`settingsDefaults.ts:498,506-511,590-592`; `FileManager.ts:146-152,630-660`). The verifier refuted that for `cropFolder` and `annotateFolder` (both default empty, `settingsDefaults.ts:504-505`): a crop or annotate run from a literature note can drop a drawing into literature/, and `embedUseExcalidrawFolder` does not cover it. The pass holds only with `cropFolder` and `annotateFolder` set to explicit paths under `Excalidraw/` as well. The file-menu create is a user-chosen placement, a hand edit only under the gesture reading (§7.1).
- B4 fail: at defaults each drawing is an `.md` with no `type` (OKF §11 rule 2). verify scans every `.md` outside `.git`, `.raw` and `.vault-meta` and reports okf-frontmatter UNMATCHED, the commit surface closes on that check (`research_vault/structure.py:36`, `:91-100`; `research_vault/verify.py:752-757`), and stamp-type cannot repair it because `Excalidraw/` derives no type (`research_vault/stamp.py:29-30`). With `compress: true` the scene is unreadable without the plugin. Seedable ways out named by the desk: `compress: false` (plain JSON); a `Template.excalidraw.md` carrying `type`, copied verbatim into new drawings (`FileManager.ts:186-190`); `compatibilityMode: true`, which makes new drawings non-`.md` `.excalidraw` files. The desk's claim that script-store installs still land as `.md` was refuted: they are stored as `.js` when `allowJavaScriptFiles` and `storeScriptFilesAsJavaScript` are both true, and both are seedable (`src/utils/scriptFileUtils.ts:47-50`). The verifier added that crop and annotate outputs are also untyped `.md` (`fileUtils.ts:515`, `:535`).
- B5 pass: AGPL-3.0 allows redistribution on conditions (`LICENSE @128a9ef:1-2`; §4 `:183`; aggregate `:230`; §6 `:233`; §6(d) `:263`). The licence text travels with the files, and the minified `main.js` is a non-source form, so the Corresponding Source (the TypeScript at the tag) or a §6(d) pointer goes with it. The aggregate clause leaves research-vault's MIT licence unaffected. The embedded MIT dependencies have no notice in the bundle, so a NOTICE file would be needed; font licences cannot be checked from the compressed bundle.
- Exit (desk): not one line, and the desk recorded that as the finding: what remains are `.excalidraw.md` drawings with compressed scenes and no `type`, plus optional exports and the `Excalidraw/` folders, and each drawing needs decompressing or exporting and typing before it is ordinary vault content. The verifier then wrote the single (long) line shown in §3.2. Both are recorded.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass† unchanged. The file-menu create into a right-clicked folder is now a person-chosen placement and is not judged. The judged creates (create-and-embed drawings, crop and annotate outputs, the library on install) are confined by these seed keys: `data.json` `"embedUseExcalidrawFolder": true`, `"cropFolder": "inbox/excalidraw"`, `"annotateFolder"` an explicit path off the roster (under inbox/ if B4's second leg is to close), `"folder": "Excalidraw"` (default), and the auto-export keys off (defaults) (`src/utils/fileUtils.ts:508-547`; `src/core/managers/CommandManager.ts:190-201`, `:640-661`; `src/core/managers/FileManager.ts:140-148`; `src/core/settingsDefaults.ts:498-505`). Convert-all (`src/core/main.ts:813-825`) is a vault-wide writer with no key. It reaches a roster path only through a `.excalidraw` file already there.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B4 fail unchanged, with the basis corrected by the verifier. The legacy-format converters (file menu and convert-all) write a type-less `.md` beside any `.excalidraw` file, and no key types that output (`src/core/main.ts:762-787`, `:813-825`; `src/constants/constants.ts:469-479`). In-vault `.excalidraw` inputs come only from mobile export or by hand; desktop export downloads outside the vault (`src/view/managers/ViewExportManager.ts:108-142`). A second leg: an annotation's frontmatter block opens with a blank line, which the flat parser rejects (`src/shared/ExcalidrawAutomate.ts:1511`, `:1533`; `src/utils/obsidianUtils.ts:405-448`; `frontmatter.py:173`). Only `annotateFolder` under inbox/ cures that leg. A partial key set that is not sufficient: `templateFilePath` a typed template, `cropFolder` and `annotateFolder` under inbox/, `allowJavaScriptFiles` and `storeScriptFilesAsJavaScript` `true`.
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass† → pass† — basis moved. Counted: save-time auto-export of SVG, PNG or `.excalidraw` copies beside the drawing; vault rename and delete hooks, reacting to any writer, that move or trash those exports when `keepInSync` is on; and the first-load library file under `Excalidraw/Libraries`, which is off the roster. The rename hook also moves the drawing's backup, which lives in the IndexedDB image cache, not the vault, and the modify hook only reloads a drawing open in a view. Settings: `data.json` `"keepInSync": false`, `"autoexportSVG": false`, `"autoexportPNG": false`, `"autoexportExcalidraw": false`, `"syncExcalidraw": false` and `"libraryFolderPath": "Excalidraw/Libraries"` (all defaults), with `startupScriptPath` empty (default). Create-and-embed, crop, annotate, the file-menu create and convert-all are now the person's commands, so `cropFolder`, `annotateFolder` and `embedUseExcalidrawFolder` no longer decide B3. Hinge: convert-all is a vault-wide palette command with no confirm, read as deliberate; if it were counted, no key confines it and the cell would fail (`src/core/managers/FileManager.ts:599-665`, `:757-774`, `:781-830`; `src/core/settingsDefaults.ts:499-501`, `:590-599`, `:719`; `src/core/main.ts:813-828`; `src/core/managers/CommandManager.ts:2552-2565` @`128a9ef`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass† → fail†∆ — create-and-embed, and the insert-drawing and insert-image dialogs (palette command, hotkey, editor menu), insert an embed link with `editor.replaceSelection` at the cursor of whatever Markdown note is open. In a wiki/ page the link resolves, so captured-set does not flag it; in a fulltext/ note, or appended at the end of a log/ day file, nothing reads it. Setting: none for the embed; `embedUseExcalidrawFolder`, `cropFolder`, `annotateFolder` and the auto-export keys move only drawing files. Not failing: the file-menu Create new drawing into a right-clicked roster folder writes a drawing whose frontmatter opens with a blank line, which the flat parser rejects, so okf-frontmatter reports it (through the commit gate for literature/, log/ and wiki/; for fulltext/ only through a worktree verify); crop and annotate outputs, auto-export and `keepInSync` are confined by keys. Verifier: confirmed; Excalidraw has no `processFrontMatter` call, and the not-failing leg holds at the default empty `templateFilePath`; whether a typed template seeded for B4 would make a wiki/ drawing typed and unreported was not checked (`src/core/managers/CommandManager.ts:190-201`, `:640-673`; `src/core/managers/FileManager.ts:210-227`; `src/constants/constants.ts:469-479` @`128a9ef`; `research_vault/captured.py:94-109`, `:133-191`; `frontmatter.py:150-182`).

**Templater (`templater-obsidian`), `B-community-1`; also seed.**

- Pin: release 2.25.1, annotated tag `4bc86c2` to commit `0ffe956a58360aee949df114ded2fd7431447ebb` (2026-09-19); HEAD `c51cf11` has 2.25.1. Assets: `main.js` (453,498 B), `manifest.json`, `styles.css`, `templater-obsidian.zip`. Installed in the sibling vaults Memoria-test and memoria-private at 2.20.5.
- Licence: AGPL-3.0 (`LICENSE.TXT @0ffe956`, 661 lines; `package.json:25` AGPL-3.0-only). `main.js` has no `@license` or copyright lines. The verifier found that it bundles MIT code (acorn, `@codemirror/*`, `@microsoft/tsdoc`) with none of its notices, and the WASM engine `@silentvoid13/rusty_engine` 0.4.0, which ships on npm with no licence field or file; its upstream repository is AGPL-3.0.
- Writes: rendered template output into the active file or new files when invoked (`tp.file.*`). Templates on file creation, startup templates and system commands run only when the matching per-device localStorage toggle is on (all default false). Its own `data.json`.
- B2: floor. The guide lists it as needed for the core workflow (comparison `:64`), and its extract-to-new-file hotkey is the floor's atomizing move (`:93`); the floor binds vault-setup and daily-log (assembly spec `:146`).
- B3 pass: safe by default, but through keys the seed cannot set. `trigger_on_file_creation`, `enable_startup_templates` and `enable_system_commands` live in localStorage, not `data.json` (`src/settings/LocalSettings.ts:3-15`; `src/settings/Settings.ts:45-47,54,67`). If a user turns `trigger_on_file_creation` on, the fallback branch runs template commands in every new `.md` of 100 KB or less, CLI-written literature notes included, whatever `trigger_on_file_creation_mode` says (`src/core/Templater.ts:544,561-567,626-630,640`; the verifier placed the `vault.modify` at `:406-426`). The only seedable defence is `data.json` `ignore_folders_on_creation`, a prefix match, named as `[{"folder":"literature"},{"folder":"fulltext"},{"folder":"wiki"},{"folder":"log"},{"folder":"system"},{"folder":"inbox/review-queue.md"}]`. The verifier confirmed that a V1-shaped seeded `data.json` with `trigger_on_file_creation: true` only produces a reset notice, because the migration deletes the key and never writes localStorage (`src/settings/migrate-settings.ts:31-34,73-80`); that a seeded `data.json` without `data_version: 2` is treated as V1 and rewritten on first load (`SettingsV1.ts:31`; `src/main.ts:71-87`); and that the prefix `log` also covers root `log.md`.
- B4 pass: output is rendered Markdown (`Templater.ts:605,625`); unexecuted `<% %>` tags stay literal only in template files. Template files and the notes made from them are scanned `.md`, so each template needs a `type` in its frontmatter (`okf-conformance.md:9-10`; `verify.py:752-757`). The verifier added that a template whose frontmatter contains `<% %>` tags may not parse as YAML, a rule-1 risk owned by the template author.
- B5 pass: same AGPL-3.0 conditions as Excalidraw (`LICENSE.TXT:1-2,:183,:230,:233,:263`). The desk's no-bundled-notices statement was corrected by the verifier (above).
- ‡ 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass → fail. 2.25.1 always registers the Obsidian-CLI handler `templater:create-from-template`, which creates a note at whatever output path the caller passes. That is a URI-class external write no Templater key disables, and the CLI switch is held in Obsidian's global config, not the vault (`src/handlers/CommandHandler.ts:87`, `:169-191`, `:214-256`; `src/core/Templater.ts:544-566`; obsidian 1.13.7 `main.js` near byte 187500). Hinge: if CLI handlers are not judged, the pass rests on `data.json` `"data_version": 2`; `"ignore_folders_on_creation": [{"folder":"literature"},{"folder":"fulltext"},{"folder":"wiki"},{"folder":"log"},{"folder":"system/propagations"},{"folder":"inbox/review-queue.md"}]` (prefix match; `log` also covers root `log.md`); `"startup_templates": []`; and `app.json` `newFileLocation` not `"current"` (`src/settings/Settings.ts:49-68`; `src/core/Templater.ts:129-151`). Resolved 2026-10-04 by the second ruling.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B4 pass unchanged. Seed key: `data.json` `"templates_folder": "system/templates"`. The seeded templates carry `type` (`daily.md:1-3`), and this also covers notes made through the CLI handler.
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 fail → fail — the hinge above is settled: CLI handlers are remote. `templater:create-from-template` is registered during command setup with no setting checked, and it creates a note at whatever output path the caller passes, roster paths included; no Templater key unregisters it, and the CLI switch is per machine. On-create and startup templates are counted too, but sit behind per-device localStorage toggles that default off; `data.json` `"startup_templates": []` and an `ignore_folders_on_creation` roster list matter only if those toggles are switched on (`src/handlers/CommandHandler.ts:87`, `:169-191`, `:214-256` @`0ffe956`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 fail‡ → fail‡ — basis widened to its commands. Insert template and Replace templates in the active file (commands with hotkeys) rewrite the open note; Create new note from template and the `templater:create-from-template` CLI handler create a note at a chosen or caller-supplied path, roster included; user template code (`tp.file.*`) can create, move or rewrite any file; on-create and startup templates sit behind per-device localStorage toggles. Undetected: writes into wiki/, typed creates and edits of existing fulltext/ files, and a date-named new log/ note once stamp-type has stamped it. Setting: none; no key unregisters the commands or the CLI handler, and `data.json` `ignore_folders_on_creation` and `"startup_templates": []` cover only the localStorage-gated legs. Verifier wording fix, result unchanged: a worktree verify reports a new untyped `.md` in fulltext/, so only typed creates and edits there go unreported (`src/handlers/CommandHandler.ts:20-52`, `:69-88`, `:169-191`, `:214-260` @`0ffe956`; `research_vault/stamp.py:93-157`; `templates/git/pre-commit:24-29`; `verify.py:752-757`).

**Dataview (`dataview`), `B-community-1`; also seed.**

- Pin: release 0.5.68, tag commit `29b1ad87e1f2b964b4fa0c209f11d0622469bdb0` (2025-03-15), the catalog version: HEAD `5ad0994` (2025-04-08) has `manifest.json:4` 0.5.68. Release 0.5.70 (2025-04-07) is named only by `manifest-beta.json` at HEAD, and its own asset `manifest.json` reports 0.5.68. Sibling vaults run 0.5.68. `main.js` 2,377,639 B.
- Licence: MIT (`LICENSE.txt @29b1ad8`, 20 lines). Notices in `main.js`: Papa Parse (`:8799-8804`), base64-arraybuffer (`:11545-11551`), a notice by artisticat1 (`:19798-19804`; the verifier identified it as obsidian-latex-suite, not obsidian-latex), localforage (the verifier found it is Apache-2.0, `:9871-9874`, which carries Apache §4 notice obligations), and a public-domain cyrb53 notice (`:13506`). luxon, preact and parsimmon are bundled too.
- Writes: nothing automatic. A click on a task checkbox in a TASK (or dataviewjs) view rewrites that line in its source file (`src/ui/views/task-view.tsx:59-91`, `:374-400`; verifier `:58-95`, `:374-404`, `rewriteTask` then `vault.adapter.write`). A completion field is added only with `taskCompletionTracking` (default false, `src/settings.ts:40`). dataviewjs can write anything, but only if enabled (default false, `:104-105`). Index cache in IndexedDB (`src/data-import/persister.ts:20-22`).
- B2: floor. The guide lists it as needed; the floor's six saved retrieval views and its task aggregator are Dataview; the repository's reading points the query surface at it (comparison `:64`, `:100`, `:356-363`). The vault's claim-link grammar is already Dataview's inline-field grammar (`okf-conformance.md:41`).
- B3 fail: no automatic writer, but no setting turns off the checkbox rewrite. A machine surface gets a checkbox only through capture's carried Zotero child-note text (`research_vault/literature_notes.py:359-363`; vault `AGENTS.md:26`), and only a query's FROM clause can confine it, which is note text, not config. Keys that keep the other write paths off: `enableDataviewJs: false`, `enableInlineDataviewJs: false`, `taskCompletionTracking: false` (defaults). The verifier added that the review queue's `- [id:: ...]` lines do not match Dataview's single-character checkbox pattern, and recorded that this fail is inconsistent with other rows (§7.1).
- B4 pass: queries sit in fenced code blocks and results are rendered, never persisted; inline fields are plain text (`src/settings.ts:96-108`).
- ‡ 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 fail → pass. The checkbox rewrite comes from a click on one task and rewrites that task's line in the one file that holds it, which is a chosen-file edit under the ruling. The only judged writer left is DataviewJS on render. Seed keys: `data.json` `"enableDataviewJs": false`, `"enableInlineDataviewJs": false` and `"taskCompletionTracking": false` (all defaults). The bundle has no CLI or protocol handler (`src/ui/views/task-view.tsx:374-404`; `src/settings.ts:39`, `:104-105`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass → pass — basis moved: the only counted behaviour is DataviewJS and inline DataviewJS running note code on render, which `data.json` `"enableDataviewJs": false` and `"enableInlineDataviewJs": false` (defaults) turn off. The checkbox rewrite follows the person's click, so `taskCompletionTracking` no longer decides B3. The bundle has no CLI, protocol or server handler, and its only interval reloads the starred cache (release `main.js:7357`, `:7384-7385`, `:13238`; `src/ui/views/task-view.tsx:373-404`; `src/settings.ts:104-105` @`29b1ad8`).
- ◊ 2026-10-04 live: B1 pass — release 0.5.68 assets copied into `.obsidian/plugins/dataview/` (`main.js` 2,377,639 B, sha256 `794e9eae…`); the manifest read back as 0.5.68, minAppVersion 0.13.11, isDesktopOnly false. `enablePluginAndSave` resolved true in 239 ms; loaded with 3 commands. Its console showed its version banner and that it had indexed the vault's 7 files; `dev:errors` captured nothing. It wrote no `data.json`; the only new vault file was `community-plugins.json`. Disabling removed it from the loaded set. Which version a store install delivers (§6.1) was not measured, because the leg copied release assets (`/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/plugin-dataview.log`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass‡ → fail‡∆ — a checkbox click in a TASK view or a dataviewjs task list rewrites that task line through `vault.adapter.write` in whichever file holds it, and its children too when `recursiveSubTaskCompletion` is on; the handler is bound to every rendered task with no settings check. Evidence-layer reports such a rewrite in a literature/ note and append-only in a log/ file; nothing reports it in a wiki/ page or a fulltext/ file. Setting: none for the checkbox rewrite; `"enableDataviewJs": false` and `"enableInlineDataviewJs": false` (defaults) remove only the note-code legs. Hinge: the path reaches wiki/ or fulltext/ only through a task line there; research-vault neither writes nor forbids one, and whether the compile tool ever writes one is not established (a grep found no task-line emitter in `research_vault/`, `skills/` or the compile tool's installed copy). Verifier: confirmed (`src/ui/views/task-view.tsx:58-95`, `:105`, `:373-405`; `src/settings.ts:40-44`, `:104-105` @`29b1ad8`; `research_vault/lints.py:109-157`, `:727-875`; `captured.py:133-191`).

**Tasks (`obsidian-tasks-plugin`), `B-community-1`.**

- Pin: release 8.4.0, tag commit `9173205606e49846f456caf438fdc0e5baded77e` (2026-08-25); HEAD `971a9ad` has 8.4.0. Assets: `main.js` (935,515 B), `manifest.json`, `styles.css`, `obsidian-tasks-8.4.0.zip`.
- Licence: MIT (`LICENSE @9173205`, 21 lines). The header of `main.js` carries 11 licence blocks: obsidian-tasks, rrule (with a python-dateutil notice), chrono-node, flatpickr, `@floating-ui/dom`, boon-js, eventemitter2, i18next, mustache, mustache-validator, safe-regex2. Desk range `:7-173`; the verifier placed the end at `:171`.
- Writes: on a checkbox toggle (Live Preview, Reading view or query results), rewrites the task line: status, a done date (`setDoneDate` true, `src/Config/Settings.ts:119`), a cancelled date (`:120`), and a new recurrence line. The create/edit modal and the auto-suggester write into the active file. Its own `data.json`.
- B2 fail: the floor's task need is met by a Dataview TASK query (comparison `:195`, `:224-225`, `:362-363`), and no step is task tracking.
- B3 named key: `data.json` `globalFilter: "#task"` (a string the CLI never writes), so `Task.fromLine` returns null for any line without it (`src/Task/Task.ts:219-223`; `src/Obsidian/LivePreviewExtension.ts:89-129`; `Settings.ts:114-115`); optionally a `globalQuery` with path exclusions. The verifier kept the pass but refuted the claim that Tasks never rewrites machine-written checklist lines: the toggle-done command still rewrites a line without the filter (`src/Commands/ToggleDone.ts:32-36`), a user command on the cursor line; and the global filter is a substring match (`src/Config/GlobalFilter.ts:52-55`), so `#task` also matches `#tasks` in carried child-note text.
- B4 pass: default `taskFormat` writes emoji markers as plain text (`Settings.ts:117`); `taskFormat: 'dataview'` would write `[key:: value]` fields instead.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass unchanged and needs no key. Every recorded write lands in the file holding the task the person acted on, and the pin has no background, bulk, remote or CLI writer, so `globalFilter` no longer decides it (`src/Commands/ToggleDone.ts:32-36`; `src/Config/Settings.ts:119-120`). The status-report file below is written at the vault root, which is off the roster.
- ‡ 2026-10-03 normalisation: B4 pass → fail. The re-judgement kept pass. The verifier refuted it, and its result is recorded: a status-review button in the settings tab creates a timestamped report `.md` at a hard-coded vault-root path, with a heading and no frontmatter. verify reports such a file as missing `type`, and no Tasks or `app.json` key governs that path (`src/Config/SettingsTab.ts:776-788`; `src/Statuses/StatusRegistryReport.ts:21`; `verify.py:752-757`; `structure.py:91-100`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass → fail∆ — `toggle-done` and `edit-task` (commands with hotkeys) and the set-status commands rewrite the cursor line of the open note and are registered with no settings check; on a line that is not a task `toggle-done` still rewrites it (a list item gains a checkbox, plain text becomes a `- [ ]` item), and the global filter does not apply. A checkbox click in Live Preview, Reading view or query results rewrites the task line in its file. `add-query-file-defaults-properties` writes `TQ_` properties with null values into the open note's frontmatter through `processFrontMatter`; in literature/ these are non-capture keys, outside both the body witness and the capture-field comparison. Undetected in wiki/ and fulltext/. Setting: none; `globalFilter` limits clicks but not `toggle-done`, and no key unregisters the commands. Whether Obsidian's re-serialisation changes a capture value is not established and decides nothing. Verifier: confirmed (`src/Commands/index.ts:23-78`; `src/Commands/ToggleDone.ts:11-49`; `src/Commands/AddQueryFileDefaultsProperties.ts` @`9173205`; `research_vault/literature_notes.py:64-68`, `:108-132`; `lints.py:618`, `:698-725`).

**Git (`obsidian-git`), `B-community-1`; also seed.**

- Pin: release 2.41.1 = HEAD `ee48439d8588ceb6128b7ef815182a76db9a17d6` (2026-10-02); annotated tag `0d44675`. Assets: `main.js` (774,737 B), `manifest.json`, `styles.css`, `obsidian-git-2.41.1.zip`. Sibling vaults run 2.38.3.
- Licence: MIT (`LICENSE @ee48439`, 21 lines). Notices at `main.js:381-405`: ieee754 (BSD-3-Clause), buffer (MIT), safe-buffer (MIT), crc-32 (SheetJS), and a ZenFS `path.join` snippet inside isomorphic-git marked LGPL-3.0-or-later (`:402`). The verifier added that, as code inside a combined work, the snippet carries LGPL §4 conditions (notice plus a way to modify and recombine).
- Writes: `.git` on a user commit; the worktree, machine surfaces included, on pull, merge, checkout or discard; a `.gitignore` line on an explicit menu action (`src/main.ts:422-447`; `src/gitActions.ts:64-80`). No conflict file: conflicts are reported by a notice (`main.ts:689-698`). Its own `data.json`, plus per-device localStorage.
- B2 fail: no sync or backup step; vault-setup's doctor `remote` probe only reads `git remote` (`research_vault/doctor.py:51-62`), and setup-vault provisions no remote. Membership of the author's seed is not a step.
- B3 named keys: `data.json` `autoSaveInterval: 0`, `autoPushInterval: 0`, `autoPullInterval: 0`, `autoPullOnBoot: false`, `autoBackupAfterFileChange: false` (defaults; `src/constants.ts:14-17,32`). On desktop, commits go through system git with hooks enabled, so the vault's pre-commit hook runs stamp-type and verify (`src/gitManager/simpleGit.ts:112-118`; `research_vault/templates/git/pre-commit:24-29`). The author's vault sets `autoSaveInterval` 30 and `autoPullOnBoot` true (§2.2). Verifier additions: on mobile, commits go through isomorphic-git 1.42.4 (`package.json:75`; `src/gitManager/isomorphicGit.ts:279-285`), which has no pre-commit hook mechanism (its only hook callbacks are `onPrePush` and `onPostCheckout`), so mobile commits bypass stamp-type and `verify --surface commit`; `isDesktopOnly` is false, so a seeded plugin loads on mobile. `syncMethod: 'reset'` (`simpleGit.ts:864-870`) keeps a pull out of the worktree but is not safe to seed, because HEAD moves while the files do not and the next commit reverts the remote changes. A user pull with merge can leave conflict markers in machine-surface files and no safe key disables it, the same class of write that failed Dataview.
- B4 pass: it writes no note content of its own; unresolved merge markers inside frontmatter would break rule-1 parsing, which is git's behaviour.
- ‡ 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass → fail. The re-judgement failed it on pull, a judged remote write into the worktree with no pull switch and no path scope. It also held that `syncMethod` reset discards the write rather than confining it. The verifier kept the fail and moved the basis to legs the ruling names directly. Discard all changes runs a whole-vault discard, and Switch branch and Switch to remote branch rewrite the worktree. Those are vault-wide rewrites that reach the machine surfaces, and no `data.json` key disables or scopes them (`src/commands.ts:136-143`, `:376-403`; `src/gitManager/simpleGit.ts:784-786`, `:822-870`; `src/constants.ts:9-72`). The keys `autoPullInterval` 0, `autoPullOnBoot` false, `autoSaveInterval` 0, `autoPushInterval` 0 and `autoBackupAfterFileChange` false (defaults) stop only the scheduled writes.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B4 pass unchanged, with the basis and key corrected by the verifier. Because B3 counts the pull's worktree output as the plugin's write, the conflict markers a merge leaves cannot be set aside as git's output. At the defaults (`syncMethod` merge, `mergeStrategy` none), markers inside frontmatter break both the flat parser and YAML. Seed key: `data.json` `"mergeStrategy": "ours"` (or `"theirs"`), which is passed to the merge so that content conflicts resolve without markers (`src/constants.ts:28`, `:30`; `src/gitManager/simpleGit.ts:828-862`; `frontmatter.py:173`). No B3 or B4 cell addresses mobile commits through isomorphic-git bypassing the pre-commit gate (above).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 fail → fail — the basis moves to the pull itself, which the ruling names as remote. It runs from the Pull command, from commit-and-sync while `pullBeforePush` is true, on a schedule (`autoPullInterval`) and on boot (`autoPullOnBoot`), and merges remote content into the worktree, roster paths included; scheduled commit and push and auto-backup after a file change are counted too. Discard all (confirmed in its modal) and the branch switches (chosen in a picker) are now deliberate. `data.json` `"autoPullInterval": 0`, `"autoPullOnBoot": false`, `"autoSaveInterval": 0`, `"autoPushInterval": 0` and `"autoBackupAfterFileChange": false` (defaults) stop only the scheduled legs. `"syncMethod": "reset"` keeps a desktop pull out of the worktree, but the mobile isomorphic-git pull never reads `syncMethod` and always merges and checks out, `isDesktopOnly` is false, and disable-on-device is a localStorage flag, so no setting covers every load path. 2.41.1 registers no CLI or protocol handler. Hinge: if a Pull the person runs in the app were read as deliberate, the default scheduled keys alone would confine it (`src/commands.ts:119-126`; `src/gitManager/isomorphicGit.ts:492-579`; `src/gitManager/simpleGit.ts:822-869`; `src/main.ts:188`, `:551`; `src/constants.ts:14-20`, `:28`, `:32`; `src/gitActions.ts:1004-1049`, `:1282-1311`; `manifest.json:7` @`ee48439`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 fail‡ → fail‡ — basis widened: Discard all changes (a confirmed modal), Switch branch and Switch to remote branch are judged with Pull. Pull writes remote content into the worktree, roster paths included, from the Pull command, commit-and-sync while `pullBeforePush` is true, `autoPullInterval` and `autoPullOnBoot`; on mobile isomorphic-git always merges and checks out. Discard reverts or removes uncommitted CLI writes on the roster; branch switches rewrite the worktree; scheduled commit, push and auto-backup run too. None is detected: the scaffold installs only `.git/hooks/pre-commit`; git 2.43.0 runs `pre-merge-commit` (not installed) for a merge commit and no hook for a fast-forward; verify's base defaults to HEAD; a discard or switch leaves nothing to diff; CI replay is opt-in (`with_ci` off by default); mobile commits through isomorphic-git run no pre-commit hook. Setting: none; the scheduled keys (`autoPullInterval` 0, `autoPullOnBoot` false, `autoSaveInterval` 0, `autoPushInterval` 0, `autoBackupAfterFileChange` false; defaults) remove only the scheduled legs, and the mobile pull ignores `syncMethod` reset. Verifier: confirmed (`src/commands.ts:119-143`, `:377-403`; `src/gitManager/simpleGit.ts:784-786`, `:822-871`; `src/gitManager/isomorphicGit.ts:492-579`; `src/constants.ts:14-20` @`ee48439`; release `main.js` 2.41.1; `research_vault/scaffold.py:105`, `:225`; `gitstate.py:318-321`; `git help githooks`, git 2.43.0, read 2026-10-04).

**Calendar (`calendar`), `B-community-1`.**

- Pin: release 1.5.10, tag commit `7d2aebda7f4a280bedc6da6d25f4da611d1625ef` (2021-04-01), the catalog version: HEAD `ef3f269` (last commit 2022-11-04) has `manifest.json:5` 1.5.10. GitHub's `releases/latest` returns 2.0.0-beta.2 (2021-04-20, not flagged prerelease), which is not the catalog version. Assets: `main.js` (141,498 B) and `manifest.json`; no `styles.css`. Unmaintained since 2021 (release) and 2022 (commit).
- Licence: MIT (`LICENSE @7d2aebd`, 21 lines; `package.json:7`). No notices in `main.js`, though it bundles svelte, obsidian-calendar-ui and obsidian-daily-notes-interface.
- Writes: a click on a date with no note creates a daily note after a confirmation (`shouldConfirmBeforeCreate` true, `src/settings.ts:34`; `src/io/dailyNotes.ts:14-44`), in the core Daily Notes folder with the format and template from `.obsidian/daily-notes.json` (`main.js:39-44`, `:225-250`). Weekly notes off (`showWeeklyNote` false). Its own `data.json`. The verifier found that when the Periodic Notes plugin is loaded, Calendar reads that plugin's daily settings instead of core's (`main.js:31-38`).
- B2: daily-log (assembly spec `:146`, `:168`; `research_vault/publish.py:416-428`), but only while the core daily-notes folder is `log`.
- B3: desk **fail**: no Calendar key prevents creation (`shouldConfirmBeforeCreate` only asks first); with `daily-notes.json` `folder: "log"`, a confirmed click writes `log/<date>.md` into a machine surface (vault `AGENTS.md:7`), and the CLI's `_append_log` then appends to that hand-made file (`publish.py:424-427`). **Verifier: pass.** The must accepts can-be-configured-not-to, and setting `daily-notes.json` `folder` to anything other than `log` (Obsidian's default `""` is the vault root) keeps every Calendar write out of machine surfaces; the desk's own note named that setting. The finding is a conflict between musts: the configuration that passes B3 removes B2's daily-log basis.
- B4 pass: holds only if the seed sets `daily-notes.json` `template` to a typed template such as `system/templates/daily` (`daily.md:1-3`; `stamp.py:9-12`). With Obsidian's default (no template) the note is empty; outside log/, where B3's passing setting puts it, it breaks §11 rule 1 and stamp-type cannot fix it. The verifier noted that judged at defaults, as Excalidraw was, this would fail (§7.2).
- B5 pass: MIT; bundled svelte and other MIT code carry no notices, so it needs the same NOTICE file as Excalidraw.
- Exit (desk): Stop loading it; the day notes it created are ordinary Markdown in the daily-notes folder, and core Daily Notes still opens them.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass† unchanged. A confirmed date click creates a day note at a path the plugin takes from the core Daily notes settings (or from Periodic Notes'), so the write is judged. Seed keys: `daily-notes.json` `"folder"` set to a folder off the roster (not `"log"`), and `"format": "YYYY-MM-DD"` (the default). The filename is the date rendered in that format, and a format can carry path segments (`src/io/dailyNotes.ts:14-44`; `main.js:31-44`, `:221-230`). The folder value that passes B3 removes B2's daily-log basis (§7.5). B4 pass unchanged; seed key `daily-notes.json` `"template": "system/templates/daily"` with `folder` not `log` (`daily.md:1-3`; `structure.py:54-67`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass† → pass† — basis moved: nothing is counted. It creates a day note only when the person clicks a date (behind a confirm dialog by default) or a week number, or runs the open-weekly-note command. Its vault create, delete and modify hooks and its file-open hook only reindex and redraw, and it has no interval, CLI or protocol handler. `daily-notes.json` `folder` and `format` no longer decide B3, so the §7.5 conflict no longer reaches B3; B2 was not re-judged (release `main.js:1046-1069`, `:4236-4240`, `:4288-4322`, `:4410-4420` @`7d2aebd`).
- ◊ 2026-10-04 live: B1 pass — release 1.5.10 (`main.js` 141,498 B, sha256 `7fb339e9…`, and `manifest.json`; no `styles.css` is released). `enablePluginAndSave` resolved true; loaded with 3 commands. Its only console line was a debug note about the Moment locale; no error. On load it wrote `.obsidian/plugins/calendar/data.json` (231 B), and `workspace.json` grew from 4,843 to 5,167 B, which suggests its sidebar view opened (inferred, not logged). Nothing outside `.obsidian/` changed. The confirmed-click write (§6.1) was not run (`/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/plugin-calendar.log`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass† → pass† — the folder keys decide B3 again. Its only writes are new notes: a date click (behind a confirm dialog by default) creates a day note at the core Daily notes folder and format, or at Periodic Notes' when that plugin is loaded; a week-number click or Open Weekly Note creates a week note at `weeklyNoteFolder`. A date-named day note in log/ would be stamped `daily` and pass unreported, so the folder key is what removes that leg. Setting: `daily-notes.json` `"folder"` off the roster (not `log`) with `"format": "YYYY-MM-DD"`; Calendar `data.json` `"weeklyNoteFolder": ""` (default) with the default `weeklyNoteFormat`; if Periodic Notes is also seeded, its `daily.folder` and `weekly.folder` off the roster. The folder that passes still removes B2's daily-log basis (§7.5). Verifier: confirmed; a release-bundle grep finds only two `vault.create` calls, one `createFolder` and `saveData`, and a date-named file passes `regenerate_log`'s shape check too (release `main.js:27-46`, `:54-78`, `:174-182`, `:221-235`, `:310-320`, `:740-746`, `:4402-4428` @`7d2aebd`; `research_vault/stamp.py:116-155`; `structure.py:54-67`, `:117-133`; `okf.py:19-37`).
