**Kanban (`obsidian-kanban`), `B-community-2`.**

- Repository: the registry lists `obsidian-community/obsidian-kanban` (`community-plugins.json:980 @e76f2c3`), which GitHub redirects to `community-archive/obsidian-kanban` (Obsidian Community Archive); the manifest's `authorUrl` still points at `mgmeyers/obsidian-kanban`. Pin: release 2.0.51 (2024-05-31) = commit `8501981a1afacb4c8fc03ec60604aa5eedfbd857`; HEAD `main` `5134c05` (2026-03-06); no release since. The tag predates the move.
- Licence: GPL-3.0 (`LICENSE.md`, 673 lines). `main.js` carries a bundled-licence block naming `@tanstack/match-sorter-utils`, `@tanstack/react-table`, `@tanstack/table-core`, choices.js, is-plain-object, mark.js and moment. The desk recorded all as MIT; the verifier found that the choices.js 9.0.1 notice embeds Fuse.js 3.4.5 under Apache-2.0 (compatible with GPL-3.0, redistributable with its notice).
- Writes: `data.json` (global settings, `src/Settings.ts:53-126`). New board (`src/main.ts:339-350`) writes frontmatter `kanban-plugin: board` (`src/parsers/common.ts:25`) at `getNewFileParent` (`:342`). Every board edit rewrites the board, re-emitting all foreign frontmatter keys (`src/parsers/parseMarkdown.ts:173-182`; desk `src/parsers/formats/list.ts:448`, verifier `:443-450`) and appending a `%% kanban:settings %%` JSON trailer (`common.ts:29-38`). New-note-from-card creates a note in `new-note-folder` or beside the board and applies `new-note-template` (`src/components/Item/ItemMenu.ts:68-79`). Pasted or dropped files go to Obsidian's attachment path (`src/components/Item/helpers.ts:415-421`). `link-date-to-daily-note` is unset by default (`Settings.ts:68`); when on, a date click opens the core daily note, so any write is the core plugin's.
- B2: desk **fail**: the guide's workflow does not use Kanban; only the starter vault ships it (comparison `:63-67`). **Verifier: pass.** The starter vault ships Kanban enabled (`a3d22d0a .obsidian/community-plugins.json:2`) and contains a real board, `03 writing/shortcuts.md` (frontmatter `kanban-plugin` basic at `:4`), used as a navigation hub linking notes, tasks and saved Dataview views; its readme names Kanban among the five plugins the vault's features depend on (`readme.md:42-47`); and the comparison counts the starter vault as part of History Notes (`:55-60`). The floor maps to vault-setup and daily-log (assembly spec `:146`).
- B3 pass†: no write without a user action. Desk named keys: `data.json` `"new-note-folder"` set to a non-machine folder (for example `inbox`), and `app.json` `newFileLocation`, `newFileFolderPath` and `attachmentFolderPath` outside the machine surfaces (`Settings.ts:77-78`). The verifier refuted two parts of the basis: its vault hooks are not only a rename listener but also modify, metadata-changed and dataview listeners (`main.ts:519-566`), all of which only refresh the view (`StateManager.ts:89-93`, `:99-110`, `:352-358`); and the folder context menu's New kanban board (`main.ts:362-377`) passes the right-clicked folder straight to `newKanban`, bypassing both `app.json` and `new-note-folder`, so a right-click on literature/ writes a board there and no key prevents it.
- B4 fail: a new board's only frontmatter key is `kanban-plugin`, with no `type` (OKF §11 rule 2; `okf-conformance.md:10`). stamp-type cannot derive a type outside literature/, log/ and inbox/ (`stamp.py:13-17`; `structure.py:54-67`, verifier's line numbers), so a board in projects/ is flagged; it is exempt in inbox/ (`structure.py:50-51`, `:78-79`). A hand-added `type` survives rewrites. The body stays readable (headed task lists), but the settings trailer renders as literal `%%` text outside Obsidian. Boards made from the folder menu can land outside inbox/ whatever the configuration.
- B5 pass†: GPL-3.0 allows redistribution with the licence text and a source offer (the source is the tag); result stands, notice record corrected (above).
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass† unchanged. The folder-menu board lands in the folder the person right-clicked, a person-chosen placement that is not judged. Board rewrites edit the board the person opened, and the vault hooks only refresh views. Seed keys for the judged creates (New board command, card notes, pasted attachments): `data.json` `"new-note-folder": "inbox"`; `app.json` `"newFileLocation": "folder"`, `"newFileFolderPath": "inbox"`; `attachmentFolderPath` off the roster (default `"/"`). When `new-note-folder` is empty, card notes fall back to the new-file parent of the board path, not to the board's own folder (`src/main.ts:338-377`; `src/Settings.ts:77-78`; `src/components/Item/ItemMenu.ts:68-79`). B4 fail unchanged: the folder-menu path writes a board whose only frontmatter key is `kanban-plugin` into any right-clicked folder, and no setting adds `type` or governs that path (`src/parsers/common.ts:24-26`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass† → pass† — basis moved: nothing is counted. Opening a board and the rename, modify, metadata and Dataview hooks re-parse it with saving off; the rename hook only re-keys the view when the board itself is renamed. Every board write, New board from the command or the folder menu, a new note from a card, archive and paste follows the person's action, and it has no interval, CLI or protocol handler. `new-note-folder` and the `app.json` `newFileLocation`, `newFileFolderPath` and `attachmentFolderPath` keys no longer decide B3 (`src/StateManager.ts:89-96`, `:138-166`, `:352-358`; `src/main.ts:519-566`; `src/KanbanView.tsx:198-202` @`8501981`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass† → pass† — the re-judgement passed it because every board it writes opens its frontmatter with a blank line the flat parser rejects, so okf-frontmatter reports a board on any roster path. The verifier refuted that deciding line, and its basis is the one recorded: the View as board, View as table and View as list commands and pane-menu items call `setView`, which rewrites the board through Obsidian's `processFrontMatter`, and that drops the blank line. After a switch, a board in log/ is stamped `daily` and okf-frontmatter matches, so the commit gate passes it; in wiki/, fulltext/ and `system/propagations/` it is still reported (no `type` after a switch, unparseable before); in literature/ evidence-layer reports it; in log/, the first capture or publish regenerates root `log.md` with an `Untitled Kanban` heading and the board's lane headings, and okf-structure reports that. Hinge: the log/ leg rests on a delayed, indirect check that names `log.md`, not the board, and the fulltext/ leg on counting a default worktree verify as detection (§2A Third ruling); if a finding that fires only at the next capture or publish does not count, the cell fails. Other creates go to folders set by keys: `data.json` `"new-note-folder": "inbox"` and `"link-date-to-daily-note": false`; `app.json` `"attachmentFolderPath"` off the roster (default `"/"`). Residuals the verifier recorded, deciding nothing: a board's own `new-note-folder` can override the seeded one; and converting an empty date-named log/ file to Kanban, then switching view, yields a lane-less board nothing reports, a file only core Daily notes or Calendar would create, with their folder set to `log` (`src/KanbanView.tsx:118-123`, `:253-268`; `src/main.ts:339-377`, `:470-500`, `:576-720`, `:626-646`; `src/parsers/common.ts:25-27`; `src/parsers/formats/list.ts:443-451`; `src/components/Item/ItemMenu.ts:68-73` @`8501981`; Obsidian 1.13.7 `app.js:1@byte 1790234`, `@byte 1791784`; `research_vault/stamp.py:116-155`; `structure.py:74-111`, `:117-133`; `okf.py:19-37`; `capture.py:567-569`; `publish.py:416-429`).
- ◇ 2026-10-04 fourth ruling: B3 pass† → fail†◇ — the third-ruling pass rested on detection of a board created on the roster (okf-frontmatter, evidence-layer, root `log.md`); the fourth ruling passes a counted path only by a setting. Counted: in the folder context menu, New kanban board passes the right-clicked folder straight to `newKanban`, bypassing `app.json` and `new-note-folder`, creates the board through `fileManager.createNewMarkdownFile` and writes its frontmatter with `vault.modify`, so it creates a note other than the open one, with the plugin's content, in a roster folder. Not judged: board rewrites and the View as board, table or list rewrite through `setView` (the open board), and a board's own `new-note-folder` override (the person setting a per-file option). Setting: none for the folder-menu create. New board from the command or ribbon (`app.json` `newFileLocation`, default root), card notes, pasted attachments and date links are confined by `data.json` `"new-note-folder": "inbox"`, `"link-date-to-daily-note": false` and `app.json` `"attachmentFolderPath"` off the roster (default `"/"`). Verifier: confirmed; hinge as for Excalidraw: if detection were reinstated as a way to pass, the cell would turn on the third ruling's basis (`src/main.ts:339-377` @`8501981`).

**Claudian (`realclaudian`), `B-community-2`.**

- Repository `YishenTu/claudian` (registry `yishentu/claudian`, `community-plugins.json:26516 @e76f2c3`). Pin: release 2.3.12 (2026-10-02), annotated tag `3faaf24` to commit `a407a9e4df8101c2399edf392cb4a6109067661c`.
- Licence: MIT (`LICENSE @a407a9e`; the copyright line gives the year 2025 and no holder). `main.js` emits no licence comments, but it bundles `@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk` 0.3.283 (`package.json:69`; absent from the externals at `esbuild.config.mjs:196-213`; patched into the bundle at `:85-99`; SDK text at `main.js` byte offset about 251035 to 251045). That SDK's `LICENSE.md:1` at npm 0.3.283 reserves all rights and makes use subject to Anthropic's legal agreements; npm's licence field points to the README. Also bundled: `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk`, ws, cross-spawn, jsonc-parser (MIT per npm), and, per the verifier, `@lezer/markdown` (MIT) and tslib (0BSD); `@codemirror/*` stays external.
- Writes: vault-root `.claudian/claudian-settings.json` instead of a `data.json` (`src/core/bootstrap/storagePaths.ts:3-5`; `src/app/settings/ClaudianSettingsStorage.ts:577`); `.claudian/sessions/**/*.meta.json` (`storagePaths.ts:8-9`); `.claude/commands/*.md`, `.claude/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` and `.agents/skills` when the user creates commands or skills (`ClaudeCommandRepository.ts`, `ClaudeSkillSync.ts`, `AgentSkillRepository.ts:25-28`). Embedded agents use the vault as working directory and write any path the model chooses, subject to `permissionMode` (default `auto`, `src/app/settings/defaultSettings.ts:9`; auto, manual, acceptEdits and yolo map to the SDK's auto, default, acceptEdits and bypassPermissions, `src/providers/claude/permissionModes.ts:9-17`). The desk listed Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode and Grok backends; the verifier found Pi and ACP backends too (`src/providers/{acp,pi}`). Verifier startup mutations: on every load it deletes the vault's `.claude/mcp.json` unconditionally (`src/main.ts:464`; `LegacyMCPConfigCleanup.ts:3-9`) and migrates or deletes `.claude/sessions/*` (`migrateSessionSidecars.ts:29-55`); skill sync, started from the settings modal, moves `.claude/skills` packages into `.agents/skills`, trashes leftovers and replaces the folder with a link (`ClaudeSkillSync.ts:23-31,141-146`).
- B1: desktop-only; needs a Claude Code or other agent binary on the host.
- B2 fail: an alternative host for agents, including the session that runs research-vault's skills, not a step; the floor uses no agent (comparison `:68`; the desk cited `:67`).
- B3: desk **needs-live**: no plugin key confines writes by path; `permissionMode: "manual"` in `.claudian/claudian-settings.json` asks per call but is not path-scoped; the Claude provider runs Claude Code with setting sources project and local (user only with `loadUserSettings`), so research-vault's PreToolUse guard (`hooks/pretooluse_guard.py:11-24`) may deny writes, only if the plugin is enabled at a loaded scope; other backends never see that hook. **Verifier: fail.** No live result could make it pass: the guard matches only Edit, Write and NotebookEdit (`hooks/hooks.json:6`; `pretooluse_guard.py:107`), so a Bash-tool write into literature/ or log/ bypasses it even when it fires; the non-Claude backends never see it; and `loadUserSettings` defaults to true (`src/providers/claude/settings.ts:35`), so user, project and local all load by default (`settings.ts:89-96`). It could pass only if per-call user approval counts as configured-not-to.
- B4 fail: commands and skills created through it are `.md` files under `.claude/` with frontmatter but no `type`. verify walks every `*.md` except `.git`, `.raw` and `.vault-meta`, and `Path.rglob` enters dot-dirs; the verifier built such files under `/tmp` and confirmed with Python 3.12.3 that `structure.is_excluded` returns False and `expected_type` returns None (`verify.py:752-757`; `structure.py:36,46-47`; `okf-conformance.md:9-10`). These files appear only when the user creates commands or skills. Its own state is JSON. What agents write into notes is model output.
- B5 fail: the plugin's code is MIT, but the released `main.js` bundles the Claude Agent SDK, whose licence reserves all rights and grants no redistribution. Whether Anthropic's linked agreements allow redistribution is open (§7.9).
- Exit (desk): Stop loading it and delete .claudian/; files its agents wrote are ordinary vault files, and any .claude/commands and .claude/skills it created stay behind as Claude Code project files.
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 fail† → pass† — counted: under the default `permissionMode` `"auto"` (the SDK's auto mode), embedded agents write paths the model chooses, through Bash too, without asking. With `"manual"`, which maps to the SDK's default mode, every tool except AskUserQuestion goes through `canUseTool` to Claudian's approval prompt, and no `allowedTools` list reaches the SDK. The load-time delete of `.claude/mcp.json`, the `.claude/sessions` migration and the files under `.claudian/` are off the roster; inline edit runs read-only tools and title generation none; it has no CLI or protocol handler. Settings, in the vault-root `.claudian/claudian-settings.json` (it keeps no `data.json`): `"permissionMode": "manual"`, and also `savedProviderPermissionMode.claude` `"manual"`, because the settings coordinator reads the saved per-provider mode first; `providerConfigs.claude.loadUserSettings` `false`, which drops user-level settings so a user allow rule cannot pre-approve a tool; `providerConfigs.codex`, `.opencode`, `.pi` and `.grok` `"enabled": false` (defaults). The vault template ships no `.claude/`, so a fresh vault carries no project allow rules; a project or local Claude Code settings file added later could. This settles the first pass's per-call-approval hinge on two readings of this pass (§2A, Second ruling): an approval prompt is a confirm dialog, and a seedable settings file outside `.obsidian/` counts. Live check inherited: whether the prompt fires for Write, Edit and Bash inside an embedded session (`src/app/settings/defaultSettings.ts:9`; `src/providers/claude/permissionModes.ts:12-17`, `:40`; `src/providers/claude/execution/ClaudeExecutionRequestEncoder.ts:119-121`, `:157`, `:170`; `src/providers/claude/execution/ClaudeInteractionHandler.ts:41-121`; `src/providers/claude/settings.ts:31`, `:35`, `:90-96`; `src/core/providers/ProviderSettingsCoordinator.ts:397-401`; `src/providers/index.ts:11-17` @`a407a9e`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass†§ → fail†§∆ — manual mode's approval prompt no longer changes the result. Embedded agents (Claude Code, plus Codex, OpenCode, Pi, Grok and ACP when enabled) write any path the model chooses through Write, Edit and Bash under every permission mode (auto, manual, acceptEdits and yolo, mapped to the SDK's auto, default, acceptEdits and bypassPermissions; the Claude provider has no read-only or plan mode). Inline edit, on accept, replaces the selection in the source note with `editor.replaceRange`. research-vault's PreToolUse guard matches only Edit, Write and NotebookEdit, and only where the plugin loads at a scope the SDK reads, so Bash writes and non-Claude backends bypass it; writes into wiki/, fulltext/ and log/ appends go unreported. Setting: none; `permissionMode` `"manual"` only adds a prompt. Verifier: confirmed (`src/providers/claude/permissionModes.ts:9-45`; `src/providers/claude/execution/ClaudeExecutionRequestEncoder.ts:150-180`; `src/features/inline-edit/ui/InlineEditModal.ts:886-907` @`a407a9e`; `hooks/hooks.json:6`; `hooks/pretooluse_guard.py:11-23`).

**Copilot (`copilot`), `B-community-2`.**

- Repository `logancyang/obsidian-copilot` (`community-plugins.json:5747 @e76f2c3`). Pin: release 4.0.13 (2026-10-02) = commit `a775bfb7a6659d5591833beaa86cbc509c1f475e`.
- Licence: AGPL-3.0 (`LICENSE @a775bfb`). `main.js` has a bundled-licence block covering react, react-dom, scheduler and react-jsx-runtime (MIT), lucide-react (ISC), `@google/generative-ai`, `@langchain/core` helpers (fast-json-patch, js-sha256, sax-js), mustache, buffer and ieee754. It also bundles `@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk` 0.3.206 (`package.json:101`; not among the externals at `esbuild.config.mjs:57-71`; SDK code at about byte 947158 to 947182 and near 1417955) with no notice. The verifier read `LICENSE.md:1` at npm 0.3.206 itself: all rights reserved, as at 0.3.283.
- Writes: `data.json` (`src/services/settingsPersistence.ts:183`), including a `userId: uuidv4()` default (`src/constants.ts:657`); autosaved chats in vault-root `copilot/copilot-conversations/` by default (`constants.ts:28-35`, `:683-685`); prompts, memory, system prompts, projects and skills under `copilot/` (`:31-35`, `:689`, `:703`, `:739`, `:747`, `:760`); hidden caches `.copilot/file-content-cache` and `.copilot/pdf-cache` (`src/cache/fileCache.ts:19`; `src/cache/pdfCache.ts:8`); startup migrations call `ensureFolderExists` (`src/system-prompts/migration.ts:40,106`); built-in agent tools `writeFile`, `editFile` and `updateMemory` on by default (`constants.ts:710`, `:727-737`; `autoAcceptEdits` false, `:745`); the OpenArtifacts share action writes an `openartifacts` frontmatter key into the shared note (`src/openArtifacts/openArtifactsFrontmatter.ts:5,96`); Agent Mode backends (default opencode, `constants.ts:751-753`; Claude SDK; Codex). Verifier additions: every new chat runs `addRecentConversation` (`src/main.ts:1353-1357`) while `enableRecentConversations` is true (default, `constants.ts:740`), writing LLM-generated text with no frontmatter to `copilot/memory/Recent Conversations.md` (`src/memory/UserMemoryManager.ts:194`, `:270-300`); `Saved Memories.md` works the same way (`:198`, `:201-264`; `enableSavedMemory` default true, `constants.ts:742`); `copilot/copilot-log.md` is created only by the open-log command (`logFileManager.ts:32`, `:165-197`).
- B2 fail: a chat and agent host; the floor uses no AI.
- B3 fail: the built-in agent can be configured (`data.json` `"enableAutonomousAgent": false`, or drop `"writeFile"` and `"editFile"` from `"autonomousAgentEnabledToolIds"`). Two legs have no confining key: the OpenArtifacts share writes into whichever note is shared, literature/ included, with no enable key; and Agent Mode backends take a per-session permission mode, not a path scope (`src/agentMode/sdk/ClaudeSdkBackendProcess.ts:132-138,315`). The desk left open whether research-vault's hook applies to the Claude SDK backend; the verifier answered on paper: with no `settingSources` passed, the SDK's documentation says all sources load (`sdk.d.ts:1861-1870` at 0.3.206), and Copilot injects its own PreToolUse hook (`:315`). Either way the guard covers only Edit, Write and NotebookEdit.
- B4 fail: conversation notes carry epoch, modelKey, topic, lastAccessedAt and tags but no `type` (`src/core/ChatPersistenceManager.ts:659-667`; desk `:660-668`), written without a save action because `autosaveChat` defaults to true; stamp-type cannot type `copilot/`. The memory files breach rule 1. Named keys: `"autosaveChat": false`, and per the verifier also `"enableRecentConversations": false` and `"enableSavedMemory": false`.
- B5 fail: AGPL-3.0 alone would allow redistribution with the licence text and source; the released artifact contains SDK code under a non-AGPL, all-rights-reserved licence.
- Fact: a seeded `data.json` would give every scaffolded vault the same `userId` unless the seed omits the key.
- Exit (desk): Stop loading it; the copilot/ notes and .copilot/ caches it wrote stay as ordinary files you can keep or delete, and any openartifacts frontmatter keys stay as plain properties.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 fail and B4 fail unchanged, each now resting on Agent Mode alone. The OpenArtifacts share is a chosen-file edit, and the built-in agent is confined by `data.json` `"enableAutonomousAgent": false`. Agent Mode is gated only on platform, so it is on for every desktop, and its backends write paths the model chooses under a per-session permission mode with no path scope (`src/agentMode/index.ts:54-56`; `src/agentMode/sdk/ClaudeSdkBackendProcess.ts:132-138`, `:315`; `src/constants.ts:710`, `:727-737`). For B4, the conversation, memory, prompt, project and skill folders are all seedable and could be routed under inbox/ (`src/constants.ts:28-35`, `:683-760`). Agent Mode's model-written files are the remaining untyped output.
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 fail → pass — the re-judgement passed it on `data.json` `"autoAcceptEdits": false` with `agentMode.backends.<id>.defaultMode` `"default"`. The verifier refuted that basis twice, and its key set is the one recorded. First, with `autoAcceptEdits` false, `writeFile` shows a preview, but the preview's file lookup first creates the parent folders and an empty file at the path the model chose, and rejecting the preview leaves them, so an unprompted create can land on any roster path. Second, the Claude SDK backend passes an `allowedTools` list that includes Write and Edit, which the SDK's options documentation says run without asking (`sdk.d.ts:1329-1335` at npm 0.3.206, paraphrased; that licence reserves all rights), so `defaultMode` `"default"` confines nothing. The pass survives on other keys: `data.json` `autonomousAgentEnabledToolIds` without `"writeFile"`, with `"enableAutonomousAgent"` left `true` (switching the agent off routes Plus chats to a runner whose action-block streamer always runs `writeFile`, which replaces the first pass's `enableAutonomousAgent` false); `"defaultChainType": "llm_chain"` (the default, no tools); and `agentMode.activeBackend` `"opencode"` (the default), whose default agent asks before edits and shell. Codex starts read-only through its launch environment, so its `defaultMode` need not be seeded, and a Claude `defaultMode` of `"plan"` is not dependable because it is applied only when a model selection resolves. Counted and off the roster at defaults: chat autosave, the Recent Conversations and Saved Memories notes, startup folder creation, the `.copilot/` caches, and a vault-root `CLAUDE.md` that an Agent Mode session creates or appends when a root `AGENTS.md` exists; an empty `convertedDocOutputFolder` writes nothing. The OpenArtifacts share is the person's action, and the `copilot-chat` URI only opens a chat. Hinge: choosing a backend or chain is read as the person changing a setting; if it is not, a Claude session has no confining key and the cell fails. Live check inherited: whether the OpenCode binary honours the ask configuration Copilot hands it (`src/tools/ComposerTools.ts:11-41`, `:44-50`, `:148-170`, `:468-478`; `src/agentMode/sdk/ClaudeSdkBackendProcess.ts:280-300`; `src/LLMProviders/chainRunner/CopilotPlusChainRunner.ts:602-605`; `src/LLMProviders/chainManager.ts:115-124`; `src/constants.ts:674`, `:710`, `:727-737`, `:745`, `:751-753`; `src/agentMode/session/AgentSessionManager.ts:806-817`, `:1309-1318`; `src/agentMode/ui/agentModelPickerHelpers.ts:315-335`; `src/agentMode/backends/opencode/OpencodeBackend.ts:382-398`; `src/agentMode/backends/codex/CodexBackend.ts:37-40` @`a775bfb`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass§ → fail§∆ — Agent Mode is gated only on platform, so it is on for every desktop; its OpenCode, Claude SDK and Codex sessions write paths the model chooses, the Claude SDK backend pre-approves Write and Edit, and OpenCode's ask prompt no longer counts. The `writeFile` preview creates parent folders and an empty file at the model's path before the person accepts or rejects. The OpenArtifacts share writes an `openartifacts` key into the shared note's frontmatter through `processFrontMatter`, a non-capture key that evidence-layer does not compare in literature/. Writes into wiki/, fulltext/ and log/ appends go unreported. Setting: none; `autonomousAgentEnabledToolIds` without `writeFile` and `defaultChainType` `llm_chain` confine only the built-in agent, and the second pass's reading that choosing a backend is a settings change no longer decides it. Verifier: confirmed (`src/agentMode/index.ts:54-56`; `src/agentMode/sdk/ClaudeSdkBackendProcess.ts:280-300`; `src/tools/ComposerTools.ts:11-50`, `:148-170`; `src/openArtifacts/openArtifactsFrontmatter.ts:5`, `:96` @`a775bfb`; `research_vault/lints.py:698-725`).

**Remotely Save (`remotely-save`), `B-community-2`.**

- Pin: release 0.5.25 (2024-10-20) = commit `e39edcea70ca65d7a6357da21d0cb46a6068380e`; last push 2024-11-10; no release since.
- Licence: split (`LICENSE:1-3 @e39edce`). `src/`, `tests/`, `docs/` and `assets/` are Apache-2.0; `pro/` is PolyForm Strict 1.0.0 (`pro/LICENSE`). `main.js` comments include `@azure/msal-common` and `msal-node`, noble-hashes and noble-ciphers (MIT), safe-buffer (MIT), ieee754 (BSD-3-Clause), punycode and lucide.
- Writes: `data.json` in an obfuscated form (`src/main.ts:1515`); local sync metadata in IndexedDB; on sync, creates, overwrites, renames and deletes any vault path from the remote, minus `ignorePaths` and `onlyAllowPaths` and, by default, `.obsidian` and `_`-prefixed items; conflicts resolve to `keep_newer` (`main.ts:148`); deletions go to the system trash (`:146`). All automatic triggers are off by default (`:132-134`).
- B2 fail: no sync step; the floor's starter vault ships no sync plugin (`a3d22d0a .obsidian/community-plugins.json`).
- B3 named key: `data.json` `"ignorePaths"` as anchored regexes for the machine surfaces, for example `^literature/`, `^fulltext/`, `^wiki/`, `^log/`, `^system/`, `^inbox/review-queue\.md$`, `^log\.md$`; matching paths are skipped in both directions (default `[]`, `main.ts:143`; the skip logic is in the PolyForm-licensed `pro/src/sync.ts:191-198`, paraphrased). Excluding them also means they never sync. The verifier added that `howToCleanEmptyFolder` defaults to `clean_both` (`main.ts:150`); empty folders under machine surfaces escape it only through the same `ignorePaths`.
- B4 pass: it copies file bytes without changing content; encryption applies only to the remote copy.
- B5 fail: the released `main.js` contains `pro/` code (the sync engine is imported from `../pro/src/sync` at `src/main.ts:65`, the pro back-ends at `:25-64`; `main.js` carries pro-only identifiers: koofr 215 times, pcloud 214, yandexdisk 190, azureblobstorage 167; Koofr at byte 3249494), and PolyForm Strict's copyright grant excludes distribution (`pro/LICENSE:11-17`).
- Verifier refuted the desk's open question that seeding requires the obfuscated format: plain JSON passes through `messyConfigToNormal` unchanged (`src/configPersist.ts:26-43`; `loadSettings` at `main.ts:1338-1343`), but `loadSettings` re-saves at once (`main.ts:1510`), obfuscating unless the seed sets `"obfuscateSettingFile": false` (default true, `main.ts:152`; save branch `:1513-1519`).
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass unchanged, with the seed key restated against the guard roster: `data.json` `"ignorePaths": ["^literature/", "^fulltext/", "^wiki/", "^log/", "^system/propagations/", "^system/bibliography\\.json$", "^inbox/review-queue\\.md$", "^log\\.md$"]` with `"obfuscateSettingFile": false`, so the plain seed survives the re-save on load. The draft list's `^system/` is narrowed to the roster's two system paths (`src/main.ts:143`, `:152`, `:1510-1519`; `pro/src/sync.ts:191-202`, `:280-302`, PolyForm, paraphrased). Cite fix: `howToCleanEmptyFolder` is at `src/main.ts:149`; `:150` is `protectModifyPercentage`.
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass → pass — basis widened, key unchanged. Sync is counted as remote whoever starts it; its automatic triggers (schedule, on save, startup) default off; it creates, overwrites, renames and deletes any path from the remote and cleans empty folders. Setting: `data.json` `"ignorePaths"` (the roster list above) with `"obfuscateSettingFile": false` keeps every sync off the roster in both directions. New fact: the `obsidian://remotely-save?func=settings` URI handler checks only that the vault name matches, then merges caller-supplied settings into `data.json` and saves them with no prompt. Its own write is `data.json`, off the roster, but it can clear `ignorePaths` and switch on automatic runs, and no key disables it. Hinge: this pass judged each counted behaviour on its own writes; under a composite reading the cell fails (`src/main.ts:132-134`, `:143`, `:152`, `:586-603`, `:1513-1519`; `src/importExport.ts:97-140` @`e39edce`; `pro/src/sync.ts:191-202`, PolyForm, paraphrased).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass → fail∆ — sync (command, ribbon, schedule, on save, startup) creates, overwrites, renames and deletes any path from the remote, and `ignorePaths` keeps it off the roster; but the `obsidian://remotely-save` handler with `func=settings` is registered unconditionally at load, checks only the vault name and that the data parses as JSON, and merges caller-supplied settings into `data.json` and saves them with no prompt. A caller can clear `ignorePaths`, point the vault at its own remote, and set `agreeToUseSyncV3`, `initRunAfterMilliseconds` or `autoRunEveryMilliseconds`; the next load arms those runs, and the sync then writes roster paths: wiki/, fulltext/ and log/ appends unreported, deleted log/ history reported by append-only. Setting: none for the URI route; `ignorePaths` (the roster list) with `"obfuscateSettingFile": false` confines every sync trigger only until the route rewrites it. This turns on `B-top20`'s reading that a remote route rewriting the confining key defeats it, which replaces the second pass's reading (§2A Third ruling); judged on the route's own write (`data.json`) alone, the cell would pass on `ignorePaths`. Verifier: confirmed; the sync's path filter applies only `ignorePaths` and `onlyAllowPaths` (`src/main.ts:132-155`, `:586-602`, `:1302-1309`, `:1757-1785`; `src/importExport.ts:97-141` @`e39edce`; `pro/src/sync.ts:185-205`, PolyForm Strict, paraphrased).

**Iconize (`obsidian-icon-folder`), `B-community-2`.**

- Repository `FlorianWoelki/obsidian-iconize` (`community-plugins.json:1767 @e76f2c3`). Pin: release 2.14.7 (2025-01-03) = commit `1264d5800a027356b69f353b009d40b9f7b47a2c`; last push 2026-04-27; no release since.
- Licence: MIT (`LICENSE:1-3`, Florian Woelki). Desk notice record: twemoji (MIT). The verifier added JSZip 3.10.1 (MIT or GPLv3), pako (MIT, named in the JSZip notice) and a Microsoft tslib notice (0BSD); `package.json` lists jszip as its only runtime dependency.
- Writes: `data.json`, holding settings and a path-to-icon map its rename and delete listeners keep current (`src/main.ts:273-290`, `:869-881`); icon pack files under `iconPacksPath`, default `.obsidian/icons` written as a literal name rather than through the config dir (`src/settings/data.ts:193`; desk `src/icon-pack-manager.ts:211-279`, `:625`; verifier `:164-279`, `:609-625`; unused icons pruned at `src/lib/icon.ts:163`); in-note icon shortcodes inserted at the cursor when the user picks a suggestion (`src/editor/icons-suggestion.ts:105-122`; `iconsInNotesEnabled` default true, `data.ts:213`). Frontmatter `icon` and `iconColor` are read only (`main.ts:575-628`; `iconInFrontmatterEnabled` default false, `data.ts:209`); the source has no `processFrontMatter`, `vault.create` or `vault.modify` call.
- B2 fail: cosmetic; the floor reading does not mention icons.
- B3 named keys: `data.json` `"iconsInNotesEnabled": false`, and `"iconPacksPath"` set explicitly to the vault's real config directory.
- B4 pass: no frontmatter writes; inserted shortcodes (identifier `:` plus icon name, `data.ts:215`) stay literal text with no meaning outside the plugin.
- B5 pass†: MIT; result stands, notice record incomplete (above).
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass unchanged and needs no key. A shortcode inserted at the cursor is a chosen-file edit, and the automatic writes (`data.json` and icon-pack files under `iconPacksPath`, default `.obsidian/icons`) are off the roster. `iconsInNotesEnabled` and `iconPacksPath` no longer decide B3 (`src/editor/icons-suggestion.ts:105-122`; `src/settings/data.ts:193`, `:213`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass → pass — now on a named key. Its only note write is the icon suggester, which replaces a typed query with a shortcode in the open note through `replaceRange`; it is registered only inside the `iconsInNotesEnabled` branch (default true). Set icon for file and the file-menu items write only `data.json`, and every adapter write, mkdir, rmdir and remove, the unused-icon prune included, goes under `iconPacksPath`. Setting: `data.json` `"iconsInNotesEnabled": false` (the seed must set it) and `"iconPacksPath"` off the roster (default `".obsidian/icons"`). Verifier: confirmed by a full-source grep: no `processFrontMatter`, no vault create, modify or process call, and no protocol or CLI handler (`src/main.ts:120-160`, `:296-306`; `src/editor/icons-suggestion.ts:105-122`; `src/settings/data.ts:193`, `:213`; `src/icon-pack-manager.ts:25-27`, `:164-279`, `:609-625`; `src/lib/icon.ts:150-170` @`1264d58`).

**QuickAdd (`quickadd`), `B-community-2`; also seed.**

- Pin: release 2.30.0 (2026-10-03) = commit `3aef4eeea899d5ac17fc64533e52d8b174100126`.
- Licence: MIT (`LICENSE:1-3`, Christian Bager Bach Houmann). `main.js` has no licence comments, though it bundles chrono-node (MIT), fuse.js 7 (Apache-2.0), svelte-dnd-action (MIT), three-way-merge (MIT) and zustand (MIT) (`package.json` dependencies at the pin; licences from npm).
- Writes: `data.json` (`src/main.ts:417`). Template, Capture and Macro choices write only when a user-defined choice runs, each naming its own target (`src/engine/TemplateEngine.ts`, `CaptureChoiceEngine.ts`, `QuickAddEngine.ts`); macros run user JS. AI vault tools are gated by `disableOnlineFeatures` (default true, `src/settings.ts:128`) and `confirmToolCalls` destructive. The `obsidian://quickadd` URI runs a named choice (`src/uri/registerQuickAddUri.ts:23`). Vault listeners are read-only index caches (`src/gui/suggesters/FileIndex.ts:200-216`).
- B2: floor. The guide lists QuickAdd as needed, for workspace switching (comparison `:63-65`). The verifier added that the floor's use is three Macro choices calling Advanced URI commands and vault user scripts (`a3d22d0a .obsidian/plugins/quickadd/data.json:2-24`), which a `choices: []` seed does not reproduce, so Advanced URI is a co-requirement for that role. It could not verify the desk's statement that the task lists QuickAdd in the author's seed (the seed list is the enumeration's, §2.2).
- B3 pass†: desk named keys `"choices": []` (default), `"disableOnlineFeatures": true` and `"enableUriCallbacks": false` (`settings.ts:114-115,128-129`), and said no writer then exists. The verifier refuted that: with no choice configured, the Apply template command and file-menu item write into the active or right-clicked note in any folder (`src/plugin/registerCoreCommands.ts:40-70`; `applyTemplateToActiveNote.ts:113,171`), and Obsidian CLI handlers registered unconditionally at layout-ready (`src/main.ts:190-202`; `src/cli/registerQuickAddCliHandlers.ts:26-35,40-42,73-78`) include `quickadd:run-template`, which creates a note from any template path at `newFileLocation` or a caller-supplied name (`src/engine/TemplateChoiceEngine.ts:337-354`), and package-import. Every write needs a user or CLI caller, so the pass survives only under the reading that user-initiated writes do not count. The author's vault has choices writing into `notes/fleeting` and `notes/claims` (§2.2).
- B4 pass: output is the user's template text; the vault's seeded templates carry `type` (`daily.md:1-3`, `project.md:1-6`); captures into inbox/ fall under the untyped-fleeting deviation (`okf-conformance.md:36`).
- ‡ 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass† → fail†. `quickadd:run-template` is an Obsidian-CLI handler registered unconditionally at layout-ready. When a caller's name starts with an existing root folder, it treats the name as a vault path, so an external caller can create a note in literature/. No QuickAdd key disables it, and the CLI switch is global, not seedable. The same registration exposes package-import and save-clipboard-image writers. Apply-template writes go to the note the person chose and are not judged (`src/cli/registerQuickAddCliHandlers.ts:38-45`, `:73-78`, `:97-120`; `src/main.ts:190-202`; `src/engine/TemplateEngine.ts:144-168`; `src/engine/TemplateChoiceEngine.ts:337-392`). `data.json` `"choices": []`, `"enableUriCallbacks": false` and `"disableOnlineFeatures": true` cover the URI and AI paths.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B4 pass unchanged. Seed keys: `data.json` `"choices": []`, with `templateFolderPaths` (a list) naming the typed `system/templates` (`src/settings.ts:115`, `:123`, `:133`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 fail† → fail† — the CLI reading is now ruled. The Obsidian-CLI handlers, registered at layout-ready and checked only for API availability, include `quickadd:run-template`, which builds a template choice from any template path the caller names and, when the choice has no folder, treats the formatted name as a vault path, so a caller can create a note on a roster path; package-import and save-clipboard-image also write. No QuickAdd key unregisters them. The `quickadd` URI and startup macros run only configured choices, covered by `data.json` `"choices": []`, `"enableUriCallbacks": false` and `"disableOnlineFeatures": true`; Apply template on a note the person chose is deliberate (`src/main.ts:190-222`; `src/cli/registerQuickAddCliHandlers.ts:38-46`, `:73-78`, `:97-120`; `src/cli/runChoice.ts:64-82`; `src/engine/TemplateChoiceEngine.ts:337-392` @`3aef4ee`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 fail†‡ → fail†‡ — basis widened to Apply template. Failing paths: the CLI handlers registered at layout-ready (`quickadd:run-template`, where a name starting with a root folder is treated as a vault path, roster included; package-import; save-clipboard-image); Apply template (command and file menu) into the open or right-clicked note; user choices and macros, removed by `"choices": []`; the quickadd URI, removed by `"enableUriCallbacks": false`. Writes into wiki/, typed creates and edits in fulltext/, and new date-named log/ files go unreported. Setting: none for the CLI handlers or Apply template. Verifier: confirmed, with the same fulltext/ wording fix as Templater (`src/main.ts:190-222`; `src/cli/registerQuickAddCliHandlers.ts:38-46`, `:73-78`, `:97-120`; `src/plugin/registerCoreCommands.ts:39-72`; `src/engine/TemplateChoiceEngine.ts:337-394` @`3aef4ee`).

**Editing Toolbar (`editing-toolbar`), `B-community-2`.**

- Repository `PKM-er/obsidian-editing-toolbar` (`community-plugins.json:4007 @e76f2c3`). Pin: release 4.1.5 (2026-10-03) = commit `293968aacfca907a1e5ae3396ffa2734ba2db112`.
- Licence: MPL-2.0 (`LICENSE:1`). Notices: Pickr 1.8.4 (MIT) and one further MIT notice, which the verifier identified as SortableJS 1.15.7.
- Writes: `data.json` (`src/plugin/main.ts:1099`); formatting edits to the open note on click. With AI enabled it creates `<note> AI Article Draft/AI Slides.md`, `.canvas` and `.base` files in the active note's folder (`src/ai/AIEditorManager.ts:1260-1270`, `:1673-1683`), rewrites canvases (`src/ai/canvasScene.ts:575,697,884`) and edits frontmatter through the editor. AI defaults: `enabled` false, `consentAccepted` false (`src/ai/types.ts:405-408`), wired in at `src/settings/settingsData.ts:563`; when enabled its remote provider defaults to pkmer-first.
- B2 fail: general editing chrome; not the long-form step.
- B3 named key: `data.json` `"ai": {"enabled": false, ...}` (default). The verifier found that with defaults an AI consent modal opens automatically 1.2 s after startup (`src/plugin/main.ts:491-494`; `shouldShowAIFeatures` always returns true, `src/util/locale.ts:12-13`), first saving `onboardingShown` true to `data.json`, and that a single click turns AI on (`AIEditorManager.ts:103-120`). Named key to suppress it: `"ai"` with `"onboardingShown": true`, or an `ai` object without that key, which `main.ts:992-993` then sets to true. Without it the desk's live leg (no AI prompt) would fail.
- B4 pass: Markdown or inline HTML formatting; with AI off it touches no frontmatter.
- B5 pass: MPL-2.0 is file-level copyleft; redistribution allowed with the licence and source availability for MPL files.
- Exit (desk): Stop loading it; all that remains is the Markdown or HTML formatting users applied through it.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass and B4 pass unchanged. Formatting edits are chosen-file edits. The judged writer is AI generation, which creates `.md`, `.canvas` and `.base` files beside the active note. Seed key: `data.json` `"ai": {"enabled": false, "consentAccepted": false, "onboardingShown": true}`, which keeps AI off and suppresses the startup consent modal (`src/ai/AIEditorManager.ts:103-120`, `:1260-1270`, `:1673-1683`; `src/plugin/main.ts:491-494`; `src/ai/types.ts:405-408`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass → pass — basis moved: the only thing it writes by itself is its own `data.json` (the `onboardingShown` save shortly after startup). AI generation of `.md`, `.canvas` and `.base` files, and canvas rewrites, run only when the person invokes them; the protocol handler and local server only complete OAuth, and the metadata and vault hooks only invalidate a stats cache. `ai.enabled` and `onboardingShown` no longer decide B3 (`src/ai/AIEditorManager.ts:68-96`, `:1260-1270`, `:1673-1683`; `src/ai/PKMerAuthService.ts:380`; `src/plugin/main.ts:491-494` @`293968a`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass → fail∆ — the formatting commands and toolbar buttons registered at load (for example renumber list, insert or remove blank lines, inline formats) rewrite the open note with no settings check; AI generation writes `.md`, `.canvas` and `.base` files beside the active note and rewrites canvases. Writes into wiki/ and fulltext/ go unreported. Setting: none for the formatting commands; `"ai": {"enabled": false, "consentAccepted": false, "onboardingShown": true}` removes only the AI legs. Verifier: confirmed (`src/plugin/main.ts:476-477`; `src/commands/commands.ts:545-555`, `:649-700`; `src/ai/AIEditorManager.ts:1260-1270`, `:1673-1683` @`293968a`).
- ◇ 2026-10-04 fourth ruling: B3 fail∆ → pass∆◇ — the formatting commands and toolbar buttons rewrite the open note and are not judged. Counted: AI generation, which creates `.md`, `.canvas` and `.base` files beside the active note (in a roster folder when the active note is there) and rewrites canvases. Setting: `data.json` `"ai": {"enabled": false, "consentAccepted": false, "onboardingShown": true}`. Every AI entry point returns early unless `ai.enabled`, the AI menu items then only open the settings tab, and `onboardingShown` stops the startup consent modal, so no command or click turns AI on and generates files. The protocol handler and local server only complete OAuth, and the vault hooks only invalidate a statistics cache. Verifier: confirmed (`src/ai/AIEditorManager.ts:64-96`, `:103-142`, `:160-182`, `:554-562`, `:1260-1270`, `:1673-1683`; `src/plugin/main.ts:355-362`, `:410-418`, `:486-494` @`293968a`).

**Omnisearch (`omnisearch`), `B-community-2`.**

- Repository `scambier/obsidian-omnisearch` (`community-plugins.json:3139 @e76f2c3`). Pin: release 1.31.0 (2026-09-05), annotated tag `2e9fc04` to commit `48b7e324d945385495f3f3db92efc0684e033059`.
- Licence: GPL-3.0 (`LICENSE:1-2`; `package.json:16` says GPL-3). The `main.js` notice block names search-query-parser with no licence line (npm 1.6.0 is MIT). Also bundled without notices: minisearch (MIT, `:49`), dexie (Apache-2.0, `:46`); the verifier added es-toolkit, markdown-link-extractor, pure-md5 and cancelable-promise as unrecorded.
- Writes: `data.json` (`src/main.ts:191`); index and history in IndexedDB through Dexie (`src/database.ts:1-18`; `useCache` default true, `src/settings/index.ts:120`); deletes only its own legacy cache files (`main.ts:328-343`); a create-note action that makes an empty note at `app.json` `newFileLocation` (`src/tools/notes.ts:146-164`); HTTP API off by default (`httpApiEnabled` false, `:156`).
- B2 fail: the step `search` is literature search (PRISMA-S, lane 4; assembly spec `:143`, `:168`); the floor retrieves with Dataview views (comparison `:100`) and ships no Omnisearch.
- B3 pass†: desk named keys `"showCreateButton": false` and `"httpApiEnabled": false` (defaults) and said it then writes nothing beyond `data.json`. The verifier refuted that: `showCreateButton` only hides the button (`src/components/ModalVault.svelte:310`); the modal's create-note hotkeys fire regardless (`src/components/modals.ts:92-99`), creating at `newFileLocation`, so `"current"` places the note beside the active note. The key that confines it is in `app.json` (`"newFileLocation": "folder"` with a non-machine `"newFileFolderPath"`). Its insert-link action also writes into the active note on a gesture.
- B4 pass: holds only if `newFileFolderPath` is inbox/; the note is an empty `.md` with no frontmatter, which outside inbox/ (the vault root under core defaults) breaches §11 rule 1 (`okf-conformance.md:9,36`; `structure.py:50-51,78-79`). The verifier recorded this as inconsistent with Kanban's B4 fail (§7.2).
- Exit (desk): Stop loading it; the vault is unchanged, because its index lives in IndexedDB outside the vault.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass† and B4 pass unchanged. Seed keys: `app.json` `"newFileLocation": "folder"`, `"newFileFolderPath": "inbox"`. In folder mode the create-note action prefixes `newFileFolderPath`, so a query containing a slash stays under inbox/; root mode would let a query such as `literature/x` through. Insert-link is a chosen-file edit, and the HTTP API serves only search (`src/tools/notes.ts:146-164`). The created note is empty and conforms only under the untyped-inbox deviation (`okf-conformance.md:36`; `structure.py:78-79`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass† → pass† — basis moved: nothing is counted into the roster. The HTTP API, off by default, answers only `/search`; the `omnisearch` URI only opens the search modal; vault hooks reindex into IndexedDB; the startup cleanup deletes only its own legacy files under the config directory; and its one interval decrements an error counter. Create-note and insert-link are the person's modal actions, so the `app.json` `newFileLocation` and `newFileFolderPath` keys no longer decide B3 (`src/tools/api-server.ts:10-40`; `src/tools/api.ts:73-75`; `src/main.ts:328-343`; `src/tools/notes.ts:146-164`; `src/repositories/documents-repository.ts:30-36` @`48b7e32`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass† → fail†∆ — Insert link (Alt+Enter in the vault search modal, always bound) writes a link with `replaceRange` at the cursor of the open note. For a `.md` result the link resolves, so a write into a wiki/ page goes unreported, and fulltext/ notes and log/ appends go unreported either way; for a non-`.md` result the fallback link does not resolve, and captured-set would flag it in wiki/. Create note (Shift+Enter or the button) writes an empty note at `newFileLocation`. Setting: none for insert-link; `app.json` `"newFileLocation": "folder"` with `"newFileFolderPath": "inbox"` removes only the create-note leg. Verifier: confirmed with the non-`.md` refinement (`src/components/modals.ts:86-100`; `src/components/ModalVault.svelte:221-257`; `src/tools/notes.ts:146-164` @`48b7e32`).
- ◇ 2026-10-04 fourth ruling: B3 fail†∆ → pass†∆◇ — Insert link at the cursor of the open note is not judged. Counted: Create note (Shift+Enter or the button in the vault search modal), which creates an empty note other than the open one through `openLinkText`. Setting: `app.json` `"newFileLocation": "folder"`, `"newFileFolderPath": "inbox"`; in folder mode the link text is the folder, a slash, the query and `.md`, so a query holding a slash stays under inbox/. Verifier: confirmed, with two edges read in Obsidian 1.13.7: a query beginning `../` leaves at most an empty directory, because an existing roster folder makes the folder create throw and the hidden-segment test drops a `..` path before a note is written; and the query `review-queue` opens the seeded `inbox/review-queue.md`, creating that roster file only if it had been deleted. The HTTP API serves only `/search`, and the URI only opens the modal. Cite correction: the create is `src/components/ModalVault.svelte:203-218`; `:221-257` is Insert link (`src/tools/notes.ts:146-164`; `src/components/modals.ts:86-100`; `src/tools/api-server.ts:10-40` @`48b7e32`; Obsidian 1.13.7 `app.js:1@byte 1539666`, `@byte 1775532`, `@byte 1392826`, `@byte 551049`).

**TaskNotes (`tasknotes`), `B-community-3`.**

- Pin: release 4.13.8, tag commit `1ba391b957c6fbbdc484716bbc5a284f5f02e59d`. Assets: `main.js` (4,544,257 B), `manifest.json`, `styles.css` (611,855 B).
- Licence: MIT (`LICENSE:1-5`, Callum Alpass; GitHub SPDX MIT). The bundled-licence block at `main.js:3710` lists only reflect-metadata (Apache-2.0). It also bundles ical.js 2.2.1, MPL-2.0 (desk `package-lock.json:6927-6930`, verifier `:6926-6930`; `package.json:98`), with no MPL notice; rrule is BSD-3-Clause. The verifier corrected the desk's view that the rest are MIT, ISC or 0BSD: non-dev lock entries also include fast-uri 3.1.8 and qs 6.16.0 (BSD-3-Clause), json-schema-typed 8.0.2 (BSD-2-Clause) and tasknotes-nlp-core 0.1.5 with no licence field (npm says MIT); the MCP SDK is bundled (`MCPService.ts:2-3`). The release ships no LICENSE file.
- Writes, desk: task notes (`.md` with YAML frontmatter) into `tasksFolder` `TaskNotes/Tasks` (`src/settings/defaults.ts:252`); on startup, default `.base` views under `TaskNotes/Views/` (`pluginBootstrap.ts:365-366`; `defaults.ts:388`); on first install, `TaskNotes/Start Here.md` with no frontmatter (`starterNote.ts:5-9,176`; attempted only when `lastSeenVersion` is absent, `main.ts:770`); instant convert rewrites the checkbox line in the open note (`InstantTaskConvertService.ts:1094`) and creates the task in `inlineTaskConvertFolder`, default `{{currentNotePath}}` (`defaults.ts:311`); renames task files when a title changes (`TaskUpdateService.ts:122-140`); the mini-calendar creates daily notes through core daily-notes (`MiniCalendarView.ts:1053-1080`); pomodoro history to `data.json` or daily notes (verifier: the daily-note write is `PomodoroService.ts:1487-1501` and `:1761`; the desk's `:1241` is a load branch); HTTP API, MCP and webhooks off by default (`defaults.ts:359-364`).
- Writes, verifier additions (all off by default except the first): `autoCreateDefaultBasesFiles` (default true, `defaults.ts:388`) also overwrites existing `.base` files on startup (`defaultBasesFiles.ts:101`); `icsIntegration.enableAutoExport` (`:179`) writes `tasknotes-calendar.ics` at the vault root (`AutoExportService.ts:144`); `calendarViewSettings.enableTimeblocking` (`:154`) creates daily notes and writes a `timeblocks` field into them (`TimeblockCreationModal.ts:395,439-442`), a direct log/ write if core daily-notes points there; `enableMdbaseSpec` (`:387`; `MdbaseSpecService.ts:134-149`); `enableGoogleCalendar` and `enableMicrosoftCalendar` (`:406-407`). ICS event notes go to `icsIntegration.defaultNoteFolder` (default `""`, the root) with tags but no type, and no identification key types them (`ICSNoteService.ts:385,451-456,508`). `data.json` carries `googleCalendarSyncTokens` and `microsoftCalendarSyncTokens` (`defaults.ts:412,416`).
- B2 fail: no step; the floor's aggregator is a checkbox-only Dataview TASK query (comparison `:225`, `:362-364`; assembly spec `:146`). The verifier noted that comparison `:15` routes the aggregator to the Obsidian ecosystem in general and `:66` records the starter vault's Kanban, which TaskNotes' kanban view overlaps; neither changed the result.
- B3 named keys (desk): `tasksFolder` outside the machine surfaces (the default is); `inlineTaskConvertFolder: ""` (falls back to `tasksFolder`) or `enableInstantTaskConvert: false`; `pomodoroStorageLocation: "plugin"`; `moveArchivedTasks: false`; `enableAPI: false`; `enableMCP: false`; `webhooks: []` (`defaults.ts:252,253,286,292,311,359,362`, verifier adds `:364`; desk `TaskCreationService.ts:428-434`, verifier `:422-439`). The mini-calendar's daily note follows the core daily-notes folder, which no TaskNotes key controls; renames rewrite links per `app.json` `alwaysUpdateLinks`. The verifier added the auto-writers above as B3 keys, and found that only `enableInstantTaskConvert: false` stops the converter rewriting the checkbox line in the open note, which can be a log/ note.
- B4 pass: holds only with `taskIdentificationMethod: "property"`, `taskPropertyName: "type"`, `taskPropertyValue: "task"` (writes `type: task` into every task note, `TaskCreationService.ts:298-302,330-335`) and `starterNoteCreated: true` (`starterNote.ts:143`). At defaults it fails twice: tags but no `type` (rule 2), and the starter note has no frontmatter (rule 1); `stamp.py:29-30` reports no-type without fixing it. The `.base` files are not `.md`, but their views name plugin-only types (`src/templates/defaultBasesFiles.ts:633-739`).
- B5 pass: MIT with the notice added (the assets ship no LICENSE); ical.js's MPL-2.0 notice and a source pointer are owed; the notice list is larger than the desk recorded (above).
- Exit (desk): Stop loading it. Task notes stay plain Markdown with YAML frontmatter, but the TaskNotes/Views/\*.base files use plugin-only view types (tasknotesTaskList, tasknotesKanban, tasknotesMiniCalendar) that stop rendering.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass unchanged. Seed keys: `data.json` `"inlineTaskConvertFolder": ""` (falls back to `tasksFolder`); `"storeTitleInFilename": false` (the default is true, so the seed must set it); `"pomodoroStorageLocation": "plugin"`; `"enableAPI": false`; `"enableMCP": false`; `"webhooks": []`; `calendarViewSettings.enableTimeblocking` false; `icsIntegration.enableAutoExport` false; `"moveArchivedTasks": false` (default); `tasksFolder` off the roster (default `"TaskNotes/Tasks"`); and `daily-notes.json` `folder` not `log`. The CLI capture and time-tracking handlers write only task notes in `tasksFolder` (`src/settings/defaults.ts:154`, `:179`, `:252-253`, `:267`, `:286`, `:311`, `:359`, `:362`, `:364`; `src/services/task-service/TaskUpdateService.ts:121-141`; `src/services/task-service/TaskCreationService.ts:422-439`; `src/cli/registerCliHandlers.ts:10-36`).
- ‡ 2026-10-03 normalisation: B4 pass → fail. This is new evidence, not a consequence of the ruling. Starting a timer pushes a time-entry map into a frontmatter list, and stopping it sets the entry's end time, through `processFrontMatter`. Obsidian 1.13.7 serialises that in block style, which the vault's flat parser rejects as a nested map. The CLI start and stop handlers are unconditional, and no key disables time tracking. The identification keys (`taskIdentificationMethod` `"property"`, `taskPropertyName` `"type"`, `taskPropertyValue` `"task"`, `starterNoteCreated` `true`) cover rule 2 at creation only (`src/services/task-service/taskTimeTrackingPlanning.ts:49-66`, `:85-106`; `src/services/TaskService.ts:1580-1600`, `:1680-1697`; `src/cli/registerCliHandlers.ts:10-18`; `src/settings/defaults.ts:150`, `:331-332`; `frontmatter.py:168`). Hinge: the fail rests on the flat parser as the vault's discharge of rule 1, and OKF itself accepts nested YAML.
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass → pass — the verifier refuted the re-judgement's residue claim, and its key set is the one recorded. Counted: the Obsidian-CLI handlers, registered unconditionally on Obsidian 1.12.2 or later. Capture always writes into `tasksFolder`; time-tracking start and stop, and pomodoro, write frontmatter into whatever task note the caller names by path, title or query. Also counted: the startup `.base` files under `TaskNotes/Views` and the first-install starter note (off the roster), completed-timer storage, the HTTP API, MCP and webhooks, ICS auto-export, and per-status auto-archive. The re-judgement held that a task note the person places on a roster path stays writable by the CLI handlers with no key to close it. The verifier found that the task resolver drops any path in `excludedFolders` before identifying a task (exact path or folder prefix; default empty), so that key closes it. Settings: `data.json` `"excludedFolders": "literature,fulltext,wiki,log,system/propagations,log.md,inbox/review-queue.md,system/bibliography.json"`; `"taskIdentificationMethod": "property"`, `"taskPropertyName": "type"` and `"taskPropertyValue": "task"`, which narrow which notes count as tasks; `"tasksFolder"` off the roster (default `"TaskNotes/Tasks"`); `"inlineTaskConvertFolder": ""`; `"pomodoroStorageLocation": "plugin"`; `"enableAPI": false`, `"enableMCP": false`, `"webhooks": []`; and `icsIntegration.enableAutoExport` `false` and each status's `autoArchive` `false` (defaults). `enableTimeblocking`, `storeTitleInFilename` and `moveArchivedTasks` no longer decide B3, because those writes follow the person's modal or edit (`src/cli/registerCliHandlers.ts:10-36`; `src/cli/helpers/taskResolution.ts:21-76`; `src/utils/TaskManager.ts:252-260`, `:694-699`; `src/utils/pathExclusions.ts:5-27`; `src/services/task-service/TaskCreationService.ts:422-439`; `src/settings/defaults.ts:252`, `:256`, `:261` @`1ba391b`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass → fail∆ — `insert-tasknote-link` and `create-inline-task` write at the cursor of the open note; `convert-to-tasknote` and `batch-convert-all-tasks` rewrite checkbox lines in it; `convert-current-note-to-task` writes task frontmatter into it through the edit modal. All are registered with no settings check, and writes into wiki/ or fulltext/ go unreported. Setting: none for the editor commands; the second pass's key set (`excludedFolders` roster list, `tasksFolder`, `inlineTaskConvertFolder`, API, MCP and webhooks off, ICS auto-export off) removes only the CLI, server and automatic legs. Verifier: confirmed (`src/commands/TranslatedCommandRegistry.ts:61-87`; `src/commands/taskNotesCommands.ts:100-134`; `src/main.ts:1325-1362`, `:1513-1590` @`1ba391b`).

**Importer (`obsidian-importer`), `B-community-3`.**

- Pin: release 3.1.12, tag commit `c44c453e73b97856e6dddceb0910cd5397323dcf`. Assets: `main.js` (5,047,456 B), `manifest.json`, `styles.css` (44,613 B).
- Licence: MIT (`LICENSE:1-3`, Obsidian). The bundled-licence block at `main.js:1001` holds long's Apache-2.0 notice and, per the verifier, only a fromcodepoint comment for sax. The verifier found sax pinned at 1.6.0, which is BlueOak-1.0.0 (npm registry and the tarball's `LICENSE.md`), whose terms need the licence text or a link to travel with the code, and `main.js` provides neither. KaTeX with mhchem, pako, turndown, protobufjs 7.6.5 (BSD-3-Clause) and `@zip.js/zip.js` 2.7.60 (BSD-3-Clause) are bundled or declared and absent from the notices.
- Writes: only when the user runs an import: notes and attachments into the output folder chosen for that run, created if missing (`src/format-importer.ts:1368-1381`); per-format default folders (`Apple Notes`, `OneNote`, `Logseq`, and so on; empty means the root); attachments to `app.json` `attachmentFolderPath` (`:91`) unless a per-importer `attachments` setting overrides it (`:89-95`). `data.json` holds `outputSettings.<importerId>` with `folder`, `attachments`, `duplicates`, `template`, and per the verifier `saveSourceId`, `idProperty`, `inlineTemplate`, `titleTemplate` (`src/plugin-data.ts:4-23`); `DuplicateHandling` update rewrites previously imported notes (`:25-29`).
- B2: desk **pass** on vault-setup, as a one-time migration of an existing corpus (`research_vault/templates/vault/AGENTS.md:17`), with the spec to decide. **Verifier: fail.** `AGENTS.md:17` defines setup-vault as create, repair or provision a vault, which does not cover migration; `skills/setup-vault/SKILL.md` has no import or migration leg; the floor's import means Zotero item to research note (comparison `:63`, `:90-92`), and its plugin list has no importer. Whether a one-time migration counts as vault-setup is open (§7.13).
- B3 pass: it never writes on its own; `outputSettings.<importerId>.folder` can be seeded outside the machine surfaces (`format-importer.ts:1305-1316`), but the user can change it on each run, so no key prevents a write into log/ or literature/. The verifier noted that the same property was used to fail Local REST API (§7.1).
- B4 fail: imported notes are plain Markdown but carry no `type` (rule 2) and many formats emit no frontmatter (rule 1); the default template is the bare content variable (`:458`), and outside the derivable folders `stamp.py:29-30` reports no-type without fixing it. The verifier refuted the desk's basis that only the CSV, Apple Notes and Airtable importers take templates: `supportsNoteTemplates` defaults to true (`:346-348`), only `FilesImporter` opts out (`src/formats/files.ts:20-22`), and every format writer routes through `writeNote`, `writePlannedNote` or `applyNoteTemplate` (`:1725-1733`, `:1772-1784`), so `inlineTemplate` or `template` can be seeded (`plugin-data.ts:10-11`). Not verified: whether such a template can add `type` without duplicating a source frontmatter block; if it can, B4 becomes configurable. Bare notes imported under inbox/ would be stamped fleeting (`structure.py:50-51`; `stamp.py:9-12`).
- Exit (desk): Stop invoking it. What it imported is ordinary Markdown and attachments already in the vault.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass unchanged, now on a named key. An import into the destination the person picks for that run is a person-chosen placement. The verifier added a judged bulk rewrite: duplicate handling defaults to Update, and the re-run index scans every Markdown file for the importer's id property. An Update re-run therefore rewrites matched notes outside the chosen destination, roster paths included. Seed key: `data.json` `outputSettings.<id>.duplicates` `"create-copy"` or `"skip"` for every importer; the files importer allows only create-copy (`src/format-importer.ts:25-29`, `:233-239`, `:1310-1326`, `:1698-1716`, `:1911-1921`). Hinge: if a bulk create into a folder chosen per run is judged, no seedable key confines that folder. Resolved 2026-10-04 by the second ruling.
- ‡ 2026-10-03 normalisation: B4 fail → pass. This answers the open question in the B4 note above. Every importer that supports templates renders the seeded inline template, and its body variable excludes the source frontmatter. A template that opens with a flat block carrying `type` therefore types every converted note without duplicating a source block. The files importer ignores templates and copies the person's bytes unchanged; the verifier moved its basis from inbox routing to that byte copy, since its destination is chosen per run. Seed keys: `data.json` `outputSettings.<id>.inlineTemplate` set to a frontmatter block with a non-empty `type` followed by the body variable, with `outputSettings.<id>.template` empty, for every id in `src/importers.ts` except files (`src/format-importer.ts:1314-1318`, `:1594-1604`, `:1725-1741`, `:1772-1825`; `src/note-template.ts:46-76`; `src/formats/files.ts:20-30`; `src/importers.ts:55-58`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass → pass — basis moved: nothing is counted. Every write follows an import the person starts from the importer dialog, where the output folder, template and duplicate handling are set before the person confirms; an Update re-run that rewrites matched notes elsewhere is a bulk change confirmed in that dialog, deliberate whatever its scope. The `importer-auth` protocol handler only completes OAuth, and it has no CLI handler, vault hook or scheduled re-sync. `outputSettings.<id>.duplicates` no longer decides B3 (`src/main.ts:59-71`; `src/format-importer.ts:233-239`, `:1257-1275`; `src/importer-flow.ts:1074` @`c44c453`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass → fail∆ — a destination chosen in the dialog no longer exempts the write. Every import writes notes and attachments into the output folder chosen for that run, created if missing, roster included; saved `outputSettings` set only its initial value. With the seeded type-bearing `inlineTemplate`, a note lands typed in wiki/ (which derives no folder type, so any non-empty type passes) or fulltext/ and goes unreported; in log/ a non-`daily` type is reported. The files importer copies bytes unchanged, and attachments are non-`.md` files nothing reads. An Update re-run (the default duplicate handling) rewrites previously imported notes wherever they are. Setting: none; `outputSettings.<id>.duplicates` `create-copy` or `skip` removes only the Update rewrite. Verifier: confirmed (`src/format-importer.ts:233-239`, `:1308-1327`, `:1366-1383`; `src/formats/files.ts:18-31` @`c44c453`; `research_vault/structure.py:28-32`, `:54-67`, `:74-111`).

### 4.4 Author's vault seed, `B-community-3`

**Homepage (`homepage`).**

- Pin: release 4.5.0, tag commit `e80151e0fbb3667c5dcb8bff2c82a62101b759bd`. Assets: `main.js` (59,450 B), `manifest.json`, `styles.css` (3,685 B). Installed in the author's vault at 4.4.2.
- Licence: MIT (`LICENSE.md:1-3`, Obsidian and novov). Bundles obsidian-daily-notes-interface and obsidian-journals-api (MIT; lock: 13 MIT, 1 0BSD); no notices in `main.js`; no LICENSE in the release.
- Writes: `data.json` `homepages['Main Homepage']` (`src/homepage.ts:10,93`; desk `src/settings.ts:24-39`, verifier `:24-40`; defaults kind File, value Home, `autoCreate` false, `commands` []). Daily Note and the other periodic kinds create the period's note through daily-notes or periodic-notes (`src/periodic.ts:70-73`; the verifier added `createPeriodicNote` at `:82`). New note kind creates a note on every launch (`homepage.ts:309-312`). File kind with `autoCreate` true creates an empty note (`:203-208`). After opening, if the file changed on disk during the open, it writes the pre-open cached content back (`:217-218`, `cachedRead` against `read`, then `vault.modify`). It runs configured commands (`:118-125`).
- B2 fail: navigation only; the nearest use is opening `index.md` or a seeded `system/bases/*.base` at startup; a Daily Note kind would make it a daily-log writer into the CLI-written log/ (vault `AGENTS.md:7`). The verifier added that its Workspace kind (`homepage.ts:51`) opens one fixed workspace at startup, adjacent to the floor's workspace switching (comparison `:64-65`) without serving it.
- B3 named keys: `homepages['Main Homepage']` with `kind` `"File"` (or `"Workspace"` or `"Nothing"`), `autoCreate: false`, `value` set to a note outside the machine surfaces, and `commands: []`. Caveat from the write-back at `:217-218`: `value` cannot be a machine-written file such as `wiki/index.md` or `log.md` without risking that a CLI write landing during the open is reverted.
- B4 pass: with `autoCreate` false and kind not New note it writes no note content; either creation path writes an empty file (rule 1).
- Author's vault: value `home`, `openOnStartup` true, `autoCreate` false (§2.2).
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass unchanged. Its judged writes all happen on open: autoCreate's empty note, the New note kind at launch, and the cached-content write-back. Seed keys: `data.json` `homepages["Main Homepage"]` with `kind` `"File"` (or `"Workspace"` or `"Nothing"`), `value` a full path off the roster (not `wiki/index.md` or root `log.md`; it resolves through `getFirstLinkpathDest`), `autoCreate` `false`, and `commands` `[]` (`src/homepage.ts:118-125`, `:200-218`, `:309-312`; `src/settings.ts:24-40`). B4 pass unchanged, with the keys corrected by the verifier. A `kind` other than New note still allows the periodic kinds, which create the period note through daily-notes or Periodic Notes with whatever template those plugins have (`src/periodic.ts:60-86`). The B3 key set (kind File, Workspace or Nothing; autoCreate false) leaves only the write-back, which adds no syntax.
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass → pass — basis widened, keys unchanged. Counted: startup through the patched opening behaviour (`openOnStartup`, default true), layout-change with `openWhenEmpty`, and the `homepage` CLI handler, now ruled remote, all through `open()`. The verifier added two triggers that bypass `open()` and call `computeValue()` directly, layout-change with `alwaysApply` and the `homepage:read` CLI handler; there only the New note kind creates a file and the periodic kinds create a period note. Settings: `data.json` `homepages["Main Homepage"]` with `"kind": "File"` (or `"Workspace"` or `"Nothing"`), `"value"` a note off the roster (not `wiki/index.md` or root `log.md`), `"autoCreate": false` and `"commands": []`. The kind rules out both direct paths and leaves only the write-back into the seeded note (`src/homepage.ts:199-218`, `:296-323`; `src/main.ts:60-78`, `:91-94`, `:197-206` @`e80151e`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass → pass — basis restated under the third ruling. The seed removes: the New note kind's `createNewFile` (launch, layout-change, the `homepage` and `homepage:read` CLI handlers); autoCreate's `vault.create` of a missing value; the Daily, Weekly, Monthly, Yearly and Journal kinds' period-note creation; the Random and Random-in-folder kinds, whose write-back can land in any file, wiki/ included; and configured commands run on open. What remains is `launchNote` writing the pre-open cached content back into the value note on every trigger that opens it, and that note is off the roster. Setting: `data.json` `homepages["Main Homepage"]` with `"kind": "File"` (or `"Workspace"` or `"Nothing"`), `"value"` a full vault path off the roster (not the default `"Home"`, `wiki/index.md` or root `log.md`), `"autoCreate": false` and `"commands": []`. Verifier condition: in 1.13.7 `getLinkpathDest` falls back to any path ending in the value when no file sits at the exact path, unless the value starts with `/`, so `value` confines only if the seed ships the note at that exact path or the value begins with `/`; with autoCreate false a miss throws before any write. Hinge: the Set to active file command rewrites `value` and can re-point the write-back at an open machine-surface note; `B-rest` read that as a settings change under the second pass's Remotely Save reading, which `B-top20`'s reading replaces (§2A Third ruling). The verifier found the hinge does not decide the cell at `"kind": "Workspace"`, where open goes to `launchWorkspace` and no trigger reaches `launchNote`, or at `"Nothing"`, where the command is unavailable (`src/main.ts:32-78`, `:91-95`, `:197-213`; `src/homepage.ts:70`, `:98-139`, `:199-222`, `:296-348`, `:403-415`; `src/settings.ts:24-40` @`e80151e`; Obsidian 1.13.7 `app.js:1@byte 1744500`).

**Local REST API with MCP (`obsidian-local-rest-api`).**

- Pin: release 5.3.1, tag commit `17a9cfd9ff5dd0156b694bf9b13ab36c786b29da`. Assets: `main.js` (4,209,760 B), `manifest.json`, `styles.css` (4,830 B). Installed in the author's vault at 4.1.2.
- Licence: MIT (`LICENSE:1-3`, Adam Coddington). The bundled-licence block at `main.js:100728` carries MIT notices for express and its middleware, `@modelcontextprotocol/server`, object-assign and safe-buffer. It also bundles node-forge (lockfile licence BSD-3-Clause OR GPL-2.0, `package-lock.json:8283-8288`, a devDependency per `package.json:31`, present as `forge.pki` in `main.js`) whose BSD-3 notice is missing from the block (verifier).
- Writes: on first load, a generated `apiKey` and `crypto` (TLS certificate and private key) into `data.json` (`src/main.ts:73-87`). On any authenticated HTTP or MCP request it writes, patches, deletes or renames any vault path (desk `src/requestHandler.ts:2765-2770`, verifier `:2765-2772`; PUT, PATCH, POST, DELETE and MOVE on `/vault/*`). The verifier added that `POST /commands/:commandId` runs any Obsidian command, including other plugins' writers (`:2784`), and that signed URLs are on by default (`src/constants.ts:36`), so a PUT works without the bearer header.
- B2 fail: the step `search` is PRISMA-S literature search (assembly spec `:143`), and the agent already reads the vault from the filesystem.
- B3 fail: its settings have no path scope and no read-only switch (`src/types.ts:58-80`; `constants.ts:32-37`). The only stop is `enableSecureServer: false` with `enableInsecureServer: false` (`main.ts:143`, `:171`), which starts no server. The verifier noted that on a literal reading this satisfies can-be-configured-not-to with nothing left running, and left that to the spec.
- B4 pass: it writes what clients send and adds no syntax.
- B5 pass: MIT with the licence added. The worker's note: a seeded `data.json` never carries `apiKey` or `crypto`, which are generated per vault.
- ‡ 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 fail → pass. Every judged write is a server write: HTTP or MCP on `/vault/*` and `/active/*`, plus `/commands`, with MCP mounted on the same express app. With both servers off nothing listens, and the remaining writes are `apiKey`, `crypto` and certificate renewal in its own `data.json`. Seed keys: `data.json` `"enableSecureServer": false` (set explicitly; an absent key means on) and `"enableInsecureServer": false` (default) (`src/main.ts:74-103`, `:143`, `:171`; `src/constants.ts:35`; `src/requestHandler.ts:2675`, `:2756-2784`). Hinge: this configuration confines by switching the plugin's function off, and the verifier asked that one ruling decide it together with Pandoc Plugin B3 and B4. B4 pass unchanged and depends on the same keys. Resolved 2026-10-04 by the second ruling.
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass → fail — counted: remote writes. Any authenticated or signed-URL HTTP or MCP request can write, patch, delete or rename any vault path through `/vault/*` and `/active/*`, and run any command through `/commands/:commandId`; the secure server starts on load unless its key is explicitly false. No setting confines it. The settings have no path scope and no read-only switch, and `data.json` `"enableSecureServer": false` with `"enableInsecureServer": false` starts no server and leaves the plugin no function beyond its own key, certificate and settings tab, which the ruling treats as disabling, not confining. The ruling kept only the output-outside-the-vault half of the first pass's disabling-or-exiling reading. Its vault and metadata listeners only invalidate a backlinks cache. Hinge: if a key that leaves the plugin loaded but idle counted as confining, the cell would be pass (`src/main.ts:74-103`, `:143`, `:171`; `src/requestHandler.ts:2757-2784`; `src/types.ts:58-80`; `src/vaultOperations.ts:108-121` @`17a9cfd`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 fail‡§ → fail‡§ — basis unchanged, paths widened by the verifier. Remote routes: authenticated or signed-URL HTTP and MCP PUT, PATCH, POST, DELETE and MOVE on `/vault/*` and `/active/*`, plus POST `/commands/:commandId`, which runs any command, other plugins' writers included; the verifier added COPY on `/vault/*`, which copies to a Destination-header path with optional overwrite. Undetected in wiki/, root `log.md`, fulltext/, and as appends to log/ files and the review queue; literature/ writes are seen by evidence-layer. Setting: no path-scoping key; `data.json` `"enableSecureServer": false` with `"enableInsecureServer": false` starts no server and so removes every path, but leaves the plugin no function, which the standing reading counts as disabling (§7.55). Hinge: the cell would pass on those two keys if a loaded-but-idle setting counted as confining (`src/main.ts:74-103`, `:137-171`; `src/constants.ts:32-37`; `src/requestHandler.ts:2013-2092`, `:2630-2675`, `:2756-2797` @`17a9cfd`; `research_vault/lints.py:727-875`).

Its fences render as plain code blocks without the plugin.
- B5 pass: Apache-2.0 permits redistribution; the vendored copy needs LICENSE and NOTICE added (Apache §4(d)).
