- ‡ 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 needs-live → fail. The re-judgement kept needs-live. The verifier refuted it, and its result is recorded. The cell counts the spawned agent's own-tool writes as judged, since the person does not choose those files. The plugin advertises terminal support, hard-coded on, and spawns whatever command the agent sends, from the vault as working directory, with no gate or key. Shell writes into literature/ or wiki/ therefore stay unconfined whether or not the guard loads under ACP, and the guard matches only Edit, Write and NotebookEdit. The §6.2 live leg no longer decides this cell. `autoAllowPermissions` `false` gives per-call approval, not a path scope. The plugin's own writes stay confined by `exportSettings.autoExportOnNewChat` and `exportSettings.autoExportOnCloseChat` `false`, `exportSettings.defaultFolder` off the roster, and `imageLocation` and `audioLocation` `"obsidian"` with `app.json` `attachmentFolderPath` `"/"` (`src/acp/acp-client.ts:432-436`; `src/acp/acp-handler.ts:206-221`; `src/acp/terminal-handler.ts:53-124`; `src/plugin.ts:994-999`, `:1042-1060`; `hooks/hooks.json:6`). Hinge: if only plugin-executed writes are judged, the cell would be needs-live on a different leg: whether the ACP adapter routes shell commands through the plugin's terminal. Resolved 2026-10-04 by the second ruling.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B4 fail unchanged. The re-judgement moved it to pass by sending exports to `exportSettings.defaultFolder` `"inbox/agent-client"`. The verifier refuted that, and its fail is recorded: every export writes a parseable block without `type`, a rule-2 shape. The register row `okf-conformance.md:36` declines only rule 1, and stamp-type refuses such a block (`stamp.py:9-18`). `structure.py:78-79` skips both rules for inbox/, a code-versus-register drift that does not license the shape. `frontmatterTag` only adds a tags line (`src/services/chat-exporter.ts:102`, `:289-298`; `src/services/settings-normalizer.ts:390-392`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 fail → fail — the hinge above is settled: the spawned agent's requests are not made by the person, so its file writes and terminal commands are counted. Terminal support is hard-coded on, and `createTerminal` runs whatever command the agent sends from the vault, with no gate or path scope. A permission prompt appears only when the agent chooses to send one, so `"autoAllowPermissions": false` confines only those requests. Also counted, with no off key: on render it injects an id into any note holding a persist agent-client fence that lacks one. Its own exports stay confined by `exportSettings.autoExportOnNewChat` and `exportSettings.autoExportOnCloseChat` `false` and `exportSettings.defaultFolder` off the roster (`src/acp/acp-client.ts:436`; `src/acp/acp-handler.ts:206-221`; `src/acp/terminal-handler.ts:53-124`; `src/acp/permission-handler.ts:77-110`; `src/plugin.ts:994-999`, `:1028-1060` @`7dc0bfa`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 fail‡ → fail‡ — basis widened. Failing paths: (1) the spawned agent's terminal commands: terminal capability is hard-coded on, and `createTerminal` runs whatever the agent sends, with no gate or path scope (its cwd is the agent-supplied `params.cwd`, else Obsidian's process cwd); (2) on render, `vault.process` injects an id into any note that holds a persist agent-client fence without one, wiki/ pages included, and no key turns it off; (3) chat exports, confined by `exportSettings.autoExportOnNewChat` false, `exportSettings.autoExportOnCloseChat` false and `exportSettings.defaultFolder` off the roster. Nothing confines (1) or (2), and no research-vault check sees them in wiki/. Verifier: confirmed, with a cite fix: the id splice is at `src/plugin.ts:1079-1085` (`src/acp/acp-client.ts:427-444`; `src/acp/terminal-handler.ts:53-124`; `src/plugin.ts:985-1000`, `:1028-1086` @`7dc0bfa`).

### 4.5 Search finds, `B-search-new`

**Longform (`longform`).**

- Pin: release 2.1.0 (2025-03-19), lightweight tag on commit `223fc21d007042bdc88278680364bbb15faed582`; `main@b7f3985` has 2.1.0, so the store installs this release; `community-plugins.json:1522 @e76f2c3`. Assets: `main.js` (1,239,095 B), `manifest.json`, `styles.css`. 188,742 downloads; reviewed listing.
- Licence: `LICENSE.md @223fc21` is the Fuck Around and Find Out License v0.2 (FAFO); `package.json:14` points to it; GitHub reports NOASSERTION; not an OSI licence. Copyright grant `:20-22`; an Ethics condition decided by the primary contributors (`:26-27`); an Excuse clause ending the licence if a written notice of non-compliance is not cured within 30 days (`:31-35`), beside a statement that no contributor can revoke it (`:45`). The licence requires no notice. Notices in `main.js`: tslib 0BSD (`:11`), lodash MIT (`:3340-3346`), Sortable MIT (`:23046`); Svelte (MIT, `:3129`) and `@popperjs/core` (MIT, `:31157`) are bundled with no notice.
- Writes: (1) index-note frontmatter, a nested `longform:` mapping (`format`, `title`, `sceneFolder`, a list-of-lists `scenes`, `ignoredFiles`, optional `workflow` and `sceneTemplate`) through `processFrontMatter` (`src/model/draft-utils.ts:69-92`, `:187-205`); a new project starts as an empty file holding only that block, and the project modal refuses an existing path (desk `new-project-modal/index.ts:33-75`; verifier `:34-40`, `:76`); (2) scene `.md` files in `sceneFolder`, empty unless a scene template is set (`src/model/note-utils.ts:16-70`); (3) the compiled manuscript at the write-to-note target, relative to the project (default `manuscript.md`; `../` allowed; `src/compile/steps/write-to-note.ts:17-23`, `:66-125`; verifier `:90-128`), which overwrites an existing target silently through `adapter.write` (`:73`, verifier); (4) an automatic write-back of index frontmatter when scenes disappear (`src/model/store-vault-sync.ts:171-175`, `:562-570`), and, per the verifier, a second automatic `processFrontMatter` on any index whose scenes array reads empty (`:469`); (5) scene renames through `fileManager.renameFile` (`SceneList.svelte:244`); (6) word-count sessions in `data.json` by default, `sessions.json` in the plugin folder under `plugin-folder`, or `sessionFile` in the vault under `file` (`src/main.ts:314-340`). Verifier, other writers: `new-draft-modal/index.ts:62`, `:75` (copies scenes, creates another untyped index); `ExplorerPane.ts:199` (`vault.trash`); `commands/templates.ts:66` (insert into an existing note); `migration.ts:96`, `:137` (inert with a seeded `projects: {}`). `sceneTemplate` is applied only when core Templates or Templater is enabled (`note-utils.ts:26-32`).
- B2: long-form, through manuscript assembly from an ordered scene list; also the floor's own drafting tool, shipped in the starter vault (comparison `:66`, `:150-167`).
- B3 named keys: `data.json` `sessionStorage` `"data"` (or `"plugin-folder"`), never `"file"` with a `sessionFile` under a machine surface (`src/model/types.ts:117`); each workflow's write-to-note target project-relative with no `../` into literature/, log/, system/ or wiki/ (`src/compile/index.ts:296-335`). Its automatic writers touch only notes that carry `longform:`. A scene rename's reach into other notes depends on `app.json` `alwaysUpdateLinks` (needs-live). Collision: if `projects/<name>/draft.md` is made the index, Longform rewrites the project note that publish reads. The verifier added that `sessionStorage` `"data"` rewrites the seeded `data.json` on every session.
- B4 fail, on two independent grounds the verifier confirmed: (1) a project created in the plugin starts as an empty file plus `longform:`, with no `type` (OKF §11 rule 2), and no setting changes it; (2) the nested `longform:` map is valid OKF YAML (`SPEC.md@ad30107:740-741`), but the vault discharges rule 1 with a flat-only parser that raises on nested maps (`research_vault/frontmatter.py:166-169`), so verify's okf-frontmatter check reports the index unparseable and the commit surface closes on it (`verify.py:54-63`, `:752-757`). Ground 2 is a vault rule, not an OKF rule; ground 1 alone decides the fail. The desk also listed: scenes empty unless `sceneTemplate` names a typed template, and stamp-type cannot type a projects/ sibling (`skills/project-flow/SKILL.md:48`); the Default Workflow strips frontmatter and writes `manuscript.md` without any (`src/compile/index.ts:302-305`), which an add-frontmatter step carrying `type` fixes. Scenes and manuscript stay readable Markdown, and the scene order is readable YAML.
- B5 pass: redistribution is inside the copyright grant; the grant is conditional (Ethics, Excuse); a vendored copy would need Svelte and Popper notices added.
- Exit (desk): Stop invoking it; scenes and manuscript.md stay ordinary Markdown, and the scene order survives as a nested longform: YAML list in each index note, which the vault's flat parser rejects until the block is removed.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass unchanged. Seed keys: `data.json` `"sessionStorage": "data"` (default) or `"plugin-folder"`; each seeded workflow's write-to-note target project-relative, with no `../` reaching a roster path; `app.json` `"alwaysUpdateLinks": false` (default) (`src/model/types.ts:117`; `src/compile/steps/write-to-note.ts:17-23`, `:90-128`; `src/model/store-vault-sync.ts:171-175`, `:469`, `:562-570`). The verifier closed the scene-rename live check (§6.1, §6.3) at desk: the rename runs Obsidian's link updater, which opens the confirmation modal unless `alwaysUpdateLinks` is true (`src/view/explorer/SceneList.svelte:244`; obsidian 1.13.7 `app.js:1@byte 1777261`, `@byte 1779273`). Cross-row inconsistency, recorded and not resolved: this cell counts that modal as confining the link update, while Files and Note composer B3 (§4.1) and Auto Note Mover B3 (§4.6) do not. Resolved 2026-10-04 by the second ruling.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B4 fail unchanged, on rule 2 alone. A project created in the plugin carries only the nested `longform:` block and no `type`, and no key adds one. The flat-parser ground is a vault rule and secondary (`src/view/project-lifecycle/new-project-modal/index.ts:34-76`; `src/model/draft-utils.ts:69-92`, `:187-205`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass → fail — the re-judgement kept pass; the verifier refuted it, and its result is recorded. Counted: on load, `discoverDrafts` writes dirty drafts back to their index files; the vault create, delete and rename hooks and the metadata-changed hook, which react to any writer (sync, a git pull, the CLI, another plugin), update the drafts store, whose change handler rewrites each changed index through `processFrontMatter`; and a second automatic `processFrontMatter` fires on an index whose scenes array reads empty. The target set is every note carrying a nested `longform:` map, with no folder bound. Once the person makes an index under wiki/ or literature/, a file another writer creates in its scene folder therefore makes Longform rewrite that machine-surface note by itself. No setting stops or scopes the sync; `waitForSync` and `fallbackWait` only delay initialisation. The re-judgement passed it on a carve-out the ruling does not state, that the targets are notes the person made; this pass counted the same shape for Quadro and Agent Client. Still confined: `data.json` `"sessionStorage": "data"` (default) or `"plugin-folder"` keeps the event-driven session write in the config dir, and scene rename, compile and project creation are the person's commands, the rename's link update going through the confirm modal. Hinge: if by-itself rewrites of notes the person turned into the plugin's own were exempt, the cell would be pass, and Quadro's fail would rest on its trash patch reaching notes from any writer (`src/main.ts:447-480`; `src/model/store-vault-sync.ts:80-155`, `:157-175`, `:236-268`, `:415-421`, `:469`, `:562-570`; `src/model/types.ts:81-132` @`223fc21`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 fail§ → fail§ — basis widened beyond the by-itself rewrite. Failing paths: (1) scene rename from SceneList goes through `fileManager.renameFile`, whose link update rewrites links in wiki/ pages, at once or after the confirm modal; (2) scene trash (`vault.trash`) and scene and draft creation inside a project placed under wiki/, where no key bounds project placement; (3) compile's write-to-note silently overwrites its target when that target reaches a roster path through `../` or lands in a wiki/ project; and, added by the verifier, (4) the Insert multi-scene and single-scene frontmatter commands, which add a nested `longform:` block to any active note through `processFrontMatter`. By-itself index rewrites are seen wherever the index sits on the roster except root `log.md`: the flat parser rejects the nested map, so okf-frontmatter closes the commit gate (evidence-layer too in literature/), but verify skips every `log.md` and `check_reserved` falls back to the whole text on a parse error, so a root `log.md` index is reported by nothing (a probe returned okf-structure matched). A new empty scene in a wiki/ project is seen as missing `type` unless a `sceneTemplate` types it; trash and the rename link update stay unseen. Setting: none for (1), (2) or (4); partial: each workflow's write-to-note target project-relative with no `../`, and `sessionStorage` `"data"` or `"plugin-folder"`. Hinge: if Obsidian's link update were not counted, the fail would still stand on scene trash in a wiki/ project and on a root `log.md` index (`src/view/explorer/SceneList.svelte:244`; `src/view/explorer/ExplorerPane.ts:199`; `src/compile/steps/write-to-note.ts:40-128`; `src/model/store-vault-sync.ts:157-175`; `src/commands/templates.ts:66-80`; `src/model/draft-utils.ts:187-205` @`223fc21`, FAFO licence, paraphrased; Obsidian 1.13.7 `app.js:1@byte 1777261`, `@byte 1779273`; `research_vault/verify.py:752-757`; `structure.py:91-100`, `:117-123`; `frontmatter.py:166-169`).

**Advanced URI (`obsidian-advanced-uri`).**

- Pin: release 2.2.0 (2026-09-23), annotated tag `3859509` to commit `2f9b9a72f50c8d21182e890044711b6016536ea4` (also `master` HEAD); `community-plugins.json:892`. `main.js` 134,901 B. 665,193 downloads.

With `useUID` true, copying a URI writes an `id: <uuid>` line into the target, creating a frontmatter block if none exists (`src/tools.ts:42-68`, `:106-116`). Settings in `data.json` (`main.ts:941`). The switch needs core Workspaces enabled (desk `handlers.ts:164-168`, verifier `:163-168`; it calls `getEnabledPluginById("workspaces")`), and the seed `gitignore:2` pattern also matches `workspaces.json`.
- B3: desk **pass**, with the named key `data.json` `useUID: false` (default, `src/constants.ts:9`) and URI writes treated as caller-directed hand edits that capture regenerates or a finding catches (vault `AGENTS.md:28`). **Verifier: fail.** `useUID: false` does not stop UID writes (`uid=` path above); the write modes put caller text into any path, machine surfaces included, and any external process or link can reach them through the protocol handlers; `daily=true` writes into the core daily-notes folder; no key restricts any target, so the can-be-configured-not-to limb cannot be met. Writes from external callers to non-open paths are not hand edits.
- B4 pass†: result stands, basis refuted. The desk's premise that nothing plugin-originated lands in notes under `useUID: false` is false, since `uid=` URIs insert an `id:` line regardless and, on a note with no frontmatter, create a block with no `type` (`tools.ts:51-54`, `:62`; rule 2). It stands because the only plugin syntax is a flat `id:` scalar and everything else is the caller's text. `processFrontMatter`-based URIs re-serialise the whole block (effect on inline maps is needs-live).
- B5 pass: MIT; notices for uuid, obsidian-community-lib, feather-icons and the copied daily-notes code would need adding.
- Exit (desk): Stop invoking it; anything URIs wrote is ordinary note text, and the only plugin-originated residue is an id: frontmatter line wherever useUID was on.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 fail† unchanged. URI writes are judged remote writes, and the unconditional handlers let any caller write to any path and write a uid whatever `useUID` says; no key restricts a target (`src/main.ts:244-256`; `src/handlers.ts:433-501`, `:555-558`). B4 pass† unchanged. Seed keys: `data.json` `"useUID": false` (default), and core `daily-notes.json` `template` pointing at a typed template, or core Daily notes off (`src/constants.ts:9`; `src/main.ts:359-379`). The verifier resolved the record's processFrontMatter caveat at desk. 1.13.7's `processFrontMatter` re-stringifies the whole block (`app.js:1@byte 1790234`, `@byte 1791784`), so an inline `generated: {by, at}` map comes back block-style (yaml 2.9.0 used as a proxy). That is valid YAML under §11, but the vault's flat parser rejects it, which is a vault-side finding (§6.3).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 fail† → fail† — basis unchanged. Remote route, any caller, through the `advanced-uri`, `adv-uri` and `hook-get-advanced-uri` protocol handlers, registered unconditionally: the write, overwrite, append, prepend and new modes put caller text at any vault path; `frontmatterkey` and `data` rewrite a field through `processFrontMatter`; `uid=` writes an id line whatever `useUID` says; `daily=true` creates the core daily note. Unseen in wiki/, in root `log.md` that keeps its shape, in fulltext/, and as appends to log/ files and the review queue. Setting: none. The desk's earlier basis that capture regenerates URI writes away is superseded 2026-10-04, third ruling. Verifier: confirmed (`src/main.ts:244-268`; `src/handlers.ts:433-501`, `:550-558` @`2f9b9a7`).

- Pin: release 0.2.0 (2026-09-12), tag commit `0eaeb9a142131b2386dd5bf31907bebef03290f6`; `master@d84b856` has 0.2.0; `community-plugins.json:717`. `main.js` 96,253 B. 145,787 downloads; maintained (pushed 2026-10-02).

- Writes: on command, into the active note only: `[^n]` references and definitions, plus lint rewrites (reindex, move to bottom, punctuation). Lint on save is off unless `lintOnSave` is true (`src/settings.ts:80`; `src/linting/linter.ts:503`); lint on creation off by default (`:81`). The prefix command writes a `footnote-prefix` property through `processFrontMatter` (desk `src/commands/set-footnote-prefix.ts:43-55`; verifier `:50-56`) and pins its type in the vault's types config (`main.ts:225-232`; `set-footnote-prefix.ts:77`). Settings in `data.json` (`main.ts:282`).
- B2: long-form, on a claim basis (`manifest.json:6`). The desk also named the floor's footnote-cited prose; the search-new verifier found the floor half unsupported because the comparison doc does not mention footnotes. Cross-family fact recorded without resolution: `B-core-1` found that the floor's starter vault ships `obsidian-footnotes` (`a3d22d0a .obsidian/community-plugins.json:4`) and pins a footnote-insert command (`.obsidian/command-palette.json:4`).
- B3 pass†: desk named `data.json` `lintOnSave: false`, `lintOnFootnoteCreation: false`, `enableFootnotePrefix: false` (defaults; `settings.ts:64,80-81`), after which it writes only into the note being edited, whose long-form target `projects/<name>/draft.md` is not a machine surface. The verifier refuted part of the basis: Set footnote prefix is registered unconditionally (`main.ts:154-173`), so `enableFootnotePrefix: false` does not prevent its frontmatter write or the type pin. The result stands because every note write targets the active note. It added that a seed needs `settingsVersion: 2`, or `loadSettings` rewrites `data.json` on first load (`main.ts:265-277`).
- B4 pass: `[^n]` is Pandoc and GFM footnote syntax (OKF `SPEC.md@ad30107:209-213`); the optional property is a flat scalar. Needs-live risk: `processFrontMatter` re-serialises the whole block, and `draft.md`'s inline `generated: {by, at}` map may come back as a nested block the vault parser rejects (`frontmatter.py:166-169`); this applies whatever `enableFootnotePrefix` says.
- Exit (desk): Stop invoking it; footnotes stay standard [^n] Markdown footnotes, plus a footnote-prefix property wherever the prefix feature was used.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass† and B4 pass unchanged, with no key required. Every note write goes into the active note through a command or the person's own save, a chosen-file edit. `"settingsVersion": 2` only stops a config-dir rewrite of `data.json` (`src/linting/linter.ts:488-508`; `src/main.ts:154-173`, `:265-282`; `src/commands/set-footnote-prefix.ts:48-56`). The verifier resolved the B4 live risk (§6.1, §6.3) at desk. 1.13.7's `processFrontMatter` re-stringifies the whole block, so on `projects/<name>/draft.md` the inline `generated: {by, at}` map comes back block-style (yaml 2.9.0 proxy). That is valid YAML with `type` kept, so §11 holds, but the vault's flat parser would report it unparseable, which is a vault-side finding (obsidian 1.13.7 `app.js:1@byte 1790234`, `@byte 1791784`; `system/templates/project.md:5`).
- ◊ 2026-10-04 live: B1 pass — release 0.2.0. The catalog repository `michabrugger/obsidian-footnotes` serves its assets from `github.com/Comprehensive-Jason/obsidian-footnotes/releases/download/0.2.0/` (§7.40); `main.js` sha256 `17e4a968…` equals that asset's GitHub digest. minAppVersion 1.13.0. `enablePluginAndSave` resolved true; loaded with 7 footnote commands; no error. On load it wrote `data.json` (638 B, sha256 `f28469b6…`). Set footnote prefix (§6.1) was not run (`/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/plugin-obsidian-footnotes.log`; `/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/step2-release-assets.log`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass† → fail†∆ — the insert-autonumbered, insert-named, insert-inline and paste-inline commands and rename-footnote (command and menu entry) write into the active note, and set-footnote-prefix writes a `footnote-prefix` property through `processFrontMatter` whatever `enableFootnotePrefix` says. In an open wiki/ page, fulltext/ note or root `log.md` none of these is seen (`[^n]` text matches no captured-set pattern); in literature/ evidence-layer sees a body change or the re-serialised block. Setting: none for the insert, rename and prefix commands. Verifier correction, result unchanged: lint-footnotes is removable by keys; it returns before any write when `lintFixPunctuation`, `lintFixLazyDefinitions`, `lintMoveToBottom` and `lintReindex` are false (defaults true), `lintDeleteOrphanedReferences`, `lintDeleteOrphanedDefinitions` and `lintMergeDuplicateDefinitions` are false (defaults) and `enableFootnotePrefix` is false; `"lintOnSave": false` and `"lintOnFootnoteCreation": false` (defaults) remove the save-time and creation-time lint (`src/main.ts:99-202`; `src/settings.ts:55-82`; `src/linting/linter.ts:349-364` @`0eaeb9a`).
- ◇ 2026-10-04 fourth ruling: B3 fail†∆ → pass†∆◇ — every writer resolves its target from the active Markdown view: the insert, paste-inline and rename-footnote commands write the open note's body, rename-footnote's menu entry is an editor-menu entry, and set-footnote-prefix writes the open note's own frontmatter through `processFrontMatter`; all are open-note editing and are not judged. The property-type pin goes to the config directory. Counted: the automatic save-time and creation-time lint, which touch only the note being edited. Setting: `data.json` `"lintOnSave": false`, `"lintOnFootnoteCreation": false` (both defaults); `"settingsVersion": 2` only avoids a config-directory rewrite. No file-menu, protocol or CLI route reaches another note. Verifier: confirmed (`src/main.ts:99-202`, `:206-240`; `src/commands/set-footnote-prefix.ts:40-56`; `src/linting/linter.ts:488-508`, `:542-550`; `src/settings.ts:55-82` @`0eaeb9a`).

**Periodic Notes (`periodic-notes`).**

- Pin: release 0.0.17 (2021-09-16), tag commit `5b96e4f14130b0c024edb5ddb7871d01d61d9522`; the store version follows the default-branch manifest (`main@a8aa7e4`: 0.0.17); last push 2024-08-23 (dormant); `community-plugins.json:598`. `main.js` 180,567 B. 771,646 downloads.
- Licence: MIT (`LICENSE:1-3`, Liam Cain). `main.js` carries no licence notice at all; it bundles obsidian-daily-notes-interface 0.9.4 (MIT; settings lookup at `:24-45`), Svelte (MIT, `:1487`) and `@popperjs/core` (MIT, `:2584`).
- Writes: a period note when the user opens today's daily (or weekly, monthly, quarterly, yearly) note and it does not exist (`src/commands.ts:71-93`), at `<folder>/<format>.md` (default format `YYYY-MM-DD`, default folder the root), with the template's dates substituted (verifier: template read and substitution at `main.js:264-293`, `vault.create` at `:273`; the desk's `:24-45` is the settings lookup). No open-at-startup in 0.0.17 (`src/index.ts:56-66`). Settings in `data.json` (`index.ts:160-164`). The bundled interface reads core daily-notes settings whenever periodic daily is not enabled (`main.js:15-19`, verifier).
- B2: daily-log, only as a human daily note outside log/, since log/ and `log.md` are machine-written (vault `AGENTS.md:7`; assembly spec `:146`, `:539`).
- B3 named keys: `data.json` `daily: {enabled: true, folder: <outside log/ and every other machine surface>, format: "", template: <typed template path>}`, and `enabled: false` for the other four periods (`src/settings/index.ts:31-35`). With `folder` `log` the default format produces `log/YYYY-MM-DD.md`, exactly the file publish appends to (`publish.py:417-429`). If core daily-notes stays enabled, its folder carries the same constraint because of the fallback.
- B4 pass: conformant only with `daily.template` pointing at a template carrying a non-empty `type`; empty, the note breaks rule 1. A folder outside literature/, log/ and inbox/ derives no type and accepts any (`structure.py:53-68`). The shipped `system/templates/daily.md` carries `type: daily` but describes itself as the log/ machine template (`daily.md:1-5`), so reusing it would mislead.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass and B4 pass unchanged. Its one judged write is the period note it creates on open, at `<folder>/<format>.md`. Seed keys: `data.json` `daily` `{"enabled": true, "folder": <a folder off the roster, not log>, "format": "", "template": <a seeded template carrying a non-empty type, not the log/ machine template>}`, with the weekly, monthly, quarterly and yearly entries `"enabled": false`. If core Daily notes stays on, its `daily-notes.json` `folder` must also be off `log/`, because of the fallback (`src/commands.ts:71-93`; `src/settings/index.ts:15-35`; release `main.js:264-293`; `publish.py:417-429`; `structure.py:54-67`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass → pass — basis moved: nothing is counted. Its single creator runs only from the open command, the ribbon click, the ribbon context menu and a modal button; `onLayoutReady` only migrates legacy weekly settings and sets up the ribbon and commands; it has no event hook or handler. The `daily.folder` key and the core `daily-notes.json` folder no longer decide B3; `daily.template` still bears on B4 (`src/commands.ts:71-93`, `:153`; `src/index.ts:56-65`, `:94-99`; `src/modal.ts:22`, `:76` @`5b96e4f`).

No error, no `data.json`, and no note created. A daily note from a seeded folder and template (§6.1) was not run (`/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/plugin-periodic-notes.log`).

Its only writer creates the period note at `<folder>/<format>.md` when absent, from the open commands, the ribbon click, the ribbon context menu and the modal button, for enabled periods only; commands for disabled periods are removed. **Pandoc Plugin (`obsidian-pandoc`).**

- Pin: release 0.4.1 (2022-09-25), annotated tag `bfefcff` to commit `68ff9e3733cc73055156455721fc736ad45c7d5e` (also `master` HEAD); last push 2024-05-15 (dormant); `community-plugins.json:1340`. `main.js` 324,162 B. 558,119 downloads.
- Licence: MIT (`LICENSE.md:1-3`, Oliver Balfour). No bundled notices; it bundles lookpath (MIT, `:53`), yaml (ISC, `:6327`), temp (MIT) and js-base64 3.7.2 (BSD-3-Clause, `:9274`). The verifier found more: `main.js` embeds `styles/app-css.ts` (around `main.js:9550`), whose source header says part of it is CSS copyrighted by the Obsidian developers and included under a permission given to the plugin author on Discord; the bundle drops that header, and the MIT licence covers only the author's code. Also bundled without notices: Prism CSS (MIT), MathJax CSS (licence not recorded), and about 13 transitive modules of temp (rimraf, glob, minimatch, mkdirp, inflight, once, wrappy, inherits, fs.realpath, path-is-absolute, brace-expansion, balanced-match, concat-map).
- B1: desktop-only; needs an external pandoc binary, and pdflatex or xelatex for PDF.
- Writes: on command only, an export named after the source with the format's extension (docx, pdf, epub, tex, html, odt, pptx, `.pandoc.md` and others; `pandoc.ts:43-57`), next to the source by default or into `outputFolder`, which takes an absolute path (`main.ts:96-99`; `global.ts:26-28`, `:46`). A metadata temp file goes to the OS temp dir (`main.ts:116-118`). It reads `<vault>/.obsidian/config` at a hard-coded path for theme detection (`renderer.ts:91-93`), ignoring a user-overridden config dir; the read is inside try/catch.
- B2: long-form manuscript output; not publish, since `skills/publish/SKILL.md` has no render step.
- B3: desk **pass**: no automatic writer; the redirect key `outputFolder` needs an absolute path, so a seed cannot carry a portable value; typical use exports `projects/<name>/draft.md`, beside the draft. **Verifier: fail.** No seedable value achieves the must; with the null default every export lands beside the open note, so exporting a literature note writes `literature/<key>.<ext>` into a machine surface, which capture does not regenerate away, and a `literature/<key>.pandoc.md` would be read by verify as a literature note (`verify.py:745-751`). These are new files, not hand-equivalent edits.
- B4: desk **pass** with a needs-live risk: the Pandoc Markdown export runs standalone with the source YAML as metadata (`pandoc.ts:46`, `:122-139`; `renderer.ts:43`), so from `draft.md` it might carry `type: project` and create a second project note (`structure.py:8-11`). **Verifier: fail.** `needsStandaloneFlag` excludes markdown output, so the export runs without `-s` (`pandoc.ts:83-89`, `:122-123`; `main.ts:115-128`, `:133-142`). Measured locally with pandoc 3.1.3 in `/tmp` on 2026-10-03: `-t markdown` without `-s` drops the YAML block entirely, so `X.pandoc.md` beside `draft.md` has no frontmatter (rule 1). With `-s`, reachable only through `extraArguments`, `type: project` comes back and the inline `generated` map is rewritten block-style, which the vault parser rejects. No seedable setting avoids both.
- B5: desk **pass** (MIT; js-base64's BSD notice to reproduce). **Verifier: fail**, on the embedded Obsidian-copyrighted CSS, for which no grant lets a third party redistribute it.
- Exit (desk): Stop invoking it; exports are standalone files (docx, pdf, epub and so on) that never feed back into notes.
- ‡ 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 fail† → pass†. The re-judgement kept fail on the ground that no portable seed value exists. The verifier refuted it, and its result is recorded: the ruled standard asks for a seedable setting that confines, not a portable one. `outputFolder` is a free-text setting that, when non-empty, becomes the export directory, and the plugin's only other write is a metadata file in the OS temp dir. Seed key: `data.json` `"outputFolder": "/tmp"`, or any absolute path outside every vault. A relative value is unsafe, because pandoc runs with the source's directory as working directory. On Windows the value resolves drive-rooted, and an absent directory is expected to fail the export without writing; this was not observed (`main.ts:96-99`, `:111`, `:116-118`; `settings.ts:100-108`; `global.ts:26-28`; `pandoc.ts:160`). Hinge: this confines by exiling the output, and the verifier asked that one ruling decide it together with Local REST API B3. Resolved 2026-10-04 by the second ruling.
- ‡ 2026-10-03 normalisation: B4 fail† → pass†. The re-judgement kept fail. The verifier refuted it, and its result is recorded; it follows from B3. With `outputFolder` outside any vault, no `X.pandoc.md` and no other file lands in the vault, so nothing the plugin writes can break conformance. The earlier grounds hold only at the null default. The flat-parser clause is a vault rule (`main.ts:96-99`; `pandoc.ts:46`, `:83-89`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass† → pass† — basis moved: nothing is counted. Exports run only from its Export-as commands on the active file, the metadata temp file goes to the OS temp dir, and it has no event hook or protocol handler. `"outputFolder"` no longer decides B3; under the ruling an absolute path outside every vault would still confine if exports were counted. B4 was not re-judged and still rests on that key (`main.ts:38-55`, `:86-118` @`68ff9e3`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass†‡ → pass†‡ — `outputFolder` decides B3 again. At the null default every Export as command writes the export beside the active note, putting files into wiki/ unseen and a non-`.md` `literature/<key>.<ext>` unseen. Setting: `data.json` `"outputFolder"` an absolute path outside every vault (for example `"/tmp"`) and `"extraArguments": ""` (default), so no pandoc flag adds an output path; every export from every Export as command then leaves the vault. Verifier: confirmed; HTML is written directly, the metadata file goes to the OS temp dir, and a grep of the tree finds no other filesystem write (`main.ts:39-55`, `:96-142`; `global.ts:26-30`, `:35-49` @`68ff9e3`).

**Enhancing Export (`obsidian-enhancing-export`).**

- Pin: release 1.11.3 (2026-08-22), annotated tag `af9ceb1` to commit `4b63d2c8798a3a140cd7dd31c136d93adf32f4f2` (also `main` HEAD); `community-plugins.json:3202`. Assets: `main.js` (188,552 B), `manifest.json`, `styles.css`, plus a misnamed `obsidian-enhancing-export-.zip`. 457,983 downloads.
- Licence: MIT (`LICENSE:1-3`, YISH). `main.js` keeps yargs-parser's ISC notices (`:5-26`); semver (ISC, `:27`) and solid-js (MIT, `:4`) have none. The verifier found three third-party files embedded as byte arrays and written into the plugin folder on load (`src/resources.ts:7-8`): `textemplate/neurips.sty` (`:1-17`, no licence statement), `textemplate/dissertation.tex` (`:1-8`, a BSD-style template by Wagler and MacFarlane whose notice travels in the bytes) and `lua/citefilter.lua` (`:1`, credits a StackExchange answer).
- B1: desktop-only; needs an external pandoc binary.
- Writes: on command, an export to the directory chosen in a dialog. `defaultExportDirectoryMode` Auto uses the last export directory or the OS Documents folder (`src/ui/ExportDialog.tsx:28`); Same uses the note's folder (`:44-46`); Custom uses `customDefaultExportDirectory` (`:47-48`). The export-with-previous command skips the dialog and reuses `lastExportDirectory` (desk `src/main.ts:50-53`; verifier `:43-56`). On load it writes its lua and textemplate resources into its own plugin folder (`main.ts:23`, `:141-150`), none of them `.md`. Settings in `data.json`.
- B2: long-form manuscript export; maintained.
- B3 named key: `data.json` `defaultExportDirectoryMode: "Auto"` (default, `src/settings.ts:137-144`), not `"Same"`; `"Custom"` takes a per-platform absolute path, not portable for a seed. Side effect: the load-time resources churn the seeded config dir.
- B4 pass†: desk said only the Markdown and Hugo targets produce `.md` (`src/export_templates.ts:21-33`). The verifier found a third, TextBundle (`text.md` inside a `.textbundle` folder, `:43-48`), and that both Markdown targets pass `-s`, so an in-vault export of `draft.md` would carry its frontmatter, `type: project` included. The result stands because the default Auto location is outside the vault.
- B5: desk **pass** (MIT; semver and solid-js notices to add). **Verifier: fail**: `neurips.sty` carries no licence statement, so permission to redistribute it is not established, and the repository's MIT licence does not cover the third-party files.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass and B4 pass† unchanged. Seed key: `data.json` `"defaultExportDirectoryMode": "Auto"` (default). Exports go to a directory the person confirms. Export-with-previous reuses a directory the person set, and Auto re-proposes the last directory, so only its first proposal is outside the vault (`src/ui/ExportDialog.tsx:28`, `:44-48`; `src/main.ts:43-56`, `:141-152`). Cite fix: the default is at `src/settings.ts:140`, not `:139`. With `-s`, pandoc re-serialises rather than copies the frontmatter (block-style maps, measured with pandoc 3.1.3). That is valid YAML that the flat parser rejects; person-directed in-vault exports of root `index.md` or `log.md` would carry no type or no block.
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass → pass — basis moved: its only automatic write is the lua and textemplate resources it releases into its own plugin folder on load. Exports run only from its commands or file-menu item, through a dialog where the person picks the location, and export-with-previous is a command the person runs. `defaultExportDirectoryMode` no longer decides B3 (`src/main.ts:21-23`, `:30-78`, `:141-152` @`4b63d2c`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass → fail∆ — a location confirmed in a dialog no longer exempts the write. The export command and the file-menu item open a dialog whose output directory is free (a typed path, or an OS folder picker that can create directories), and export-with-previous reuses `lastExportDirectory` with no dialog; either can write an export into wiki/ or literature/. Setting: none; `defaultExportDirectoryMode` sets only the dialog's first proposal. Verifier correction, result unchanged: a `.md` export into wiki/ is not reliably unseen, since pandoc re-serialises the block and a block-style list the flat parser rejects is reported by okf-frontmatter; the deciding unseen write is a non-`.md` export (docx, pdf, html) into wiki/ or literature/, since every check reads only `*.md`. The load-time lua and textemplate writes go to its own plugin folder (`src/main.ts:30-79`; `src/ui/ExportDialog.tsx:28-60` @`4b63d2c`; `research_vault/lints.py:586-601`; `verify.py:745-757`; `structure.py:185-219`).

**Novel word count (`novel-word-count`).**

- Pin: release 5.0.0 (2026-08-03), tag commit `5bfba093e619ad9492a73e3db60d0fa26c80f807` (also `master` HEAD); `community-plugins.json:2838`. `main.js` 84,094 B. 199,242 downloads.
- Licence: MIT (`LICENSE:1-3`, Isaac Lyman); no runtime dependencies; no third-party notices.
- Writes: only its own `data.json`, holding settings and, when `writeCountsToFile` is true, a per-file count cache, saved with a 5-minute debounce (`logic/saved_data.ts:30-42`); `saveData` is the only write call in the tree. It reads optional word-goal properties and writes none.
- B2: long-form progress: per-file, per-folder and vault counts in the file explorer (core Word count covers only the active note).
- B3 named key: `writeCountsToFile: false` (default, `logic/settings/settings.ts:147`), keeping the count cache out of `data.json`.
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass → pass — basis moved: its one automatic write, a debounced `saveData`, lands in its own config-dir `data.json`, so `writeCountsToFile` no longer decides B3 (`logic/saved_data.ts:30-42` @`5bfba09`).
- ◊ 2026-10-04 live: B1 needs-live¤, with a load failure observed on this host (the leg reported fail; ¤ line below) — release 5.0.0 (`main.js` sha256 `65d5f85a…`, equal to the GitHub digest); minAppVersion 0.13.31. `enablePluginAndSave` resolved false and the plugin was not loaded. The console logged a plugin failure for `novel-word-count` with RangeError "Invalid language tag: c", while `dev:errors` stayed empty. At module top level the bundle builds an `Intl.NumberFormat` from `navigator.languages` plus `en-US`, with no guard (`/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/downloads/novel-word-count/main.js:670-672`, read by the verifier). This WSL Obsidian runs with `LANG=C.UTF-8`, so `navigator.languages` is `["en-US","c"]`; the same Intl call reproduced the RangeError in `eval`, and `["en-US"]` alone did not. ¤ The list comes from Electron: on Linux, Electron 39.8.3 puts the app locale first and then the language names glib derives from the environment, drops only names that are exactly `C`, and canonicalises the rest, so `C.UTF-8` survives as `c` (§7.42). Diagnostic retry: with `navigator.languages` overridden in memory to `["en-US"]`, the plugin loaded and wrote `data.json` (1,885 B); the override was then removed and the plugin disabled. A load on a host whose language list holds only valid BCP 47 tags was not measured (§7.38). ¤ Deciding check, not run: relaunch Obsidian 1.13.7 on the same scratch vault with `LANG` unset, `LANG=C` or `LANG=en_US.UTF-8`, and with `LANGUAGE` and `LC_ALL` unset, or use a Windows or macOS desktop; then run `enablePluginAndSave` on the same 5.0.0 assets and read back the loaded state and the console (`/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/plugin-novel-word-count.log`; `/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/plugin-novel-word-count-diagnosis.log`; electron/electron @`v39.8.3` `shell/common/language_util_linux.cc:21-31` (GetPreferredLanguages), `shell/browser/api/electron_api_web_contents.cc:924-938`).
- ¤ 2026-10-04 check novel-word-count: B1 fail◊ → needs-live◊ — the load failure depends on the host's process locale, not on the plugin under a language list of valid tags. The plugin builds three `Intl.NumberFormat` objects at module top level from `navigator.languages` plus `en-US`, with no guard, so one invalid tag in the list aborts the module load. On Linux, Electron 39.8.3 builds that list from glib's environment-derived language names and drops only exact `C` entries, so this host's `LANG=C.UTF-8` leaves the invalid tag `c`; overridden to `["en-US"]`, the list an unset `LANG` or bare `C` yields, the plugin loaded. In an offline reproduction, an unset `LANG`, bare `C`, POSIX and language_COUNTRY.codeset locales without an @modifier gave lists the plugin's Intl call accepts, and Windows and macOS desktops take the list from the OS language preferences; no load on such a host was measured, and @modifier locales are open (§7.43). The ◊ stays because the observation stands; the desk fact (0.13.31; false) is unchanged. Cells moved: §3.4 B1, and in §6.5 the run-table row, the verifier summary sentence and the Not-run bullet, and §7.38; the finding is §7.42 (`isaaclyman/novel-word-count-obsidian` `logic/locale_format.ts:1-11` @`5bfba09`, MIT, `LICENSE:1-3`; electron/electron @`v39.8.3` `shell/common/language_util_linux.cc:21-31` (GetPreferredLanguages), `shell/browser/api/electron_api_web_contents.cc:924-938`; `/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/plugin-novel-word-count.log`, 01:45:11 RangeError and enable resolved false; `/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/plugin-novel-word-count-diagnosis.log`, 01:45:29 language list and `LANG`, 01:45:51 Intl reproduction, 01:45:55 loaded under the override; `/tmp/nwc-check/langs.py`).

**Focus Mode (`obsidian-focus-mode`).**

**Quadro (`quadro`).**

- Pin: release 1.29.0 (2026-07-02), tag commit `c67be32e8b26ce5ea32db378073f2162c0e0c3b2`; `main@b3c1d75` has 1.29.0; `community-plugins.json:9460`. `main.js` 196,559 B. 6,794 downloads.
- Licence: MIT (`LICENSE:1-3`, Christopher Grieser). Notices for `@kurkle/color` 0.3.4 and chart.js 4.5.1 at `main.js:49-67`; monkey-around (ISC) is inlined with no notice (imported at `src/shared/trashing-watcher.ts:1`). The skia-canvas dependency does not appear in `main.js`.
- Writes: (1) into any data file, meaning any `.md` outside the code and extraction folders (desk `src/shared/validation.ts:25-27`; verifier `:22-27`): `==highlight==` markup, a wikilink to the code or extraction and a `^id-<timestamp>` block id (`add-blockid-to-datafile.ts:13-17`, `:52-54`), and a `read: true` property through `processFrontMatter` (`progress-tracker.ts:43-45`); (2) code files in `Codes/` with `code description:` frontmatter plus `Codes/Template.md` if present (`create-new-code-file.ts:180-202`); (3) extraction files in `Extractions/<type>/` with template frontmatter plus `extraction-date` and `extraction-source` (`extract-from-paragraph.ts:68-91`); (4) `Analysis/progress.json` (`progress-tracker.ts:16-20`, `:69`); (5) a monkey-patch of `app.vault.trash`: trashing a code or extraction file strips every link to it from every linking file (`trashing-watcher.ts:14-35`, `:56`); (6) it forces vault config `useMarkdownLinks` to false (`add-blockid-to-datafile.ts:44`); (7) merges create backup folders (`file-merging.ts:33`). Verifier additions: a second `app.json` override setting `propertiesInDocument` to visible (`extraction-utils.ts:39`); a `progress.json` write whenever any data file is trashed in Obsidian (`trashing-watcher.ts:27-28`); vault-wide link rewrites on merge (`file-merging.ts:116`); a binary chart write (`theoretical-saturation.ts:139`).
- B2: scoping-review, a weak fit: structured extraction with CSV export (`export-extractions-as-csv.ts:80-87`) resembles charting, but the manifest positions it as qualitative analysis of primary data (`manifest.json:4`), and PRISMA-ScR screening is lane 4's floor (assembly spec `:144`).
- B3 fail: the data files for its step would be the vault's evidence notes in literature/ and fulltext/; coding and extraction write highlights, links and block ids into them, and no setting excludes a folder from data-file status (only `coding.folder`, `extraction.folder` and `analysis.folder` move its own outputs, `defaults.ts:34,39,47`). The trash patch is a vault-wide automatic rewriter, and the `useMarkdownLinks` write silently overrides a seeded `app.json`. Capture would regenerate the coded notes and orphan every code.
- B4 fail: code files carry only `code description:` and extraction files only template keys plus `extraction-*`, so neither has a `type` unless a vault-side `Template.md` supplies one (rule 2); the generated `Template.md` files are untyped notes too (`create-new-extraction-type.ts:70-76`). Highlights and block ids stay legible. The `[[path#^id]]` wikilinks are Obsidian form rather than OKF §6.1 markdown links, a deviation the vault registers only for claim links.
- Exit (desk): Stop invoking it; code and extraction notes stay Markdown, but the ==highlight== \[[link]\] ^id marks it wrote into data files remain as inert plain text.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 fail unchanged, on a new basis. Its coding marks are now chosen-file edits to the open note. The fail rests on two bulk rewrites no key disables. The unconditional `vault.trash` patch strips links to a trashed code or extraction file from every linking file, and a merge rewrites links in every file that points at the merged-away file. Both reach any coded literature/ or fulltext/ note. The folder keys move only Quadro's own outputs (`src/main.ts:60`; `src/shared/trashing-watcher.ts:14-74`; `src/shared/file-merging.ts:103-125`; `src/settings/defaults.ts:33-48`). B4 fail unchanged, on rule 2. The generated `Template.md` holds only a dimension key, and code files carry only a description key unless a template supplies more. Both are typeless blocks, so routing the stores under inbox/ would not help. Quadro also forces `useMarkdownLinks` false before each link it writes (`src/extraction/create-new-extraction-type.ts:69-76`; `src/coding/create-new-code-file.ts:180-202`; `src/shared/add-blockid-to-datafile.ts:44-50`; `okf-conformance.md:36`, `:41`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 fail → fail — the basis narrows to the `vault.trash` patch, installed unconditionally on load before settings load. Whenever any caller trashes a code or extraction file, it walks the resolved links and rewrites every linking note through `vault.process`, coded literature/ or fulltext/ notes included. The merge link rewrite is now a command the person runs on files they chose, coding marks are the person's edits, and its `progress.json` writes and property-type pins are off the roster. No setting confines the patch: `coding.folder`, `extraction.folder` and `analysis.folder` move only its own outputs (`src/main.ts:59-60`; `src/shared/trashing-watcher.ts:14-74`; `src/settings/defaults.ts:33-48` @`c67be32`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 fail → fail — basis widened to the coding commands and merge. Failing paths: (1) a patch of `app.vault.trash`, installed on load, rewrites every linking note through `vault.process` when any caller trashes a code or extraction file, wiki/ pages included; (2) the coding and extraction commands write `==highlight==` marks, a wikilink and a `^id` block id, plus `read: true` through `processFrontMatter`, into the open data file, any `.md` outside Quadro's own folders, wiki/ and fulltext/ included (forcing `useMarkdownLinks` false before the link); (3) the merge command rewrites links across the vault. The links it writes into wiki/ resolve, because captured-set counts every non-literature `.md` as a page, so these writes are unseen; literature/ notes are seen by evidence-layer. Setting: none; `coding.folder`, `extraction.folder` and `analysis.folder` move only Quadro's own outputs. Verifier: confirmed (`src/main.ts:59-60`; `src/shared/trashing-watcher.ts:14-74`; `src/shared/add-blockid-to-datafile.ts:13-17`, `:34-54`; `src/shared/file-merging.ts:103-125`; `src/settings/defaults.ts:33-48` @`c67be32`; `research_vault/captured.py:94-109`).

### 4.6 Floor vault, `B-floor-vault`

Nothing was installed; release assets were read under `/tmp` (at the desk read, 2026-10-03; superseded: the 2026-10-04 run copied pinned release assets, one plugin at a time, into a scratch research-vault scaffold, §6.5). Floor cites are at `a3d22d0a` (full SHA in §2.5), registry cites at `obsidian-releases@f8d2f51`, research-vault cites at `3780fb7`, and claude-obsidian cites at 2.2.0 (pin `32ac5a0`). The family cited `app.js` both ways: `app.js:1@N` is a character offset, as in §4.1, and `app.js:1@byte N` a byte offset, as in §4.2; none was renumbered. The verifier re-read every pin and found the release asset hashes as the desk gave them.

**Tag shape (verifier; applies to Auto Note Mover and Tag Wrangler).** Literature notes carry frontmatter `tags` as Zotero's list of `{tag, type}` mappings (`tests/test_literature_notes.py:447`; `research_vault/literature_notes.py:308-322`). Obsidian 1.13.7 keeps only string entries when it parses frontmatter tags (the string filter at `app.js:1@1366724`, reached through `@1367032` and `getAllTags` at `@1373846`), the same finding §4.2 settled for Tags view. Neither plugin can therefore act on a literature note through its Zotero tags; only hashtags typed into a literature note's body reach them. Their realistic machine-surface exposure is wiki/: claude-obsidian 2.2.0 requires a `tags` field on every page (`claude_obsidian/lint_engine.py:63`), written as a string list (`skills/wiki/references/frontmatter.md:16-17`).

**Auto Note Mover (`auto-note-mover`).**

- Pin: release 1.2.0 = HEAD `631c4bec47adfd45a2d83ab886dbeaaa97504977` (2022-04-16); last push 2023-10-25; no release since 2022, so unmaintained in practice; `community-plugins.json:2635`. Assets: `main.js` (sha256 `f8ca8bf6…`), `manifest.json` (`515cc7f3…`) and a zip; no `styles.css`.
- Licence: MIT (`LICENSE:1-3`, 2022 faru; `package.json:12`). The build bundles `@popperjs/core` ^2.11.2 (MIT; `package.json:24`): the release `main.js` carries its `node_modules/@popperjs/core` path comments from `:65`, and no licence, copyright or MIT string anywhere.
- Floor: 1.2.0 (`a3d22d0a .obsidian/plugins/auto-note-mover/manifest.json:4`); the floor's `main.js` and `manifest.json` are byte-identical to the release assets. Floor settings, paraphrased from `data.json:1-66`: trigger Automatic (`:2`); tag rules matched as regular expressions (`:3`); status-bar indicator on (`:4`); ten rules (`:5-56`) that send notes by type tag into the floor's numbered folders (source tags to the notes folder, analysis to the analysis folder, the five entity tags for place, event, person, institution and work to the index folder, and task, archive and diary tags each to their own folder); no title-pattern rule; excluded folders matched by exact path, not regex (`:57`), listing only the floor's Dataview and Zotero folders under `meta/` (`:58-65`). The floor's `app.json:2` sets `alwaysUpdateLinks` true. Neither the floor readme's plugin acknowledgements (`readme.md:42-47`) nor the comparison doc names it.
- Writes: moves (renames) whole note files through `app.fileManager.renameFile` (`utils/Utils.ts:53`), after checking that the destination folder exists (`:32-36`; otherwise an error notice and no move) and that no same-named file is there; it never edits note content. Triggers are vault create, metadataCache changed and vault rename for any file (`main.ts:96-100`), though the manifest description says it moves active notes. Its own `data.json`, including the Toggle Auto-Manual command's save (`main.ts:124-139`; `saveData` at `:130`, `:134`). Obsidian's link update after a move asks first unless `alwaysUpdateLinks` is true (`app.js:1@1774483`, §4.1 Files); `B-floor-config`'s verifier found that the update rewrites only links whose resolution or regenerated text changes (§8.3).
- Verifier, missed: `fileCheck` reads `fileCache.frontmatter` with no null check (`utils/Utils.ts:5`), so create events for non-Markdown or uncached files throw in the handler; nothing is written, but the live leg will see console errors.
- B2: floor. It sorts the floor's notes into its numbered folders by type tag (`a3d22d0a .obsidian/community-plugins.json:8`; `data.json:5-56`). It serves no step, because research-vault's CLI places every machine-surface note.
- B3 pass†: named key `data.json` `trigger_auto_manual` `"Manual"`. The automatic path is gated on it (`main.ts:14`; default Automatic, `settings/settings.ts:28`); after that only the Move-the-note command acts, on the active note, as a user gesture. The Toggle Auto-Manual command flips the key back and saves it (`main.ts:124-139`). Alternative in Automatic mode: `use_regex_to_check_for_excluded_folder` true with `excluded_folder` regexes covering `^(literature|fulltext|wiki|log|system)(/|$)` and `^inbox$` (the review queue is `inbox/review-queue.md`, `research_vault/structure.py:50-52`, and exclusion works per folder; in exact-match mode an exclusion of `wiki` does not cover its subfolders, `main.ts:25-39`), every `folder_tag_pattern[].folder` outside the machine surfaces, and `app.json` `alwaysUpdateLinks` false (the default) so link rewrites prompt. The per-note frontmatter opt-out (`AutoNoteMover: disable`, `utils/Utils.ts:4-11`) cannot protect literature notes, because capture owns their frontmatter. Desk example of the hazard: Automatic mode moves a CLI-written literature/ note whose Zotero tags match a rule, and the link rewrite fans into wiki/. Verifier: refuted, since Zotero tags never reach `getAllTags` (tag shape, above); the exposure is wiki/ pages with string tags and hashtags typed into literature/ bodies. The result is unchanged; the surface the hazard reaches moved. Seeded unchanged, the floor's own `data.json` (Automatic; exact-match exclusions of the floor's meta folders) is the failing configuration; in a research-vault vault none of its destination folders exists, so nothing moves until a user creates one.
- B4 pass: it moves files only, and links Obsidian rewrites stay ordinary links. Caveat: a note moved into or out of a typed folder (literature/, log/, inbox/) keeps its old `type`, a folder-type finding (`docs/agents/okf-conformance.md:13`), which stamp-type does not repair because it never overrides an existing type (`research_vault/stamp.py:5`).
- B5 pass: MIT permits redistributing `main.js` and `manifest.json`; there is no `styles.css`. A seeded copy would also carry Popper's MIT notice, which the bundle omits.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass† unchanged, on a different key. The re-judgement kept pass on `trigger_auto_manual` `"Manual"` with `app.json` `alwaysUpdateLinks` `false`. The verifier refuted those keys. Even under Manual, the Move-the-note command calls `renameFile`, and the resulting link update writes into the files that link to the moved note, which the person did not choose. The modal does not keep those writes off wiki/, literature/ or log/, and its always-update choice sets the key true. The pass survives only on a seed that makes no move at all: `data.json` `"folder_tag_pattern": []`. That empty array replaces the placeholder rule, because `loadSettings` merges `data.json` over the defaults with `Object.assign`, and the plugin's only remaining write is its own `data.json` (`main.ts:14`, `:53-80`, `:102-122`, `:147`; `utils/Utils.ts:53`; `settings/settings.ts:31`; obsidian 1.13.7 `app.js:1@byte 1777251`, `@byte 1777501`, `@byte 1779251`, `@byte 1779645`, `@byte 1746594`, `@byte 1792033`). Record discrepancy: the family verifier's record carries a result field of fail for this cell, while its reason states that the pass survives on that key. That verifier used the field as its verdict on the re-judgement (its Tag Wrangler B3 entry reads pass beside a confirmed fail), so the must result here is taken from the reason. Hinge: this confines by configuring the plugin to do nothing, the same shape as Local REST API B3 and Pandoc Plugin. Resolved 2026-10-04 by the second ruling.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B4 pass unchanged, with the keys widened by the verifier. Exclusion tests a regex against the parent path, and the re-judgement's regex left `wiki/index.md` and root `index.md` movable. A title or tag rule could then move either into a folder where `check_reserved` reports a nested index carrying frontmatter (OKF rule 3). Seed keys: `data.json` `"use_regex_to_check_for_excluded_folder": true` with `"excluded_folder": [{"folder": "^(/|inbox|literature|log|wiki)(/|$)"}]` (the root folder's path is `/`); every `folder_tag_pattern[].folder` outside literature/, log/, inbox/ and projects/; and `app.json` `"newLinkFormat": "relative"`. With the B3 key (`folder_tag_pattern` `[]`), nothing moves at all (`main.ts:24-39`, `:53-80`, `:107`; `structure.py:41`, `:136-164`; claude-obsidian 2.2.0 `templates/vault/wiki/index.md:1-10`; obsidian 1.13.7 `app.js:1@byte 1384193`, `@byte 1741795`, `@byte 1747467`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 pass† → pass† — the confining key moves back to `data.json` `"trigger_auto_manual": "Manual"`, which must be seeded because both the default and the floor's value are Automatic; `"folder_tag_pattern": []` is no longer needed. Counted in Automatic mode: the create, metadata-changed and rename hooks, for any file and any writer, move every note whose tags or title match a rule through `fileManager.renameFile`. Under Manual every event returns at the gate except the Move-the-note command on the active note, which the person runs. The link update after it follows from that deliberate rename, so the verifier found the pass does not depend on `alwaysUpdateLinks`, and the floor's `true` does not defeat it. Manual is a mode, not disabling: the command still works. The §6.4 live leg (whether a CLI write fires metadataCache `changed`) no longer decides the cell, because a note the person creates or retags in Obsidian already triggers a counted move in Automatic mode (`main.ts:14`, `:96-100`, `:102-122`, `:147`; `settings/settings.ts:28`; `utils/Utils.ts:53` @`631c4be`; floor `a3d22d0a .obsidian/plugins/auto-note-mover/data.json:2`).
- ◊ 2026-10-04 live: B1 pass — release 1.2.0 (`main.js` sha256 `f8ca8bf6…` and `manifest.json` `515cc7f3…`, both as pinned above); minAppVersion 0.12.0. `enablePluginAndSave` resolved true; loaded with its 2 commands (move the note; toggle Auto-Manual). No error and no `data.json` in the 3 s window, so it ran at its default settings, and the tree diff shows no note moved. The §6.4 measurements (a CLI write firing `changed`, Manual mode, regex exclusion, console errors on non-Markdown creates) were not run (`/tmp/lane23-live/obsidian/logs/plugin-auto-note-mover.log`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass† → fail†∆ — (1) moves through `fileManager.renameFile`, from the create, metadata-changed and rename hooks (any writer, Automatic mode) and from the Move the note command (any mode), can move a matching note out of wiki/ or root `log.md`, or into a rule destination under wiki/, unseen (moves out of literature/ are seen by evidence-layer); (2) every move runs Obsidian's link update, which rewrites links in wiki/ pages and root `log.md` that point at the moved note, at once under `alwaysUpdateLinks` true (as the floor sets it) or after the confirm modal. Setting for (1): `data.json` `"use_regex_to_check_for_excluded_folder": true` with `excluded_folder` regexes for `^(literature|fulltext|wiki|log|system/propagations)(/|$)`, `^inbox$` and `^/$` (the check runs on the hook and command paths alike), and every `folder_tag_pattern[].folder` off the roster. For (2) only `"folder_tag_pattern": []` removes it, by making no move at all, which the standing reading counts as disabling (§7.55). Hinges: it would pass on `[]` if a loaded-but-idle setting counted, or on the exclusion and destination keys if the link update were not counted as the plugin's path (counted because the plugin chose `renameFile` over `vault.rename`). Verifier: confirmed (`main.ts:13-39`, `:96-122`; `utils/Utils.ts:35-56`; `settings/settings.ts:28-33` @`631c4be`; Obsidian 1.13.7 `app.js:1@byte 1777261`, `@byte 1779273`).
- ◇ 2026-10-04 fourth ruling: B3 fail†∆ → pass†∆◇ — Obsidian's link update after the plugin's `fileManager.renameFile` move is now app behaviour; that resolves the third ruling's hinge and replaces the §2A Third ruling reading that counted it as the plugin's path. Counted: the plugin's own moves, from the create, metadata-changed and rename hooks (Automatic mode, any writer) and from the Move the note command (any mode), which can move a matching note out of wiki/ or root `log.md`, or into a rule destination under wiki/. Setting: `data.json` `"use_regex_to_check_for_excluded_folder": true`, `"excluded_folder": [{"folder": "^(literature|fulltext|wiki|log|system/propagations)(/|$)"}, {"folder": "^inbox$"}, {"folder": "^/$"}]`, and every `folder_tag_pattern[].folder` off the roster and neither the vault root nor `inbox`. The exclusion loop runs inside `fileCheck` before any rule, on the hook and command paths alike, and a root file's parent path is `/`, so `^/$` covers root `log.md`. Notes elsewhere still move, so this confines rather than disables; `"trigger_auto_manual": "Manual"` alone does not suffice, because the command still moves notes. Verifier: confirmed, adding the destination limit: the move refuses only an existing same-named file, so a root or `inbox` destination could create root `log.md` or `inbox/review-queue.md` when that file is absent (`main.ts:13-39`, `:96-122`; `utils/Utils.ts:30-56`; `settings/settings.ts:28-33` @`631c4be`).

**Better Word Count (`better-word-count`).**

- Pin: release 0.10.1, tag commit `c93e045c213875c07bf8ceca4d960c3d4a57cec9` (2024-01-23); HEAD `044c1bd` (2024-06-19) is 7 unreleased commits past the tag; last push 2025-06-20; `community-plugins.json:150`. Assets: `main.js` (`ce8ea4a7…`), `manifest.json` (`45d6a164…`, identical to the tag's), `styles.css` (`f279f198…`).
- Licence: MIT (`LICENSE:1-3`, 2022 Luke Leppan; `package.json:24`). Bundled with notices: Chart.js 4.4.1 (MIT; `main.js:9298-9302`, `:11347-11351`), `@kurkle/color` 0.3.2 (MIT; `:8717-8721`) and an is-promise snippet (MIT; `:9-10`). Bundled with no notice: the Svelte 3.59.2 runtime (MIT; marker `:529`) and, found by the verifier, moment 2.29.4 (MIT), inlined as a CommonJS module from `:1903` with only a marker comment and its version string at `:7530-7532`; `src/stats/StatsManager.ts:4` imports it, and `package.json` does not declare it. `svelte-icons` is declared but absent from the bundle.
- Floor: 0.9.6 (`a3d22d0a .obsidian/plugins/better-word-count/manifest.json:4`). Floor settings, paraphrased from `data.json:1-11`: the status bar shows the file's words and characters, and the alternate bar shows file count and vault totals; comments are not counted; statistics collection is on. These are 0.9.x keys (`preset`, `statusBarQuery`, `statusBarAltQuery`) that 0.10.x no longer reads; it uses `statusBar` and `altBar` arrays (`src/settings/Settings.ts:39-47`). The floor tracks the resulting `.obsidian/vault-stats.json` (history 2023-05-23 to 2023-05-27) and turns core Word count off in its favour (`core-plugins-migration.json:21`). It also tracks a root-level `.vault-stats` written by an older version that stored statistics at the vault root (bwc commit `5aa5198`; 2022 history, §8.5); 0.10.1 does not write there.
- Writes: its own `data.json` (`src/main.ts:78`); when `collectStats` is true, a JSON statistics file at `statsPath` (default `.obsidian/vault-stats.json`), created and rewritten through `vault.adapter.write` (`src/stats/StatsManager.ts:53`, `:62`, `:72`). Nothing in notes: `src/` has no `vault.modify`, `create`, `process` or `fileManager` call.
- B1: the manifest has no `minAppVersion` key and the repository no `versions.json`, so nothing gates the version.
- B2: long-form (word, character and page counts for a draft, a selection and sections) and the floor, which uses it in place of core Word count (`a3d22d0a .obsidian/community-plugins.json:16`). Recorded by the verifier: `B-search-new` had dropped it as overlapping core Word count and `novel-word-count` (§2.4).
- B3 pass: named key `data.json` `collectStats` `false` (the default, `Settings.ts:79`); `StatsManager` is constructed only when it is true (`main.ts:40-42`). Alternatively `statsPath` outside the machine surfaces (default `.obsidian/vault-stats.json`, `Settings.ts:82`; editable at `SettingsTab.ts:71-73`). The floor sets `collectStats` true, but the default path keeps the writes out of the machine surfaces. `statsPath` is a literal `.obsidian/` path that ignores an overridden config directory.
- B4 pass: it writes nothing into notes.
- B5 pass†: MIT and its bundled MIT parts permit redistributing `main.js`, `manifest.json` and `styles.css`. Desk: a seeded copy must add Svelte's notice. Verifier: moment's notice too; the result holds and the notice list is completed.
- Exit: the verifier kept the desk line (§3.5) with one wording fix and the flag unset: the statistics file sits at the literal path `.obsidian/vault-stats.json`, which is not necessarily the config directory.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 pass and B4 pass unchanged. The pass does not depend on `collectStats`: with it true, the default `statsPath` `.obsidian/vault-stats.json` also keeps the statistics write off the roster. - Licence: none at the pin. There is no LICENSE file (GitHub's licence endpoint answers 404 and the licence field is null), and the released files carry no author copyright notice. `package.json:18` declares MIT, but `package.json` is not a released file. Bundled: tslib, with Microsoft's 0BSD notice (`main.js:7-21`), and azu/codemirror-typewriter-scrolling (MIT on GitHub), credited at `main.js:76` by URL only.

Verifier, sharpened: `Plugin.registerCodeMirror` is a no-op stub in 1.13.7 (`app.js:1@byte 2898790`), so the CM5 option path (`main.ts:112-115`) never runs and the CM6 scroll-effect extension (`extension.ts:58-77`) is the only live path.

- B5 fail: no licence text and no author copyright notice exist at the pin; the only grant is an SPDX string in a file that is not shipped, so a seeded copy has no notice to carry, and the bundled azu code lacks its notice too. It turns to pass only if a `package.json`-only declaration is accepted as the licence. Verifier, flag unset: the evidence is accurate, but the same family accepts a `package.json`-only ISC declaration for monkey-around inside Tag Wrangler's pass, so one reading should decide both (§7.27).

**Tag Wrangler (`tag-wrangler`).**

- Pin: release 0.6.5 = HEAD `5930a24235d8290fb1eafb04938a882c201c3d1f` (2026-08-13); active; `community-plugins.json:584`. Assets: `main.js` (`f390bf13…`), `manifest.json` (`271a93e6…`, identical to the tag's); no `styles.css` (the floor's 0.5.8 ships one).
- Licence: ISC (`LICENSE:1-5`, 2021 PJ Eby; `package.json:7`). The minified `main.js` carries no notices for the build dependencies it bundles (`package.json:9-15`): yaml 2.0.0-10 (ISC; its `parseDocument` is present), `@ophidian/core` (ISC), uneventful (ISC) and monkey-around (ISC per its own `package.json:8` only; that repository has no LICENSE file). The verifier added to-use 0.3.3 (ISC, P.J. Eby), a dependency of `@ophidian/core` (`pnpm-lock.yaml:905-920`), whose distinctive keys and error message are in the release `main.js`. i18next resolves to Obsidian's global and is not bundled.
- Floor: 0.5.8 (`a3d22d0a .obsidian/plugins/tag-wrangler/manifest.json:6`). No `data.json`; the plugin has no settings surface at either version (0.6.5 `src/` has no `loadData`, `saveData` or `addSettingTab`). The floor's bundle carries the same vault-wide rename and the same tag-page writer (floor `main.js:146-152`). The floor's `app.json:6` sets `newFileLocation` `"current"`, so tag pages are created beside the active note.
- Writes: (1) tag rename, from the Tags view or editor context menu (`src/plugin.js:254`, `:301-302`) or by dragging one tag onto another (`:147`). It prompts for the new name, warns only when the rename would merge into an existing tag (which cannot be undone), then rewrites the tag and its subtags in every cached file whose inline tags, frontmatter tags or tag-valued aliases match (`src/renaming.js:45-61`), through `vault.modify` (`src/File.js:36`; rename loop `:16-39`), editing frontmatter through a format-preserving YAML CST (`:42-88`). Verifier cite fix: the desk's `File.js:107-130`, `:127` and `:133-178` do not exist at the pin, where the file has 89 lines. Object-valued frontmatter tag entries are never edited: `parseFrontMatterTags` filters them out in `findTargets` (`renaming.js:53`), and `Replacement.inArray` returns non-strings unchanged (`src/Tag.js:51`). (2) Create tag page: `vault.create` of a new note whose only frontmatter is an `Aliases` list holding the tag, placed by `fileManager.getNewFileParent`, which follows `app.json` `newFileLocation` (`src/plugin.js:39-46`).
- B2: floor. Its index navigation and note routing are tag-based (`a3d22d0a .obsidian/community-plugins.json:3`; eight saved tag searches by source and entity type, `.obsidian/bookmarks.json:2-51`, §4.1 Bookmarks), and this plugin keeps those tags tidy. No step.
- B3 fail†: one rename gesture fans out into machine surfaces, and the plugin has no settings, so no key can scope or prevent it; that is the fail condition of the `B-core-1` reading (§1.1), which the spec has not yet confirmed (§7.1; at the desk read, 2026-10-03; superseded: §2A's rulings replaced that reading). Desk basis: a rename rewrites every literature/ note carrying the tag, because literature notes carry Zotero tags in frontmatter `tags` (`research_vault/literature_notes.py:51`; `CONTEXT.md:57`). Verifier: refuted, since those tags are mappings the tag parser drops (tag shape, above), and aliases hold the title, which matches only if it begins with a hash (`src/Tag.js:17`). The fail stands on a different basis: wiki/, whose claude-obsidian pages carry string-list tags (`lint_engine.py:63`; `frontmatter.md:16-17`), plus any body hashtag in a literature/ or log/ note. Tag-page creation has a named key: `app.json` `newFileLocation` `"root"`, or `"folder"` with `newFileFolderPath` outside the machine surfaces; the floor's `"current"` puts a tag page inside literature/ or wiki/ when a note there is active.
- B4 fail: every tag page carries a frontmatter block with no `type` (OKF §11 rule 2; `docs/agents/okf-conformance.md:10`). `expected_type` derives a type only for literature/, log/, the review queue and `projects/<n>/draft.md` (`research_vault/structure.py:54-67`), and `stamp.py:13-17` inserts one only where it can be derived and reports the rest (`:30`), so stamp-type repairs a tag page only when it lands in literature/ or log/, both machine surfaces. Rename edits stay ordinary Markdown and YAML.
- B5 pass†: ISC and its bundled ISC parts permit redistributing `main.js` and `manifest.json`. Desk: a seeded copy would carry notices for yaml, `@ophidian/core`, uneventful and monkey-around. Verifier: to-use as well; the result holds and the notice list is completed.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B3 fail† unchanged, now resting on the ruled standard rather than on the `B-core-1` gesture reading. The ruling names a tag rename across files as a judged bulk write. `findTargets` walks every cached file and never runs the ignore-filter check, so `userIgnoreFilters` cannot confine it, and the plugin has no settings (`src/renaming.js:45-61`; `src/File.js:16-39`; obsidian 1.13.7 `app.js:1@byte 1746030`, `@byte 1759015`). Tag-page creation alone is confined by `app.json` `"newFileLocation": "root"`, or `"folder"` with `newFileFolderPath` off the roster.
- 2026-10-03 normalisation: B4 fail unchanged. The re-judgement moved it to pass by routing tag pages to inbox/ (`app.json` `"newFileLocation": "folder"`, `"newFileFolderPath": "inbox"`). The verifier refuted that, and its fail is recorded. A tag page always carries a block without `type`, a rule-2 shape. The register row `okf-conformance.md:36` declines only rule 1, and stamp-type refuses a parseable typeless block (`stamp.py:13-17`); no other seed helps. If one ruling extends that row to typeless blocks in inbox/, the inbox seed would pass (`src/plugin.js:39-46`; `structure.py:78-79`; `okf-conformance.md:9-10`, `:31`, `:36`).
- § 2026-10-04 by-itself: B3 fail† → pass† — nothing is counted. Its background hooks (the layout-ready scan, metadataCache `changed`, vault `delete`) and its `getTags` wrapper change only in-memory maps. Tag rename runs from the Rename menu item or from a drag of one tag onto another, whose drop handler calls the same rename pre-filled with the drop target. Nothing is written until the person accepts the new-name prompt, which resolves false unless the person presses OK or Enter (`ophidian-lib/core@46b123f src/ui/prompt.ts:6-15`, `:53-61`, the version the lock pins), and a merge adds a confirm; only then does `vault.modify` run across the matching files, a bulk change the person confirms. Create tag page runs from a menu item or from a confirm modal on alt- or mod-click. It registers no command, URI or CLI handler. No setting is needed, and the plugin has none. A tag page's location follows `app.json` `newFileLocation`; if placement were counted, `"root"`, or `"folder"` with `newFileFolderPath` off the roster, would confine it (`src/renaming.js:7-9`, `:63-72`, `:74-90`; `src/plugin.js:39-46`, `:140-163`, `:182-222`, `:254`, `:262`, `:392-397`; `src/File.js:36` @`5930a24`).
- ∆ 2026-10-04 third ruling: B3 pass†§ → fail†§∆ — confirm prompts no longer exempt it. (1) Tag rename and merge, from the Rename menu item or a drag of one tag onto another (after the new-name prompt and, for a merge, a confirm), rewrite through `vault.modify` every cached file whose string frontmatter tags, inline hashtags or tag-valued aliases match, with no ignore filter: wiki/ pages (claude-obsidian writes string-list tags) and hashtags in root `log.md` or fulltext/ go unseen; literature/ bodies are seen by evidence-layer and committed log/ files by append-only. (2) Create tag page writes an Aliases-only block with no `type`, which okf-frontmatter (and evidence-layer in literature/) reports wherever it lands on the roster, and `getAvailablePath` avoids overwriting. Setting: none for (1); the plugin has no settings (no `addSettingTab`, `loadData` or `saveData`). Verifier: confirmed (`src/renaming.js:7-91`; `src/File.js:16-39`; `src/plugin.js:32-47` @`5930a24`; `research_vault/structure.py:74-111`).
